# Hatzolah CPG v6.2 — Page-by-Page Visual Audit

Every content page (5–163) rendered as an image and compared against the app's stored
content. Confirmed findings survived an independent skeptic prompted to refute them.

- units audited: **60**
- confirmed critical/high findings: **172**
- medium/low findings (not independently verified): **381**
- CPG graphics with no representation in the app: **286**
- protocols entirely absent: **4**

finding types: missing-section (182), wrong-value (123), colour-coding (83), render-risk (55), typo (46), missing-graphic (33), not-in-cpg (20), table-misrendered (11)

## `asthma-fr-p48` — p48-53 · coverage: partial

**Graphics not represented in the app:**
- p48: Green DEFINITION block: definition paragraph plus a two-column bullet grid of six exacerbation triggers (Allergens / Exercise / Respiratory infections / Weather (thunderstorm asthma) / Medications (NSAIDs) / Irritants), each with a green asterisk and triangle glyph markers.
- p49: Red STOP algorithm box (Adult/16+): two-branch decision — 'IF Patient becomes unresponsive [&] No pulse -> Manage as Cardiac Arrest!' (Cardiac Arrest in red type) and 'IF Patient becomes unresponsive [&] Has pulse -> Position: Supine; Ventilate once every 8-12 seconds (5-8x p/min)' with a purple drug chip 'Oxygen, BVM (8-15L O2)'.
- p49: Navy MANAGEMENT algorithm table (Adult/16+): left condition column with black IF / & / OR / UNTIL operator chips, right-hand purple drug chips (Oxygen Face Mask 8L O2, Salbutamol pMDI via Spacer, Salbutamol Nebulised 8L O2, Atrovent Nebulised 8L O2), indented bullet dose rows and a bold 'Repeat:' column. Uses the down-arrow glyph as a comparison operator ('SpO2 ↓ 92%').
- p49: Vertical black flow-connector bar linking the STOP box to the MANAGEMENT box and continuing off the bottom of the page to the next section (the algorithm's spine).
- p50: Red THUNDERSTORM ASTHMA box: three stacked AND conditions (Suspected thunderstorm asthma [&] Nil improvement despite Salbutamol + Atrovent [&] Clinician unavailable) leading to a purple drug chip 'Adrenaline, IM (Epi-Pen)' with a weight branch using arrow glyphs — 0.3 mg (Epi-Pen) IF ↑ 20 kgs / 0.15 mg (Epi-Pen Jr) IF ↓ 20 kgs.
- p50: Red ESCALATION OF CARE box with phone icon: 'IF Moderate, severe [OR] thunderstorm — Call for Ambulance / 000'.
- p50: Vertical black flow-connector bars entering the THUNDERSTORM ASTHMA box and joining it to the ESCALATION OF CARE box.
- p51: Red STOP algorithm box (12 to 15 years): identical two-branch unresponsive decision, ventilate once every 8-12 seconds (5-8x p/min), purple chip 'Oxygen, BVM (8-15L O2)'.
- p51: Navy MANAGEMENT algorithm table (12 to 15 years): same chip/branch layout as the adult page but with 'SpO2 ↓ 96%', Salbutamol Nebulised 5 mg (1 neb) Repeat 20 mins, and Atrovent 500 mcg (2 nebules).
- p51: Vertical black flow-connector bar linking STOP -> MANAGEMENT -> next page.
- p52: Red THUNDERSTORM ASTHMA box (12 to 15 years) with Adrenaline IM (Epi-Pen) purple chip and the ↑/↓ 20 kgs weight branch, plus red ESCALATION OF CARE box ('Call for Ambulance / 000') and connector bars.
- p53: Red STOP algorithm box (6 to 11 years): same two-branch unresponsive decision but with the paediatric ventilation rate 'Ventilate once every 5-6 seconds (10-14x p/min)' and purple chip 'Oxygen, BVM (8-15L O2)'.
- p53: Navy MANAGEMENT algorithm table (6 to 11 years): same chip/branch layout, 'SpO2 ↓ 96%', Salbutamol Nebulised 5 mg (1 neb) Repeat @ 20 mins, Atrovent 250 mcg (1 nebule).
- p53: Vertical black flow-connector bar linking STOP -> MANAGEMENT -> next page.

**Confirmed (verified) findings:**

### [critical] wrong-value · p51 — Ipratropium (Atrovent) dose for the 12–15 year band is halved in the app: CPG gives 500 mcg (2 nebules), app says 250 mcg.
- **CPG:** p51, MANAGEMENT: 'IF Severe' → 'Atrovent, Nebulised (8L O2)' → '500 mcg (2 nebules)'
- **App:** ageGroups[1] (12–15 years) ipratropium: 'Ipratropium Bromide 250 mcg nebulised if no improvement'
- **Fix:** Change the 12–15 year Ipratropium dose to '500 mcg (2 nebules) nebulised, 8L O2'. The CPG only drops to 250 mcg (1 nebule) at the 6–11 year band (p53); the adult and 12–15 bands are both 500 mcg.

### [critical] missing-graphic · p49 — The red STOP block that appears at the top of every age band (deterioration to unresponsiveness) is entirely absent from the app, including the age-specific BVM ventilation rates.
- **CPG:** p49/p51/p53, red 'STOP' box: 'IF Patient becomes unresponsive & No pulse → Manage as Cardiac Arrest!'; 'IF Patient becomes unresponsive & Has pulse → Position: Supine; Ventilate once every…' with 'Oxygen, BVM (8-15L O2)' — 8-12 seconds (5-8x p/min) for Adult/16+ and 12–15 years, 5-6 seconds (10-14x p/min) for 6–11 years.
- **App:** No stop/deterioration section at all. content only has 'recognition' and 'ageGroups'.
- **Fix:** Add a red-coded section (e.g. key 'immediateAction' or 'dangersAndSafety' so it renders red) per age band carrying both branches verbatim, including 'Oxygen, BVM (8-15L O2)' and the band-specific ventilation rate — 8-12 s (5-8x p/min) for ≥12 y, 5-6 s (10-14x p/min) for 6–11 y.

### [critical] missing-graphic · p50 — The red THUNDERSTORM ASTHMA rescue block (IM Adrenaline / Epi-Pen with a 20 kg weight branch) is completely missing from the app.
- **CPG:** p50/p52, red 'THUNDERSTORM ASTHMA' box: 'IF Suspected thunderstorm asthma & Nil improvement despite Salbutamol + Atrovent & Clinician unavailable' → 'Adrenaline, IM (Epi-Pen)': '0.3 mg (Epi-Pen) IF ↑ 20 kgs', '0.15 mg (Epi-Pen Jr) IF ↓ 20 kgs'
- **App:** No thunderstorm asthma content anywhere in asthma-fr.json.
- **Fix:** Add a red-coded 'thunderstormAsthma' section with all three AND conditions and the Adrenaline IM dosing branch. Render the arrow glyphs as explicit operators: 0.3 mg (Epi-Pen) if >20 kg; 0.15 mg (Epi-Pen Jr) if <20 kg.

### [high] missing-section · p50 — ESCALATION OF CARE (call 000) is missing — the app never tells the responder when to call an ambulance.
- **CPG:** p50/p52, red 'ESCALATION OF CARE' box: 'IF Moderate, severe OR thunderstorm — Call for Ambulance / 000'
- **App:** No escalation field. The schema's 'escalation' key is not present.
- **Fix:** Add escalation: 'Moderate, severe OR thunderstorm asthma — Call for Ambulance / 000', and render it red.

### [high] wrong-value · p49 — Oxygen therapy and its SpO2 trigger thresholds are missing entirely, including the fact that the adult threshold (92%) differs from the paediatric bands (96%).
- **CPG:** p49: 'IF Conscious & SpO2 ↓ 92%' → 'Oxygen, Face Mask (8L O2)'. p51 and p53: 'IF Conscious & SpO2 ↓ 96%' → 'Oxygen, Face Mask (8L O2)'.
- **App:** No oxygen row in any age group. ageGroups objects only carry age / mild_moderate / severe / ipratropium.
- **Fix:** Add an 'oxygen' field per age band: Adult/16+ — 'If conscious and SpO2 below 92%: Oxygen, face mask 8L O2'; 12–15 and 6–11 — 'If conscious and SpO2 below 96%: Oxygen, face mask 8L O2'. Render '↓' as 'below' / '<', not as an arrow.

### [high] not-in-cpg · p49 — The app invents a time-based trigger for Ipratropium ('if no improvement after 20 min Salbutamol' / 'if no improvement'); the CPG triggers Atrovent purely on severity.
- **CPG:** p49/p51/p53, MANAGEMENT: the Atrovent row is gated only by 'IF Severe' → 'Atrovent, Nebulised (8L O2)'. No waiting period or trial-of-Salbutamol condition appears anywhere on these pages.
- **App:** Adult: 'If no improvement after 20 min Salbutamol → Ipratropium Bromide 500 mcg nebulised'. 12–15 and 6–11: 'Ipratropium Bromide 250 mcg nebulised if no improvement'.
- **Fix:** Replace the invented condition with the CPG's: 'If SEVERE: Atrovent (Ipratropium Bromide), nebulised 8L O2 — 500 mcg (2 nebules)' for Adult/16+ and 12–15; '250 mcg (1 nebule)' for 6–11. Delete the 20-minute wait, which would delay Atrovent in a severe presentation.

**Medium/low (unverified):**
- [medium] missing-section p48 — The whole green DEFINITION block on p48 — the asthma definition and the six exacerbation triggers — is absent.  
  _fix:_ Add content.definition (green-rendered) with the definition sentence and a 'triggers' array of the six factors — the thunderstorm trigger in particular sets up the p50 thunderstorm rescue.
- [medium] missing-section p49 — Salbutamol pMDI administration technique is truncated: '4 breaths for each dose' is dropped, and the CPG's 'doses' is rewritten as 'puffs'.  
  _fix:_ Restore the technique: 'Salbutamol, pMDI via spacer — 4 to 12 doses, 4 breaths for each dose. Repeat @ 20 mins until difficult breathing relieved.'
- [medium] missing-section p49 — The MANAGEMENT positioning rows and 'Minimise patient exertion' — the first instructions on every age page — are missing.  
  _fix:_ Add a management preamble shared by all bands: minimise patient exertion; conscious → sitting / position of comfort; altered conscious → supine.
- [medium] missing-section p49 — The transport-disposition row 'IF Mild & Responds to Mx — Use VVED as minimum' is missing from all three age bands.  
  _fix:_ Add to each age band: 'If mild and responds to management — use VVED as a minimum.'
- [medium] render-risk p49 — content.ageGroups is an array of uniform objects with three long-text columns, so it will degrade to stacked record cards, and the snake_case key 'mild_moderate' will not humanise cleanly.  
  _fix:_ Split each age band into its own nested object with structured sub-keys (stop, oxygen, pmdi, nebulised, atrovent, escalation) and rename 'mild_moderate' to camelCase 'mildModerate' so the humaniser produces 'Mild Moderate' rather than 'Mild_moderate'.
- [low] wrong-value p49 — Nebuliser units are described as 'ampules'; the CPG counts 'nebs' for Salbutamol and 'nebules' for Atrovent.  
  _fix:_ Use the CPG's own unit words — 'nebs' for Salbutamol and 'nebules' for Atrovent — so the count matches what is physically on the shelf.
- [low] wrong-value p49 — The app calls the drug 'Ipratropium Bromide'; on these algorithm pages the CPG names it 'Atrovent', including in the thunderstorm criteria.  
  _fix:_ Show both names, e.g. 'Atrovent (Ipratropium Bromide), nebulised 8L O2', so responders searching either term find it.

## `asthma-fr-p54` — p54-59 · coverage: partial

**Graphics not represented in the app:**
- p54: Red-header algorithm block 'THUNDERSTORM ASTHMA' with a vertical black connector line entering from the previous page. Three stacked conditions joined by inverted black IF / & / & badges, a purple medication chip 'Adrenaline, IM (Epi-Pen)', and two indented dose branches each gated by a black IF badge plus an arrow glyph (up-arrow 20 kgs / down-arrow 20 kgs).
- p54: Red-header algorithm block 'ESCALATION OF CARE' with a phone icon, black IF and OR badges: 'Moderate, severe OR thunderstorm  Call for Ambulance / 000'.
- p55: Navy double-ruled band header '2 to 5 YEARS OLD' that opens the age band.
- p55: Red-header algorithm block 'STOP': two IF+& condition rows (unresponsive & no pulse -> 'Manage as Cardiac Arrest!' in red text; unresponsive & has pulse -> Position: Supine), then a purple medication chip 'Oxygen, BVM (8-15L O2)' with two age-gated ventilation-rate branches.
- p55: Navy-header 'MANAGEMENT' three-column flow table for 2-5 years: condition column with black IF / & / OR / UNTIL badges and a down-arrow operator glyph (SpO2 down-arrow 96%), purple medication chips (Oxygen Face Mask 8L O2, Salbutamol pMDI via Spacer, Salbutamol Nebulised 8L O2, Atrovent Nebulised 8L O2), indented dose bullets and clock-icon repeat rows. A vertical connector line runs off the bottom of the page.
- p56: Red-header algorithm block 'THUNDERSTORM ASTHMA' (the 2-5 years band copy), identical layout to p54: IF / & / & conditions, purple 'Adrenaline, IM (Epi-Pen)' chip, 0.3 mg / 0.15 mg branches gated on up-arrow / down-arrow 20 kgs.
- p56: Red-header algorithm block 'ESCALATION OF CARE' — 'IF Moderate, severe OR thunderstorm  Call for Ambulance / 000'.
- p58: Grey-header drug spec card: Ipratropium Bromide IF No improvement after 20 mins Salbutamol, Demographic up-arrow 11 years old, Nebulised, Initial 500 mcg (2 nebules), Repeat Nil, Maximum 500 mcg (total dose).
- p58: Grey-header drug spec card: Adrenaline IF Thunderstorm Asthma, Demographic >= 6 years old & up-arrow 20 kgs, Route Intramuscular (IM) Epi-Pen, Initial 0.3 mg Epi-Pen, Repeat Consult, Maximum Consult.
- p59: Grey-header drug spec card: Adrenaline IF Thunderstorm Asthma, Demographic down-arrow 6 years old OR down-arrow 20 kgs, Route Intramuscular (IM) Epi-Pen, Initial 0.15 mg Epi-Pen Jr, Repeat Consult, Maximum Consult.
- p59: Green-header 'FURTHER NOTES' block with seven asterisk-bulleted notes, using bold NOT / AVOID emphasis and inverted black '&' and 'NO' badges.

**Confirmed (verified) findings:**

### [critical] missing-section · p56 — The entire THUNDERSTORM ASTHMA block (Adrenaline IM / Epi-Pen rescue) is absent from the app. It appears twice in this page range (p54 and p56) as a red algorithm block, and again as two Adrenaline drug cards on p58-p59.
- **CPG:** THUNDERSTORM ASTHMA — 'IF Suspected thunderstorm asthma & Nil improvement despite Salbutamol + Atrovent & Clinician unavailable' -> 'Adrenaline, IM (Epi-Pen)': '0.3 mg (Epi-Pen) IF [up-arrow] 20 kgs'; '0.15 mg (Epi-Pen Jr) IF [down-arrow] 20 kgs'. Drug cards add Demographic '>= 6 years old & [up-arrow] 20 kgs' -> 0.3 mg, and '[down-arrow] 6 years old OR [down-arrow] 20 kgs' -> 0.15 mg, both Route 'Intramuscular (IM)  Epi-Pen', Repeat Dose(s) 'Consult', Maximum 'Consult'.
- **App:** Nothing. content has only `recognition` and `ageGroups`; the strings 'Adrenaline', 'Epi-Pen' and 'thunderstorm' do not appear anywhere in asthma-fr.json.
- **Fix:** Add a red-coloured `immediateAction` section, e.g. content.immediateAction.thunderstormAsthma = { trigger: ['Suspected thunderstorm asthma', 'AND nil improvement despite Salbutamol + Atrovent', 'AND clinician unavailable'], dosing: [{ drug: 'Adrenaline', route: 'IM (Epi-Pen)', indication: 'Thunderstorm asthma', adult: { dose: '0.3 mg (Epi-Pen)', criteria: '>= 6 years old AND >20 kg', repeat: 'Consult', max: 'Consult' }, paediatric: { dose: '0.15 mg (Epi-Pen Jr)', criteria: '<6 years old OR <20 kg', repeat: 'Consult', max: 'Consult' } }] }.

### [critical] wrong-value · p58 — Ipratropium Bromide dose for the 12-15 years age band is 250 mcg in the app, but the CPG's Ipratropium demographic split puts 12-15 year olds in the 500 mcg (2 nebules) group.
- **CPG:** p58, card 2: 'Ipratropium Bromide | IF No improvement after 20 mins Salbutamol' / 'Demographic  [up-arrow] 11 years old' / 'Route Nebulised' / 'Initial Dose 500 mcg (2 nebules)' / 'Repeat Dose(s) Nil' / 'Maximum 500 mcg (total dose)'. The complementary card is 'Demographic [down-arrow] 12 years old' -> 250 mcg (1 nebules). A 12-15 year old is above 11 years old, so the 500 mcg card applies.
- **App:** ageGroups[1] (age '12–15 years'): ipratropium = 'Ipratropium Bromide 250 mcg nebulised if no improvement'.
- **Fix:** Change the 12–15 years ipratropium value to 'Ipratropium Bromide 500 mcg (2 nebules) nebulised if no improvement after 20 min Salbutamol — no repeat, max 500 mcg total'. Cross-check against the 12-15 flow page p51-52 (other agent's range); if that page states 250 mcg the CPG is internally inconsistent and must be escalated to the clinical author rather than silently resolved.

### [critical] missing-section · p55 — The red STOP block for the 2-5 years band — deterioration to unresponsiveness, cardiac-arrest handoff and BVM ventilation rates — is entirely absent from the app.
- **CPG:** p55 STOP (red header): 'IF Patient becomes unresponsive & No pulse' -> 'Manage as Cardiac Arrest!' (in red). 'IF Patient becomes unresponsive & Has pulse' -> 'Position: Supine'; 'Ventilate once every…' with 'Oxygen, BVM (8-15L O2)': '5 - 6 seconds  (10 - 14x p/min)  IF 5 years old'; '4 - 5 seconds  (12 - 15x p/min)  IF 2 - 4 years old'.
- **App:** Nothing. No 'STOP', 'unresponsive', 'BVM', 'ventilate' or cardiac-arrest content exists in asthma-fr.json.
- **Fix:** Add a red-mapped `immediateAction` section for the 2-5 band: { stop: { noPulse: 'Unresponsive AND no pulse -> Manage as Cardiac Arrest!', hasPulse: ['Unresponsive AND has pulse -> Position: Supine', 'Ventilate with Oxygen, BVM (8-15L O2)'], ventilationRates: { headers: ['Age', 'Ventilate once every', 'Rate'], rows: [['5 years old', '5 - 6 seconds', '10 - 14x p/min'], ['2 - 4 years old', '4 - 5 seconds', '12 - 15x p/min']] } } }. Transcribe the rates exactly as printed.

### [high] missing-section · p56 — The red ESCALATION OF CARE block (call 000) is missing from the app; there is no escalation field at all.
- **CPG:** ESCALATION OF CARE (red header, p54 and p56): 'IF Moderate, severe OR thunderstorm    Call for Ambulance / 000'.
- **App:** No `escalation` key. The app never tells the responder to call an ambulance.
- **Fix:** Add content.escalation = 'IF moderate, severe OR thunderstorm asthma — Call for Ambulance / 000' and render it in a red-mapped section (e.g. under `immediateAction`) so it is not styled as an ordinary navy note.

### [high] missing-section · p59 — The whole green FURTHER NOTES block (seven safety notes, including the guideline's own age floor) is absent from the app.
- **CPG:** FURTHER NOTES (green header, p59): 'If 000 had NOT been called OR ambulance is NOT attending for mild asthma the VVED must be used as a minimum'; 'Asthmatic patients can show initial improvement with treatment prior to rapid deterioration - ensure ongoing monitoring'; 'Improving SpO2 may NOT necessarily be a sign of improvement in clinical condition'; 'Beware of patient presenting with wheeze & history of heart failure but NO history of asthma / COPD - they do NOT meet his guideline'; 'Over ventilation of the asthmatic patient may result in worse patient outcomes'; 'Management is primarily targeted at relieving difficult breathing - AVOID administering large doses of Salbutamol when wheeze is the only ongoing concern'; 'This guideline is only designed for patients that are 2 years old and over'.
- **App:** No `notes` key at all.
- **Fix:** Add content.notes as an array of the seven strings above, transcribed verbatim (including the CPG's 'his guideline' wording). The 'only designed for patients that are 2 years old and over' note is a scope limit and should also be surfaced in the protocol summary.

### [high] missing-section · p55 — Almost all of the 2-5 years MANAGEMENT flow is missing — the app keeps only the two Salbutamol doses and drops exertion, positioning, oxygen, VVED and the anaphylaxis check.
- **CPG:** p55 MANAGEMENT: 'Minimise patient exertion'; 'IF Conscious -> Position: Sitting / Position of comfort'; 'IF Altered conscious -> Position: Supine'; 'IF Conscious & SpO2 [down-arrow] 96% -> Oxygen, Face Mask (8L O2)'; 'IF Mild & Responds to Mx -> Use VVED as minimum'; 'IF Severe resp. distress despite Salbutamol  Consider if Anaphylaxis'.
- **App:** ageGroups[3] holds only mild_moderate, severe and ipratropium dose strings. No positioning, no oxygen threshold, no VVED, no anaphylaxis consideration.
- **Fix:** Give each age band a `management` array (or nested object) carrying these steps, e.g. management: ['Minimise patient exertion', 'Conscious -> Position: Sitting / Position of comfort', 'Altered conscious -> Position: Supine', 'Conscious AND SpO2 <96% -> Oxygen, Face Mask (8L O2)', 'Mild AND responds to Mx -> Use VVED as minimum', 'Severe respiratory distress despite Salbutamol -> Consider if Anaphylaxis'].

**Medium/low (unverified):**
- [medium] wrong-value p58 — The app omits the Ipratropium Bromide repeat and maximum limits; a responder reading only the app has no ceiling on Atrovent.  
  _fix:_ Append the limits to every ipratropium string, or better, model Ipratropium as a `dosing` entry with repeat: 'Nil' and max: '500 mcg (total dose)' / '250 mcg (total dose)' so it renders as a dosing card.
- [medium] wrong-value p55 — For 2-5 years the app gates Atrovent on 'no improvement', but the p55 age-band flow gates it on 'Severe' and specifies the presentation as 1 nebule with 8L O2.  
  _fix:_ State both triggers as the CPG does: 'Severe, OR no improvement after 20 min Salbutamol -> Atrovent (Ipratropium Bromide) 250 mcg (1 nebule) nebulised with 8L O2 — no repeat, max 250 mcg'.
- [medium] wrong-value p57 — The pMDI administration technique '4x breaths per puff' / '4 breaths for each dose' is dropped from all four age bands, so the app understates how the puffer is to be given.  
  _fix:_ Rewrite as 'Salbutamol pMDI 2-6 puffs via spacer, 4 breaths per puff; repeat 2-6 puffs @ 20 min; maximum: nil' (and 4-12 puffs for >= 6 years).
- [medium] wrong-value p55 — The app's Salbutamol strings drop the 'until difficult breathing relieved' endpoint and the 'Maximum: Nil' ceiling that the CPG states for every Salbutamol route.  
  _fix:_ Add the endpoint and ceiling to each band, e.g. severe: 'Salbutamol nebulised 2.5 mg (1/2 ampule) with 8L O2; repeat 2.5 mg @ 20 min until difficult breathing relieved; maximum: nil'.
- [medium] render-risk p57 — `ageGroups` is an array of four uniform objects with three long-text columns, so it degrades to stacked record cards rather than a comparison table, and the snake_case key `mild_moderate` humanises to 'Mild_moderate'.  
  _fix:_ Rename `mild_moderate` to `mildModerate` so the humanised label reads 'Mild Moderate', and either shorten the cells to fit a table on 375px or restructure each band as its own nested subsection with a `dosing` array (which renders as dosing cards).
- [low] typo p59 — The CPG itself contains two typos in this page range; if these strings are transcribed into the app they should be reproduced (or flagged to the author), not silently corrected.  
  _fix:_ Transcribe verbatim when adding these sections and raise both typos with the CPG author; do not paraphrase them into corrected text without sign-off.

## `birth-newborn-resuscitation-p60` — p60-65 · coverage: missing
_Full replacement content was extracted by the audit agent._

**Graphics not represented in the app:**

- p63: Black connector from the navy 'NEWBORN MANAGEMENT — ALL Newborns' box down into the red 'NEWBORN RESUSCITATION' box, whose header carries the entry criteria chips 'IF HR ↓ 100' and 'OR Apnoea / Gasping'.
- p63: Purple pill-icon medication banners 'Room Air, Newborn BVM' and, in two branches, 'O2, Newborn BVM (5L O2)'.
- p63: Three-way branching algorithm: a black horizontal bracket splits the reassessment step into three side-by-side red-headed columns — IF HR ↓ 60, IF HR 60 - 100, IF HR ↑ 100 — each with its own body of actions.
- p64: Vertical flowchart chaining three navy boxes: MOTHER MANAGEMENT: PART 1 → BIRTH: PLACENTA → MOTHER MANAGEMENT: PART 2, joined by black connector bars.
- p64: CLUB mnemonic for placental separation signs, encoded as underlined leading letters (Cramping, Lengthening, Uterus, Bleeding).
- p64: Ambulance icon marking the transport action 'Transport placenta + membranes to hospital'.
- p65: Green 'FURTHER NOTES' block with green asterisk bullets over alternating green bands (five notes).

**Confirmed (verified) findings:**

### [critical] missing-section · p60 — The entire Birth & Newborn Resuscitation protocol (CPG p60-65, six pages, 18 sections) has no entry in the app.
- **CPG:** "BIRTH & NEWBORN RESUSCITATION" spanning p60-65: STOP, RECOGNITION, PREPERATION, OBSTERIC HISTORY: IMMINENT BIRTH, BIRTH: HEAD, UMBILICAL CORD CHECK, BIRTH: HEAD ROTATION, BIRTH: SHOULDERS + BODY, NEWBORN MANAGEMENT, NEWBORN RESUSCITATION, MOTHER MANAGEMENT: PART 1, BIRTH: PLACENTA, MOTHER MANAGEMENT: PART 2, FURTHER NOTES.
- **App:** Nothing. grep of src/data/contentData.js and src/data/extensions/ for 'birth', 'newborn', 'obstetric', 'placenta', 'PIPER' returns no protocol entry; the 'obstetric' category defined in CATEGORY_COLORS has zero members.
- **Fix:** Add a new conditionsContent entry keyed 'birth-newborn-resuscitation' with level 'FR' (pages 4-117 default; no IV/IM/accredited procedure appears in this section) and category 'obstetric', using the proposedContent object supplied in this audit.

### [critical] missing-graphic · p63 — The newborn resuscitation three-way heart-rate branching algorithm is absent — the entire post-ventilation decision tree is lost.
- **CPG:** A black bracket splits from "Re-Assess: Heart Rate every 30 seconds / And each time manage below as appropriate" into three red-headed boxes: "IF HR ↓ 60" (Call PIPER: 1300 137 650; O2, Newborn BVM (5L O2); Clamp + cut umbilical cord — Clamp 1 @ 10cm, Clamp 2 @ 15cm, Cut between clamps; CPR @ 3:1 — 120 events p/min, 3 : 1 ratio), "IF HR 60 - 100" (Call PIPER: 1300 137 650; O2, Newborn BVM (5L O2); Ventilate once every 1 - 1.5 seconds; 40 - 60x p/min), and "IF HR ↑ 100" (No resuscitation required).
- **App:** No representation of any kind.
- **Fix:** Render as a { question, branches:[{condition,goTo,label}] } decision-chip block (newbornHeartRateBranch) with sibling keys hrUnder60, hr60To100, hrOver100, matching the existing clinical-approach-fr disposition pattern. Consider a custom SVG under a '_' key to reproduce the three-column bracket.

### [high] missing-graphic · p62 — The sequential birth-stage flowchart (four navy stage boxes joined by black connectors) is absent, so the mandatory order of birth steps is lost.
- **CPG:** Four navy section headers connected by short black vertical connector bars in strict sequence: "BIRTH: HEAD" → "UMBILICAL CORD CHECK" → "BIRTH: HEAD ROTATION" → "BIRTH: SHOULDERS + BODY".
- **App:** No representation of any kind.
- **Fix:** Emit the four stages as ordered sibling content keys (birthHead, umbilicalCordCheck, birthHeadRotation, birthShouldersAndBody) so their rendered order preserves the flow, and add an SVG stage-chain diagram under a '_' key if a visual is wanted.

### [high] missing-graphic · p64 — The mother/placenta flow chain (MOTHER MANAGEMENT: PART 1 → BIRTH: PLACENTA → MOTHER MANAGEMENT: PART 2) is absent, including the second blood-loss reassessment.
- **CPG:** Three navy boxes joined by black connectors: "MOTHER MANAGEMENT: PART 1" → "BIRTH: PLACENTA" → "MOTHER MANAGEMENT: PART 2". Part 1 and Part 2 both begin "Assess: Maternal blood loss" — Part 2 adds "IF Fundus NOT firm OR ↑ 500mL bleed → Fundal massage" and "IF ↑ 500mL bleed → Consult: Clinician".
- **App:** No representation of any kind.
- **Fix:** Emit motherManagementPart1, birthPlacenta, motherManagementPart2 as three ordered sibling keys so the repeat blood-loss assessment before and after placental delivery is preserved.

### [high] missing-section · p61 — The whole OBSTERIC HISTORY: IMMINENT BIRTH questionnaire (p61) — 12 history questions under two sub-headings — is absent.
- **CPG:** "OBSTERIC HISTORY: IMMINENT BIRTH" with "CURRENT PREGNANCY" (antenatal care; "Number of weeks gestation? (37+ weeks considered full term, 22+ weeks considered viable)"; number of babies expected; contraction onset/frequency/duration; membrane rupture time/colour/quantity/smell; vaginal bleeding; baby position; fetal movement; pregnancy complications) and "PREVIOUS PREGNANCIES" (gravidity/parity; previous complications e.g. c-section; length of previous labours).
- **App:** No representation of any kind.
- **Fix:** Add a nested obstetricHistoryImminentBirth object with currentPregnancy and previousPregnancies arrays; the renderer will humanise the camelCase keys into sub-sections.

### [high] missing-graphic · p60 — The red STOP escalation block, including the PIPER phone number rendered as ten individual red digit tiles, is absent.
- **CPG:** Red "STOP" header over: "For ALL birthing emergencies → SitRep: Hatzolah dispatch + call ambulance"; "IF ANY of the following → Consult: Clinician + PIPER" (presenting part other than head; "Gestation ↓ 37 weeks"; significant complications during pregnancy; history of previous complicated births); and "PIPER phone number" shown as boxed digits 1 3 0 0  1 3 7  6 5 0.
- **App:** No representation of any kind.
- **Fix:** Put the STOP block in a key named immediateAction so the renderer colours it red, and store the PIPER number as its own key (piperPhoneNumber: '1300 137 650') so it can be surfaced as a tap-to-call affordance.

**Medium/low (unverified):**
- [medium] missing-graphic p60 — The HUBCAPS and CLUB mnemonics are encoded only as typographic emphasis (bold/underlined first letters) and would be lost by a text-layer extraction.  
  _fix:_ Emit both as mnemonic arrays of { letter, meaning } (mnemonic for HUBCAPS, placentaSeparationMnemonic for CLUB) alongside the plain recognition list.
- [medium] colour-coding p63 — Four CPG blocks are red-coded (urgent) but only one app section key maps to red, so the urgency of the newborn resuscitation pathway would be flattened to navy.  
  _fix:_ Either name the STOP block immediateAction (red) and extend the renderer's red set to include newbornResuscitation and the hr* branch keys, or add an explicit per-section colour hint, so the red CPG blocks are not shown as routine navy.
- [medium] render-risk p63 — Threshold operators are arrow glyphs (↓ = less than, ↑ = greater than) which a text-layer extraction can drop, silently inverting or erasing clinical thresholds.  
  _fix:_ Preserve the ↓/↑ glyphs verbatim, matching the existing repo convention in contentData.js (e.g. '↑6 years AND ↑20 kg'); verify every arrow digit-by-digit against the page image rather than the text layer.
- [medium] render-risk p62 — The CPG's two- and three-column IF/THEN rows carry long text in both columns and will degrade to stacked record cards on a 375px phone if emitted as uniform objects.  
  _fix:_ Flatten each conditional to a single string of the form 'IF <condition> → <action>' inside a string array rather than a { headers, rows } table or an array of objects, so it renders as a readable bullet list at 375px.
- [medium] missing-graphic p63 — The purple medication banner rows that label which oxygen delivery applies to each pathway are absent.  
  _fix:_ Keep the oxygen/BVM line as the first item of each pathway array, prefixed with the medication marker (💊) already used elsewhere in contentData.js, so the change from room air to 5L O2 is not lost.
- [medium] missing-graphic p60 — The CPG's icon legend (phone, magnifier, plus, ✖, clock, speech bubble, ambulance) encodes the action type of every row and is not carried anywhere.  
  _fix:_ Preserve the semantics inline with the emoji markers used in the proposed content (🔍 assess, ✖ do NOT, 🕐 note time, ☎ consult, 🚑 transport, 💊 medication).
- [low] typo p60 — The CPG contains three spelling errors that must be reproduced knowingly rather than silently 'corrected' — PREPERATION, OBSTERIC, breached.  
  _fix:_ Use corrected section keys (preparation, obstetricHistoryImminentBirth) for the app headings, but keep the body text verbatim including 'breached'; record the CPG typos so a later CPG revision is not mistaken for a data regression.

## `falls-p77` — p77-82 · coverage: partial

**Graphics not represented in the app:**
- p77: Red full-width 'FALLS' title banner with double red rules above and below (the CPG's condition-header treatment).
- p77: Green 'RECOGNITION' header bar with speech-bubble icon over a green-tinted body block (definition colour code).
- p77: Navy 'ASSESSMENT' header bar with clipboard icon over a pale-lavender two-column decision table that runs continuously from p77 to p82.
- p77: Icon-coded disposition column: blue ambulance = Hospital transport, blue telephone = Virtual ED, red cross = treatment/home-care action, magnifier = 'Assess:', speech bubble = question to ask. This icon system is the guideline's actual algorithm.
- p77: Arrow glyphs used as comparison operators throughout (down arrow = less than, up arrow = greater than), e.g. 'GCS ↓ 13', 'LOC ↑ 5 minutes', 'a fall of ↓ than 1 meter'.
- p77: Reverse-video black inline logic chips: IF, ANY, and the '&' conjunction badge.
- p78: Peach/salmon highlighted action row 'IF Required (Refer to spinal trauma) | Spinal immobilisation' with a red cross icon — the only colour-highlighted row in the Falls assessment table.
- p78: Telephone-icon rows marking the Virtual ED head-injury branch (LOC ↓ 5 minutes; Amnesia lasting ↓ 30 minutes), visually separating it from the ambulance-icon Hospital transport branch above.
- p79: Reverse-video black 'UNLESS' qualifier chips in the right-hand column of the vital-signs table ('UNLESS Normal for patient', 'UNLESS Chronic hypoxaemia').
- p80: Reverse-video black 'REGARDLESS' chip qualifying the Virtual ED GCS ↓ 15 row ('REGARDLESS whether or not normal for patient').
- p80: AEIOU TIPS mnemonic laid out as a 12-row table with the leading mnemonic letter bolded down the left column (A-E-I-I-O-U-U-T-I-P-P-S), each row carrying its own disposition icon (ambulance / phone / cross).
- p81: Ambulance-icon 'ANY high-risk factors' block with an 'UNLESS Normal for patient (E.G. dementia)' chip and a 'Preventing assessment' qualifier in the second column.
- p82: Reverse-video black 'BUT' chip on the moderate-risk 'Poor recollection | BUT Normal for patient (E.G. dementia)' row — deliberately mirroring the p81 'UNLESS' row to split the same finding between two dispositions.
- p82: Red-cross action row 'Refer patient to Hatzolah social worker' closing the assessment table.
- p82: Green 'FURTHER NOTES' header bar with list icon over a green-tinted body block of seven asterisk-bulleted notes.

**Confirmed (verified) findings:**

### [critical] missing-section · p78 — The entire Virtual ED disposition pathway is absent from the app. Falls is a three-way disposition algorithm (Hospital transport / Virtual ED / Home care) and the app only carries one of the three arms.
- **CPG:** Rows across pp.78-82 are marked with a telephone icon and the action 'Virtual ED', e.g. 'IF ANY of the following signs — Virtual ED: LOC ↓ 5 minutes; Amnesia lasting ↓ 30 minutes' (p78); a second full vital-sign set (p80); 'IF ANY other syncope / faint — Virtual ED + forward 12 Lead ECG (if available)' (p81); 'IF ANY moderate risk factors — Virtual ED' (p82). p82 further notes: 'Recommend Virtual ED IF patient meets ANY “Virtual ED” criteria & NO “Hospital Transport” criteria'.
- **App:** content has only a `hospitalTransport` array. No `virtualED` key, no 'Home care' outcome, no 'Hospital OR Private transport to PPCC' outcome, and no decision rule explaining how the responder chooses between them.
- **Fix:** Add `virtualED` and `homeCare` keys mirroring `hospitalTransport`, and add a `furtherNotes` entry stating the selection rule: Hospital transport if ANY Hospital Transport criterion; Virtual ED if ANY Virtual ED criterion AND NO Hospital Transport criterion.

### [critical] missing-section · p79 — The abnormal vital signs criteria are almost entirely missing — 12 of the 13 numeric thresholds are absent.
- **CPG:** p79 'Assess: For abnormal vital signs — IF ANY of the following signs — Hospital transport': GCS ↓ 13 (UNLESS Normal for patient); sBP ↓ 90; HR ↑ 120; HR ↓ 50; SpO2 ↓ 90% (UNLESS Chronic hypoxaemia); RR ↑ 30; BGL ↓ 4 despite management. p80 'IF ANY of the following signs — Virtual ED': GCS ↓ 15 (REGARDLESS whether or not normal for patient); sBP ↓ 100; HR ↑ 100 at rest; SpO2 ↓ 92%; RR ↑ 22 at rest; Temperature ↑ 38°C.
- **App:** hospitalTransport contains only 'GCS <13'. No sBP, HR, SpO2, RR, BGL or temperature thresholds at all, and none of the Virtual ED thresholds.
- **Fix:** Add a `vitalSignThresholds` table (or two arrays) reproducing both sets verbatim, preserving the ↓/↑ as < and > and the UNLESS / REGARDLESS qualifiers.

### [critical] missing-section · p78 — The whole spinal injury block is reduced to a single bullet — the spinal immobilisation action and all 8 spinal-injury signs with their examination techniques are gone.
- **CPG:** 'IF Uncontrolled fall — Assess: Spinal injury'; then a colour-highlighted action row 'IF Required (Refer to spinal trauma) — Spinal immobilisation'; then 'IF ANY signs of spinal injury — Hospital transport': Sensory deficits General (Ask: Any numb, tingle, burn OR altered sensation); Sensory deficits Arms (Assess: Light touch across palm & back of hand); Sensory deficits Legs (Assess: Light touch lateral side of calcaneus); Motor deficits Arms (Assess: Ability to grasp, pull & push); Motor deficits Legs (Assess: Ability to plantar flex, dorsiflex & leg raise); Midline tenderness on palpation (Assess: Full length of spine); Can’t rotate neck 45° left & right (E.G. Causes pain); History of spinal abnormality (E.G. Disease, surgery, known weakness OR injury).
- **App:** assessment: 'Assess for spinal injury if uncontrolled fall'. Nothing else — no signs, no examination technique, no immobilisation instruction, and no spinal-injury entries in hospitalTransport.
- **Fix:** Add a `spinalInjury` subsection with the immobilisation action (marked urgent) and a two-column table of the 8 signs and their assessment technique.

### [high] missing-section · p80 — The AEIOU TIPS block is named but never expanded — 12 causes each with its own disposition are missing, and the three the app does carry lose their disposition context.
- **CPG:** 'Assess: Confirm cause of fall (AEIOU TIPS)': Arrhythmia / Cardiac (E.G. STEMI) — Hospital transport; Epilepsy / Seizure — Hospital transport; Insulin (I.E. corrected ↓ BGL) — Virtual ED; Intoxication inhibiting assessment — Hospital transport; Overdose (E.G. deliberate OD) — Hospital transport; Underdose — Virtual ED; Urinary Tract Infection (UTI) — Virtual ED; Trip / Mechanical fall — Home care; Infection — Virtual ED; Pharmacological (Accidental OD) — Virtual ED; Postural changes — Virtual ED; Stroke / TIA — Hospital transport.
- **App:** assessment: 'Assess cause of fall (AEIOUTIPS)' with no expansion. hospitalTransport picks up only 'Suspected STEMI/arrhythmia', 'Epilepsy/seizure' and 'Deliberate OD'. Intoxication inhibiting assessment and Stroke/TIA are missing from the transport list; Insulin, Underdose, UTI, Infection, Pharmacological, Postural changes and Trip/Mechanical fall are absent entirely.
- **Fix:** Add a `causeOfFall` table with headers ['Cause', 'Disposition'] and all 12 rows, with the mnemonic letter emphasised as in the CPG.

### [high] missing-section · p80 — The high-risk syncope / faint criteria (11 symptoms) are missing and are replaced by an inaccurate one-line paraphrase.
- **CPG:** 'IF Fall was caused by syncope / faint — Assess: High risk syncope / faint. IF ANY high risk syncope symptoms — Hospital transport': Nil symptoms prior to fall; Associated with palpitations; Cardiac device (E.G. Pacemaker); Chest pain; Exertional onset; Occurred while seated / supine; Family history of young sudden cardiac death (↓ 50 years); Ischemic OR structural heart disease; Known 2nd° / 3rd° AV block; Known SVT / paroxysmal AF; Known pre-excited QRS’s. 'IF ANY other syncope / faint — Virtual ED + forward 12 Lead ECG (if available)'.
- **App:** assessment: 'Assess for syncope: cardiac history, ECG findings'. None of the 11 named symptoms, and no 'other syncope -> Virtual ED + forward 12 Lead ECG' fallback.
- **Fix:** Add a `syncope` subsection listing all 11 high-risk symptoms verbatim under a Hospital transport heading, plus the 'ANY other syncope / faint -> Virtual ED + forward 12 Lead ECG (if available)' rule.

### [high] missing-section · p82 — The entire FURTHER NOTES block (7 notes) is missing, including the disposition decision rule, the definition of 'uncontrolled fall' that gates the spinal pathway, and the mandatory discharge / safety-netting advice.
- **CPG:** FURTHER NOTES: 'Recommend hospital transport IF patient meets ANY “Hospital Transport” criteria'; 'Recommend Virtual ED IF patient meets ANY “Virtual ED” criteria & NO “Hospital Transport” criteria'; 'Call Ambulance Victoria if hospital transport is required'; 'An “uncontrolled fall” is one where the patients decent was NOT slowed (E.G. by being caught by a bystander who then gently lowered them to the floor)'; 'Hatzolah members are advised to escalate care if they have any further concerns'; a definition of intoxication ('unable to be accurate historians'); and 'All patients who are not attended to by ambulance or VVED, must be advised to see their GP... Ensure the patient can mobilise as per their normal, is left with another person, has access to a phone, has access to the bathroom, food, water, and advised to call for help should they deteriorate or become concerned.'
- **App:** No notes key of any kind. The responder has no rule for choosing a disposition, no definition of 'uncontrolled fall' (which the app's own bullet depends on), and no discharge advice.
- **Fix:** Add a `furtherNotes` array with all 7 notes verbatim (reproduce the CPG spelling 'decent' as-is or flag it separately).

**Medium/low (unverified):**
- [medium] missing-section p81 — 'Risk of falling again' is named in the app but its four determining criteria are missing.  
  _fix:_ Add the four re-fall risk criteria under the existing 'Likely to re-fall within 24 hours' item.
- [medium] missing-section p77 — The 'Cause of fall' and 'Mechanism of fall' history-taking question sets (11 questions with worked examples) are compressed into two lossy bullets.  
  _fix:_ Restore both question sets as a two-column table ['Ask', 'E.G.'], including 'Any attempt to control the fall?', 'Objects struck during fall?', 'Was there a head strike?' and 'Was there a loss of consciousness?'.
- [medium] wrong-value p79 — The app's 'GCS <13' criterion drops the CPG's 'UNLESS Normal for patient' qualifier, and the SpO2 criterion (with its own qualifier) is absent.  
  _fix:_ Carry the qualifier column: 'GCS <13 — UNLESS normal for patient', 'SpO2 <90% — UNLESS chronic hypoxaemia', 'GCS <15 (Virtual ED) — REGARDLESS whether or not normal for patient'.
- [medium] missing-section p77 — Two explicit action rows are missing: the accredited 12-lead ECG and the social worker referral.  
  _fix:_ Add both as explicit management actions — '12 Lead ECG if accredited to do so' and 'Refer patient to Hatzolah social worker'.
- [medium] not-in-cpg p80 — The app's syncope bullet invents criteria that the CPG does not use.  
  _fix:_ Replace with the CPG's 11 named high-risk syncope symptoms.
- [low] not-in-cpg p77 — The app's mechanism bullet asks about 'height', which is not one of the CPG's mechanism questions.  
  _fix:_ Drop 'height' from the mechanism list (it is already covered by the recognition criterion) and restore the four dropped mechanism questions.
- [medium] table-misrendered p77 — The CPG's assessment section is a two-column decision table (criterion -> action/example) but the app flattens it into single-column bullet lists, so every criterion loses the action, example or qualifier attached to it.  
  _fix:_ Model each block as { headers: ['Finding', 'Action'], rows: [...] } so the disposition/example column survives, or as nested subsections keyed by disposition.
- [medium] missing-graphic p77 — The icon-coded disposition column that carries the outcome of every row in the guideline is not represented at all.  
  _fix:_ Encode the disposition per row (e.g. a 'Disposition' column with values Hospital transport / Virtual ED / Home care / PPCC) and colour-code it, mirroring the CPG's icon system.
- [medium] colour-coding p78 — The only colour-highlighted action row in the guideline — the peach/amber 'Spinal immobilisation' row — has no representation and therefore no urgency marking in the app.  
  _fix:_ Add the spinal immobilisation action under a key that the renderer colours red/amber (e.g. `immediateAction`), with the cross-reference to the Spinal Trauma protocol.
- [low] colour-coding p77 — The CPG's two green blocks (RECOGNITION and FURTHER NOTES) will not render green in the app.  
  _fix:_ Either extend the renderer's green list to include `recognition` and `furtherNotes`, or key these blocks so they inherit the green treatment.
- [low] missing-graphic p80 — The CPG's inline logic badges (IF / ANY / UNLESS / REGARDLESS / BUT / &) are reverse-video black chips that carry the boolean logic of the algorithm; none survive into the app.  
  _fix:_ Preserve the qualifier text in a second column and state the ANY/ALL semantics in each block's lead-in line.
- [low] typo p77 — Minor wording drift from the CPG source in the recognition line and two transport criteria.  
  _fix:_ Match the CPG wording exactly, or keep the paraphrase only where it does not widen or narrow the criterion.

## `infection-sepsis-p86` — p86-89 · coverage: partial

**Graphics not represented in the app:**
- p86: Green-headed DEFINITION block with '?' icon and three green asterisk (✱) bullets defining Infection, Sepsis and the PPE/cross-infection rationale. Green = definition colour-coding.
- p86: GENERAL SYMPTOMS OF INFECTION - green-headed two-column table (magnifier icon). Left column symptom, right column worked example: Fever | E.G. Temperature ↑ 37.5°C; Hot and cold flushes | (blank); Myalgia | E.G. Generalised muscle pain; Malaise | I.E. General feeling of being unwell; Fatigue | (blank).
- p86: COMMON SOURCES OF INFECTIONS - SUNWARD mnemonic graphic: a green letter column (S U N W A R D) beside bolded/underlined initial letters of each source, plus a third example column. The letter-column layout is the mnemonic device and is lost in a plain text extract.
- p87: Red STOP banner (white ▶ icon) over a pink/red row: red telephone icon + inset black 'IF' chip, 'Meeting Red Flag vital signs' | 'Call for Ambulance / 000'. Red = immediate action colour-coding.

**Confirmed (verified) findings:**

### [critical] missing-section · p86 — The entire SUNWARD 'COMMON SOURCES OF INFECTIONS' mnemonic is absent from the app.
- **CPG:** COMMON SOURCES OF INFECTIONS - 'S Surgery / Hospital acquired | E.G. Recent surgery / stay in hospital', 'U Urinary | E.G. Painful / discoloured / malodorous urination', 'N Neurological | E.G. Headache, neck stiffness, photophobia', 'W Wounds | E.G. Lacerations, pressure ulcers, cellulitis', 'A Abdominal (I.E. Gastrointestinal) | E.G. Nausea, vomiting, diarrhoea, abdo. cramping', 'R Respiratory | E.G. SOB, coughing, sneezing, sore throat, rhinitis', 'D Device | E.G. Pacemakers, insulin pumps, urinary catheters'
- **App:** Nothing. content has only sirsCriteria, management, infectionControl. No source-of-infection content of any kind.
- **Fix:** Add a mnemonic section, e.g. content.commonSources = { headers: ['','Source','Example'], rows: [['S','Surgery / Hospital acquired','E.G. Recent surgery / stay in hospital'],['U','Urinary','E.G. Painful / discoloured / malodorous urination'],['N','Neurological','E.G. Headache, neck stiffness, photophobia'],['W','Wounds','E.G. Lacerations, pressure ulcers, cellulitis'],['A','Abdominal (I.E. Gastrointestinal)','E.G. Nausea, vomiting, diarrhoea, abdo. cramping'],['R','Respiratory','E.G. SOB, coughing, sneezing, sore throat, rhinitis'],['D','Device','E.G. Pacemakers, insulin pumps, urinary catheters']] } (or the mnemonic [{letter, meaning}] shape) so SUNWARD survives.

### [high] missing-section · p88 — CONTACT PRECAUTIONS - a whole sub-block of Infection Prevention and Control - is missing from the app.
- **CPG:** '✚ CONTACT PRECAUTIONS - Must be used in addition to standard precautions whenever there is a risk of direct or indirect contact transmission of pathogenic microorganisms (E.G. possible contact with body fluid OR patients with known / possible contact transmissible disease)' then: Gloves; Gown / apron – if splash is likely; Eye protection (I.E. safety goggles) – if splash is likely; Fluid resistant mask – if splash is likely; Appropriate distancing to avoid splash contamination; Ensure infected areas of the patient's body are appropriately contained and covered; Communication of patient's infectious status to other health providers
- **App:** content.infectionControl has only 'standard' and 'dropletAirborne'. There is no contact-precautions key, so gloves, gown/apron, eye protection and fluid-resistant mask are nowhere in the app.
- **Fix:** Add content.infectionControl.contact = { description: 'Must be used in addition to standard precautions whenever there is a risk of direct or indirect contact transmission of pathogenic microorganisms (E.G. possible contact with body fluid OR patients with known / possible contact transmissible disease)', items: [ ...all 7 CPG bullets verbatim ] }.

### [high] missing-section · p89 — Droplet & airborne precautions are reduced from 8 bullets to 3, and the 'ANY of the following criteria' trigger list is dropped entirely.
- **CPG:** 'Must be used in addition to standard precautions with all patients meeting ANY of the following criteria': Sneezing / coughing; Vomiting; Known / suspected to have a droplet OR airborne transmissible disease regardless of their current symptoms (as such diseases can be spread simply by talking or breathing). 'These additional precautions include': Staff immunisations; P2 / N95 facemask / filtering respiratory; Patient must wear a mask covering both their mouth and nose – and leave it on; Patient to practice respiratory hygiene (I.E. covering mouth when sneezing and cleaning hands afterwards); Eye protection (I.E. safety goggles); Appropriate distancing from other patients; Appropriate distancing from medical providers whenever possible; Extra cleaning of equipment and environment after patient use
- **App:** "dropletAirborne": ["P2/N95 mask for staff","Patient must wear mask","Staff immunisations"] - 3 of 8 precautions, no trigger criteria, and no statement that these are additional to standard precautions.
- **Fix:** Replace with { description: 'Must be used in addition to standard precautions with all patients meeting ANY of the following criteria', criteria: ['Sneezing / coughing','Vomiting','Known / suspected to have a droplet OR airborne transmissible disease regardless of their current symptoms (as such diseases can be spread simply by talking or breathing)'], precautions: [ all 8 CPG bullets verbatim ] }.

### [high] missing-section · p87 — The third MANAGEMENT row - the disposition for patients meeting neither SIRS nor immunocompromised - is missing, so the app gives no guidance for the commonest case.
- **CPG:** MANAGEMENT: 'IF None of the above | Refer to GP as minimum'
- **App:** "management": only three lines, ending at 'If immunocompromised → VVED minimum'. No 'none of the above' row.
- **Fix:** Add 'If none of the above → refer to GP as minimum' as the final management item.

### [high] missing-section · p86 — The green DEFINITION block (infection, sepsis, and the PPE / cross-infection rationale) is entirely absent.
- **CPG:** DEFINITION: 'Infection refers to the body becoming invaded by harmful microorganisms. These microorganisms multiply, cause damage to the body tissue and trigger an immune response which itself may become detrimental to the body.' / 'Sepsis is a life-threatening condition that occurs when the body's own response to an infection injures its own tissues and organs. Sepsis can lead to shock, multiple organ failure and death.' / 'In some circumstances – if managed incorrectly – infections may spread between people. For this reason, certain precautions and Personal Protective Equipment (PPE) must be maintained by Hatzolah staff to ensure patient and staff safety.'
- **App:** No definition key at all; content starts at sirsCriteria.
- **Fix:** Add content.definition as an array of the three CPG paragraphs verbatim. The key name 'definition' also picks up the app's green section colouring, matching the CPG's green DEFINITION block.

### [high] missing-section · p86 — GENERAL SYMPTOMS OF INFECTION (the recognition list) is entirely absent from the app.
- **CPG:** GENERAL SYMPTOMS OF INFECTION: 'Fever | E.G. Temperature ↑ 37.5°C'; 'Hot and cold flushes'; 'Myalgia | E.G. Generalised muscle pain'; 'Malaise | I.E. General feeling of being unwell'; 'Fatigue'
- **App:** Nothing - the app has no recognition/symptoms section, so a responder cannot see what infection looks like, only the SIRS vitals.
- **Fix:** Add content.generalSymptoms as { headers: ['Symptom','Example'], rows: [['Fever','E.G. Temperature >37.5°C'],['Hot and cold flushes',''],['Myalgia','E.G. Generalised muscle pain'],['Malaise','I.E. General feeling of being unwell'],['Fatigue','']] }.

**Medium/low (unverified):**
- [medium] missing-section p88 — Standard precautions list drops 2 of the CPG's 7 bullets and truncates the explanatory parentheticals of the rest.  
  _fix:_ Restore all 7 bullets with the CPG's full wording, and add the intro 'Must be used with all patients and includes'.
- [medium] wrong-value p87 — SIRS heart-rate threshold operator is wrong: the CPG's up-arrow (rendered as '>' everywhere else in the same list) has become '≥'.  
  _fix:_ Change to 'HR >90 BPM' so the arrow-to-operator mapping is consistent with the other four criteria in the same list.
- [medium] colour-coding p87 — The CPG's red STOP block (call 000 for red-flag vitals) is folded into the navy MANAGEMENT list, losing its critical styling and its precedence.  
  _fix:_ Move it into its own key that the renderer colours red (e.g. content.immediateAction = ['If meeting Red Flag vital signs → Call for Ambulance / 000']) and leave only the three VVED/GP dispositions under management.
- [low] typo p87 — The CPG's respiratory-rate SIRS criterion carries no comparison glyph at all; the app has silently inserted '>'.  
  _fix:_ This is a defect in the CPG source (the arrow appears to have been dropped in authoring). Do not silently invent the operator: either mirror the CPG ('RR 20 BPM') or annotate it, and raise it with the CPG authors rather than resolving it from general medical knowledge.
- [low] typo p89 — Two source-side typos on p89 that any re-extraction must reproduce rather than silently correct.  
  _fix:_ When restoring the droplet/airborne list, quote the CPG bullets verbatim and raise both typos with the CPG authors; do not add 'for staff'.
- [low] render-risk p87 — The SIRS two-condition structure is weakened: the CPG's inset black '&' chip binding 'suspect infection' to '2 or more abnormal vitals' is flattened into one sentence.  
  _fix:_ Keep the two conditions as separate lines joined by an emphasised 'AND', e.g. description: 'Suspect infection' / 'AND they have 2 or more of the following abnormal vital signs'.

## `head-trauma-p104` — p104 · coverage: partial

**Graphics not represented in the app:**
- p104: The entire page is one colour-coded IF -> THEN decision table: a narrow left icon column (blue ambulance icon on each of the three disposition rows, blue pill/capsule icon on the medication row), a black 'IF' badge, a criteria column, and a right-hand OUTCOME column carrying the action ('Treat as time critical', 'Transport to hospital', 'Virtual ED', 'Pain Relief, PRN'). The final outcome cell 'Pain Relief, PRN' is a filled indigo/purple block with bold white text. The app has no representation of this IF->THEN criteria-to-action mapping; it stores a single flat array named timeCritical.
- p104: Blue telephone-handset icon marking the first action line under the time-critical block: 'SitRep: Hatzolah dispatch + call ambulance'. This is the only action attached to the time-critical criteria and it is icon-marked rather than bulleted.
- p104: Two-level bullet hierarchy: 'Skull fracture' is a parent criterion with five indented sub-bullets (Blood leaking from ear / CSF leaking from the ears OR nose / Battle sign / Racoon eyes / Other obvious sign of skull fracture). The indentation is the only thing that shows these are signs OF skull fracture.
- p104: Red page title banner 'HEAD TRAUMA' bounded by two red rules, above a navy 'MANAGEMENT – HEAD INJURY' bar.

**Confirmed (verified) findings:**

### [critical] missing-section · p104 — The entire 'Transport to hospital' criteria block is absent from the app.
- **CPG:** 'IF Meeting ANY of the following criteria — Transport to hospital': 'Altered mental status (from baseline)', 'Dangerous mechanism of injury', 'Amnesia (up-arrow) 29 minutes', 'Intoxication'.
- **App:** Nothing. content has only timeCritical and management; no transport/disposition criteria of any kind.
- **Fix:** Add content.transportToHospital = ['Altered mental status (from baseline)', 'Dangerous mechanism of injury', 'Amnesia >29 minutes', 'Intoxication'] with a heading that reproduces the CPG's 'Meeting ANY of the following criteria' framing.

### [critical] missing-section · p104 — Two time-critical criteria — 'Seizure' and 'Worsening sign and symptoms' — are missing from the app's timeCritical list.
- **CPG:** Under 'Treat as time critical', after 'Neurological deficit': '- Seizure' and '- Worsening sign and symptoms'.
- **App:** content.timeCritical ends at 'Neurological deficit'. Neither 'Seizure' nor 'Worsening sign and symptoms' appears anywhere.
- **Fix:** Append 'Seizure' and 'Worsening sign and symptoms' to content.timeCritical.

### [critical] missing-section · p104 — The action attached to the time-critical criteria — SitRep to Hatzolah dispatch and calling an ambulance — is missing entirely.
- **CPG:** First line under 'Treat as time critical', marked with a telephone icon: 'SitRep: Hatzolah dispatch + call ambulance'.
- **App:** No escalation field, no SitRep instruction, no 'call ambulance' text anywhere in the protocol.
- **Fix:** Add content.escalation (or an immediateAction entry) = 'SitRep: Hatzolah dispatch + call ambulance' so the time-critical block states what to DO, not just what to recognise.

### [high] not-in-cpg · p104 — All four items in the app's 'management' array appear nowhere on the Head Trauma page.
- **CPG:** Page 104 contains only the four IF->THEN rows (time critical / transport to hospital / Virtual ED / Pain Relief, PRN). There is no spinal-alignment, bleeding-control, suction or airway-adjunct instruction on this page.
- **App:** content.management = ['Maintain neutral spine alignment', 'Control life-threatening bleeding + dress wound', 'Suction airway if necessary (avoid stimulating gag reflex)', 'Airway adjunct only if necessary for ventilation'].
- **Fix:** Remove these four steps from head-trauma, or move them to the protocol whose CPG page actually carries them (they are not on p104). Replace content.management with the CPG's actual management row: 'Pain Relief, PRN' plus the disposition actions.

### [high] missing-graphic · p104 — The page's IF -> THEN structure (four criteria groups each mapping to a distinct disposition/action) is lost; the app flattens one group into a bare list.
- **CPG:** The page is a decision table: each ambulance-icon row reads 'IF Meeting ANY of the following criteria' on the left and its outcome on the right — 'Treat as time critical', 'Transport to hospital', 'Virtual ED' — and the pill-icon row reads 'IF Required' -> 'Pain Relief, PRN'.
- **App:** A single flat array key 'timeCritical' plus an unrelated 'management' array. No criteria-to-outcome pairing, no disposition tiers, no icons.
- **Fix:** Model the page as three (or four) named criteria sections keyed to their outcome, e.g. timeCritical / transportToHospital / virtualED / painRelief, each rendered with its CPG outcome label as the section title.

### [high] missing-section · p104 — The entire 'Virtual ED' criteria block is absent from the app.
- **CPG:** 'IF Meeting ANY of the following criteria — Virtual ED': 'Age (up-arrow) 64', 'Anticoagulant OR antiplatelet (not including aspirin)', 'Coagulopathy'.
- **App:** Nothing. No Virtual ED referral pathway, no age criterion, no anticoagulant/antiplatelet or coagulopathy criterion.
- **Fix:** Add content.virtualED = ['Age >64', 'Anticoagulant OR antiplatelet (not including aspirin)', 'Coagulopathy'].

**Medium/low (unverified):**
- [medium] missing-section p104 — The only treatment row on the page — 'IF Required: Pain Relief, PRN' — is missing.  
  _fix:_ Add a treatment entry 'Pain Relief, PRN (if required)' cross-referencing the Pain Relief (Non-Cardiac) protocol.
- [medium] missing-section p104 — The catch-all skull-fracture sign 'Other obvious sign of skull fracture' is missing.  
  _fix:_ Add 'Other obvious sign of skull fracture' as the last skull-fracture sign.
- [low] table-misrendered p104 — The skull-fracture sub-bullets are promoted to top level, so the app loses the fact that they are signs OF skull fracture.  
  _fix:_ Nest them, e.g. { 'Skull fracture': ['Blood leaking from ear', 'Cerebral Spinal Fluid (CSF) leaking from the ears OR nose', 'Battle sign (bruising on the skull behind the earlobes)', 'Racoon eyes (bruising under the eyes)', 'Other obvious sign of skull fracture'] }, so the renderer produces a nested subsection.
- [low] colour-coding p104 — The time-critical block renders navy in the app, carrying no urgency emphasis despite being the highest-acuity content on a red-bannered page.  
  _fix:_ Either add 'timeCritical' to the red-mapped section keys in the renderer, or key the block as 'immediateAction' so it renders red.
- [low] typo p104 — App paraphrases two CPG sign descriptions, dropping wording present in the source.  
  _fix:_ Restore the CPG's exact wording for both bullets.

## `burns-p109` — p109-111 · coverage: partial

**Graphics not represented in the app:**
- p109: Red 'BURNS' title banner with double rules, then three colour-coded section header bars with icons: DEFINITION (green, '?' icon), STOP (red, flag icon), RECOGNITION (green, speech-bubble icon). The green/red coding is the CPG's own severity signalling.
- p109: RECOGNITION two-column bullet layout of the 8 airway-burn suspicion signs (left column: Respiratory distress / Sooty spit / Facial / airway swelling / Hypoxia; right column: Facial / airway swelling / Burns to upper torso, neck or face / Incident occurred in an enclosed space / Singed facial hair). Inline black 'IF' chip glyph in the lead line.
- p109: Final RECOGNITION row reads only 'Burns surface area' (magnifier bullet) with no text, table or figure following it — the remainder of the page below the block is blank white space. This reads as a burn-surface-area figure/estimation aid that is absent from the rendered page.
- p110: MANAGEMENT block rendered as a two-column condition -> action table with a left icon gutter (magnifier, phone, ambulance, pill, blue '+', black 'X') and inline inverted chip glyphs: IF, OR, AS, UNLESS, REGARDLESS, '&'. Indented sub-rows nest under 'Cool burn with CLEAN running water' and 'Warm the patient'.
- p110: Two purple/violet highlighted medication action rows in the MANAGEMENT table: 'Oxygen, NRB (10-15L O2)' and 'Analgesia – As per Pain Relief (Non Cardiac)' — the CPG's visual marker for a drug/therapy action.
- p111: FURTHER NOTES green-headed block (list icon) with 4 asterisk-bulleted notes.

**Confirmed (verified) findings:**

### [critical] wrong-value · p110 — App instructs a non-adherent dressing; the CPG specifies longitudinal clingwrap applied loosely.
- **CPG:** 'Cover burns with longitudinal clingwrap' with sub-row 'Apply loosely to allow for swelling' (MANAGEMENT, p110). The words 'dressing' and 'non-adherent' appear nowhere on p109-111.
- **App:** management[7]: 'Dress burn with non-adherent dressing'
- **Fix:** Replace with 'Cover burns with longitudinal clingwrap' and a sub-item 'Apply loosely to allow for swelling'.

### [critical] missing-section · p109 — The entire RECOGNITION section — the 8 signs used to suspect airway burns — is absent from the app.
- **CPG:** 'Suspect airway burns IF any of the following': Respiratory distress; Sooty spit; Facial / airway swelling; Hypoxia (E.G. irritable, cyanotic); Facial / airway swelling; Burns to upper torso, neck or face; Incident occurred in an enclosed space; Singed facial hair (E.G. eyebrows / lashes, beard). Then 'Any airway burns MUST be treated as Time Critical, SitRep: Hatzolah dispatch + call ambulance'.
- **App:** No recognition key at all. Only timeCritical: 'Airway burns → immediate time critical management' — the responder is told what to do about airway burns but never how to identify them.
- **Fix:** Add a recognition object, e.g. { airwayBurnsSuspectIf: [ 'Respiratory distress', 'Sooty spit', 'Facial / airway swelling', 'Hypoxia (E.G. irritable, cyanotic)', 'Burns to upper torso, neck or face', 'Incident occurred in an enclosed space', 'Singed facial hair (E.G. eyebrows / lashes, beard)' ], note: 'Any airway burns MUST be treated as Time Critical, SitRep: Hatzolah dispatch + call ambulance' }. (The CPG lists 'Facial / airway swelling' twice — dedupe or reproduce, but flag as CPG duplication.)

### [high] not-in-cpg · p110 — App forbids 'butter' — a word that does not appear in the CPG — while omitting the CPG's actual prohibition on dam water.
- **CPG:** 'Do NOT use ice water / dam water' (MANAGEMENT, p110). Reinforced on p111: 'AVOID dirty water (E.G. dam water) as using it increases risk of infection'. 'Butter' appears nowhere on p109-111.
- **App:** management[5]: 'Do NOT use ice, iced water, or butter'
- **Fix:** Replace with 'Do NOT use ice water / dam water' exactly as the CPG states; remove the invented 'butter'.

### [high] not-in-cpg · p110 — 'Do NOT burst blisters' is invented — no blister instruction exists anywhere on p109-111.
- **CPG:** No mention of blisters in DEFINITION, STOP, RECOGNITION, MANAGEMENT or FURTHER NOTES on p109, p110 or p111.
- **App:** management[6]: 'Do NOT burst blisters'
- **Fix:** Delete this line — it is not sourced from the CPG.

### [high] missing-section · p111 — The whole FURTHER NOTES section (p111, four notes) is missing from the app.
- **CPG:** 1) 'If running water is not available, cooling may be achieved by immersing the injury in CLEAN still water, using a spray bottle or applying moist towels'; 2) 'AVOID dirty water (E.G. dam water) as using it increases risk of infection'; 3) 'Aim to remove jewellery prior to swelling occurring (E.G. rings may become stuck)'; 4) 'Advise all patients not being attended by Ambulance Victoria or VVED with minor superficial burns to see their GP'.
- **App:** No notes / furtherNotes key at all.
- **Fix:** Add a notes array containing all four p111 notes verbatim.

### [high] missing-section · p110 — The 'Warm the patient' management block is entirely absent — hypothermia prevention after cooling is lost.
- **CPG:** 'Warm the patient' with sub-rows: 'AS Required — Blanket patient'; 'AS Required — Warm environment (E.G. heaters)'; 'Avoid: Patient shivering'.
- **App:** Nothing. The app jumps from cooling straight to dressing.
- **Fix:** Add 'Warm the patient' with sub-items 'As required: Blanket patient', 'As required: Warm environment (E.G. heaters)', 'Avoid: Patient shivering'.

**Medium/low (unverified):**
- [medium] missing-section p109 — One of the three red STOP safety items — burnt clothing / chemical contamination — is missing.  
  _fix:_ Add the third STOP item verbatim as its own entry.
- [medium] colour-coding p109 — The red STOP safety block is flattened into the first bullet of a navy management list, losing its danger colouring and prominence.  
  _fix:_ Move the three STOP items into a key named dangersAndSafety (renders red per the app's section colouring) instead of burying them in management.
- [medium] missing-graphic p109 — The 'Burns surface area' item — the last RECOGNITION row, with no content rendered beneath it in the CPG — has no counterpart in the app.  
  _fix:_ Carry across the 'Burns surface area' heading and verify against the source PDF whether a surface-area figure/table belongs here that failed to render — do not invent a rule-of-nines chart.
- [medium] missing-section p110 — The explicit upper limit on cooling — 'Do NOT continue cooling after 20 mins' — is missing.  
  _fix:_ Add 'Do NOT continue cooling after 20 mins' as its own step.
- [medium] missing-section p110 — 'Remove jewellery / clothing from burn UNLESS stuck to skin' is missing from management.  
  _fix:_ Add 'Unless stuck to skin: remove jewellery / clothing from burn'.
- [medium] missing-section p110 — The hydration instruction for conscious patients facing a hospital delay is missing.  
  _fix:_ Add 'If conscious + alert AND delay to hospital: encourage patient to maintain hydration'.
- [medium] render-risk p110 — The CPG's condition -> action two-column structure is flattened into a plain bullet list, so qualifiers lose their gating role.  
  _fix:_ Model management as objects of the form { when: 'Regardless of SpO2', action: 'Oxygen, NRB (10-15L O2)' } so the app renders a condition/action table, or keep bullets but lead with the condition so it is scannable at 375px.
- [low] colour-coding p109 — The green DEFINITION block is stored under a 'types' key, so it renders navy rather than green.  
  _fix:_ Rename to a definition key (renders green per the app's colouring) and include the lead sentence 'A burn refers to damage to skin or other tissue primarily due to'.
- [low] wrong-value p110 — Oxygen flow is restated as '10–15 L/min'; the CPG writes '10-15L O2' with no rate unit.  
  _fix:_ Quote the CPG verbatim: 'Oxygen, NRB (10-15L O2)' — regardless of SpO2.
- [low] typo p109 — The CPG's DEFINITION contains the typo 'lighting strikes'; the app silently corrects it to 'lightning'.  
  _fix:_ No clinical impact. Either mirror the CPG wording or keep the correction, but record it as a known deviation so a future re-extraction diff does not flag it as drift.

## `wound-care-p112` — p112-117 · coverage: partial

**Graphics not represented in the app:**
- p112: Colour-coded section banner blocks: green DEFINITION banner (? icon), red STOP banner (▶ icon) with pink-tinted rows, green RECOGNITION banner (speech-bubble icon). Colour encodes urgency.
- p112: Two-column bullet grid under 'Assess wound for:' — left column Type / Dimensions / Mechanism / Contamination, right column Patient risk factors / Range of movement / sensation / Time of injury / Bleeding.
- p112: Two-column bullet grid under 'Suspect infection IF ANY of the following' — left Pain / Swelling / Heat / Redness, right Pus / Odour / Fever. Uses an inline black 'IF' badge glyph.
- p113: Five navy clipboard-icon management blocks (ABRAISION, MAMAL BITES, AVULSION, CUT / LACERATION (MINOR), INCISION), each with '+' plus-glyph action rows and indented sub-bullets. INCISION rows carry inline black 'IF' badges.
- p114: Tetanus prone wounds conditional row rendered as a two-cell layout with inline black 'IF' and 'OR' badges and an up-arrow comparison glyph: 'IF Tetanus status unknown OR ↑5 years | Tetanus injection is required within 24 hours'.
- p114: SKIN TEAR row 'Apply Melolin plus bandage ␣ silicone dressing' — a visible blank gap where a connector glyph (an OR/plus badge) failed to render in the source PDF.
- p116: Green FURTHER NOTES block (list icon) with nested definition sub-lists for Wound age, Wound depth (Superficial / Partial thickness / Full thickness / Deep-complicated) and Wound type (Incision / Laceration / Avulsion / Abrasion / Puncture).
- p117: Photo panel: three product photographs of the supplied dressings with captions and sizes — Silicone foam (Biatain) 7.5 × 7.5 cm and 12.5 × 12.5 cm; Adhesive wound tape (Steri-Strips) 6 × 100 mm strips; Non adherent dressing (Melolin) 10 × 10 cm.

**Confirmed (verified) findings:**

### [critical] missing-section · p114 — The entire TETANUS PRONE WOUNDS - MANAGEMENT block is absent from the app.
- **CPG:** TETANUS PRONE WOUNDS - MANAGEMENT: 'ANY wound other than a clean, minor cut should be treated as a tetanus prone wound'; 'IF Tetanus status unknown OR ↑5 years  Tetanus injection is required within 24 hours'.
- **App:** Nothing. Tetanus is mentioned only in passing inside the mammalBite bullet ('antibiotics + tetanus may be required'). The 24-hour tetanus injection rule and the definition of a tetanus-prone wound do not exist anywhere in the app.
- **Fix:** Add content.tetanusProneWounds = ['ANY wound other than a clean, minor cut should be treated as a tetanus prone wound', 'If tetanus status is unknown OR >5 years since last dose: tetanus injection is required within 24 hours'].

### [critical] missing-section · p112 — The red STOP block (PPE/hand hygiene and the uncontrolled-haemorrhage redirect) is missing entirely.
- **CPG:** STOP (red banner): 'Safety: Ensure PPE is worn and perform 5 moments of hand hygiene'; 'For uncontrolled haemorrhage manage as per General Trauma Approach'.
- **App:** No STOP / safety / dangers section at all. A responder reading this protocol is never told to divert to General Trauma Approach for uncontrolled haemorrhage.
- **Fix:** Add content.dangersAndSafety = ['Safety: Ensure PPE is worn and perform 5 moments of hand hygiene', 'For uncontrolled haemorrhage manage as per General Trauma Approach'] — the key dangersAndSafety renders red, matching the CPG banner.

### [high] missing-section · p112 — The RECOGNITION block is missing: the 8-point wound assessment and the 7 signs of infection.
- **CPG:** RECOGNITION: 'Assess wound for:' Type, Dimensions, Mechanism, Contamination, Patient risk factors, Range of movement / sensation, Time of injury, Bleeding. 'Suspect infection IF ANY of the following': Pain, Swelling, Heat, Redness, Pus, Odour, Fever.
- **App:** No recognition key. The app jumps straight to per-type management, and 'Signs of infection' appears only as an unexplained referral trigger in referToED.
- **Fix:** Add content.recognition = { assessWoundFor: ['Type','Dimensions','Mechanism','Contamination','Patient risk factors','Range of movement / sensation','Time of injury','Bleeding'], suspectInfectionIfAny: ['Pain','Swelling','Heat','Redness','Pus','Odour','Fever'] }.

### [high] missing-section · p113 — CUT / LACERATION (MINOR) - MANAGEMENT is completely absent from content.types.
- **CPG:** CUT / LACERATION (MINOR) - MANAGEMENT: 'Gently clean with 0.9% sodium chloride'; 'Cover with low-absorbent dressing to prevent further trauma and absorb exudate' → 'E.G. Dry island dressing'; 'Refer to GP for review within 24 hours'.
- **App:** content.types has only abrasion, mammalBite, avulsion, puncture, skinTear. Cuts and lacerations — the commonest wound presentation — have no entry.
- **Fix:** Add types.cutLacerationMinor = ['Gently clean with 0.9% sodium chloride', 'Cover with low-absorbent dressing to prevent further trauma and absorb exudate (e.g. dry island dressing)', 'Refer to GP for review within 24 hours'].

### [high] missing-section · p113 — INCISION - MANAGEMENT is completely absent from content.types.
- **CPG:** INCISION - MANAGEMENT: 'IF Contaminated gently clean with 0.9% sodium chloride'; 'Cover with NAD plus dressing OR island dressing'; 'IF Closure is required refer to GP / PPCC / ED for review within 8 hours'.
- **App:** No incision entry, so the 8-hour closure referral window is nowhere in the app.
- **Fix:** Add types.incision = ['If contaminated: gently clean with 0.9% sodium chloride', 'Cover with NAD plus dressing OR island dressing', 'If closure is required: refer to GP / PPCC / ED for review within 8 hours'].

### [high] missing-section · p116 — The whole green FURTHER NOTES page (wound healing principles, primary closure window, facial wounds, wound age/depth/type definitions) is missing.
- **CPG:** FURTHER NOTES: 'Wound healing is optimised when the wound is cleansed to remove debris and devitalised tissue, kept moist, infection is prevented, and the wound has adequate blood supply.'; 'Primary closure: For a clean wound that requires closure (sutures/glue) review within 8 hours is recommended at a PPCC/ED'; 'Wounds to the face may cause unwanted scarring - for cosmetic reasons refer to definitive care'; Wound age (Fresh: up to 8 hours; Old: more than 8 hours); Wound depth (Superficial / Partial thickness / Full thickness / Deep-complicated); Wound type (Incision / Laceration / Avulsion / Abrasion / Puncture definitions).
- **App:** No notes key of any kind. The definitions that tell a responder which of the app's wound-type entries to use are absent, as are both 8-hour timing rules.
- **Fix:** Add content.furtherNotes with the three prose bullets plus nested woundAge, woundDepth and woundType objects reproducing the CPG definitions verbatim.

**Medium/low (unverified):**
- [medium] missing-section p112 — The green DEFINITION block is missing.  
  _fix:_ Add content.definition = [...the three CPG bullets...]; the key name 'definition' triggers the green section colour that matches the CPG banner.
- [medium] missing-graphic p117 — The dressing product photo panel (p117) is not represented in the app in any form.  
  _fix:_ Add content.suppliedDressings as a table with headers ['Dressing','Product','Size'] and rows [['Silicone foam','Biatain','7.5 × 7.5 cm and 12.5 × 12.5 cm'],['Adhesive wound tape','Steri-Strips','6 × 100 mm strips'],['Non adherent dressing','Melolin','10 × 10 cm']].
- [medium] wrong-value p114 — Skin tear: the technique sub-bullet for realigning the flap is missing, and the instruction is truncated.  
  _fix:_ Restore the full step and add the sub-bullet 'Use supplied tweezers and / or gauze moistened with saline'.
- [medium] wrong-value p113 — The mammal bite entry drops the CPG's explicit inclusion of human bites.  
  _fix:_ Rename the rendered label to 'Mammal Bites (including human bites)' — e.g. key mammalBitesIncludingHumanBites, or add a first bullet stating the scope.
- [medium] colour-coding p112 — No section in the app renders red or green, so the CPG's entire urgency colour scheme for this protocol is lost.  
  _fix:_ Once the missing blocks are added, name them so the renderer colours them correctly: 'dangersAndSafety' (red) for the STOP content, 'definition' (green) for the definition block.
- [low] wrong-value p113 — Avulsion referral drops VVED and the 'for further review' qualifier.  
  _fix:_ Change to 'Refer to ED / VVED for further review'.
- [low] wrong-value p115 — The referral block is titled after ED only; the CPG's destination is VVED / ED.  
  _fix:_ Rename the key to referToVvedEd and add the lead line 'Refer to VVED / ED if ANY of the following'.
- [low] wrong-value p113 — Every cleaning step drops the CPG's word 'Gently'.  
  _fix:_ Restore 'Gently' in all five cleaning steps.
- [low] typo p113 — The CPG itself misspells two block headings; recording so the app's corrected spelling is a documented divergence.  
  _fix:_ No app change needed — keep the corrected spelling, but log the source typos for feedback to the CPG authors.
- [low] render-risk p113 — content.types is a nested object of arrays, so on a 375px phone the wound types render as stacked nested subsections with no index.  
  _fix:_ Promote the wound types to a top-level structure the renderer can index (e.g. decision-branch chips via a 'branches' array) so a responder can jump straight to their wound type.

## `clinical-flags-p7` — p7-9 · coverage: partial

**Graphics not represented in the app:**
- p7: Colour-coded red-flag threshold TABLE: red header row (Age | AVPU/GCS | RR | SPO2 | HR | BP), 19 age rows with graded pink/salmon banding grouping age bands (<24h/<3mo, 3-12mo, 1-4y, 5-11y, 12-15y, >15y), and up-arrow/down-arrow glyphs used as the comparison operators (down-arrow = below, up-arrow = above). Red horizontal rules separate the age bands. This is the operative red-flag trigger table for vital signs.
- p8: Ambulance icon in a pink/red-tinted banner at the top of p8 carrying the escalation instruction for persistently out-of-range vital signs.
- p9: Amber section header bar with magnifier icon: 'YELLOW FLAG CRITERIA – PAEDIATRICS ONLY', over a yellow-tinted list with one bullet and one indented sub-bullet.

**Confirmed (verified) findings:**
