### [critical] not-in-cpg · p9 — Seven of the app's eight yellowFlags entries do not appear anywhere in the CPG's yellow flag criteria. The app's yellow flag list is almost entirely invented.
- **CPG:** YELLOW FLAG CRITERIA – ADULTS & PAEDIATRICS lists exactly: 'Ongoing patient or carer concern'; 'Infection not responding to community-based care (e.g. oral antibiotics)'; 'Immunocompromised with suspected infection' (sub: 'Attend hospital with relevant history when appropriate'); 'Surgical procedure within past 14 days'; 'Significant unexplained pain (e.g. ≥5)'; 'Syncope (asymptomatic, normal vital signs, normal ECG)'; 'Abdominal pain'; 'Mental health'; 'Hypertension with systolic blood pressure ≥180'; 'Skill mix: All cases must have at least a Hatzolah First Responder (Level 3) or above present and they must not be under supervision.'
- **App:** yellowFlags: 'Patient declining recommended care', 'Isolated systolic BP 90–100 mmHg (adult)', 'Vomiting not controlled with treatment', 'Pain not controlled with treatment', 'Significant mechanism of injury without obvious injury', 'Isolated SpO2 88–92% (known COPD)', 'Immunocompromised patient', 'Pregnancy >20 weeks with any complication'.
- **Fix:** Delete all seven fabricated entries and replace yellowFlags with the CPG's ten items verbatim, keeping 'Immunocompromised with suspected infection' with its sub-bullet 'Attend hospital with relevant history when appropriate'.

### [critical] missing-section · p9 — Ten CPG yellow flag criteria (adults & paediatrics) are missing from the app, including the 'Skill mix' governance rule and the SBP ≥180 hypertension trigger.
- **CPG:** 'Ongoing patient or carer concern'; 'Infection not responding to community-based care (e.g. oral antibiotics)'; 'Immunocompromised with suspected infection' → 'Attend hospital with relevant history when appropriate'; 'Surgical procedure within past 14 days'; 'Significant unexplained pain (e.g. ≥5)'; 'Syncope (asymptomatic, normal vital signs, normal ECG)'; 'Abdominal pain'; 'Mental health'; 'Hypertension with systolic blood pressure ≥180'; 'Skill mix: All cases must have at least a Hatzolah First Responder (Level 3) or above present and they must not be under supervision.'
- **App:** Only 'Immunocompromised patient' loosely corresponds; the other nine have no counterpart.
- **Fix:** Populate yellowFlags with all ten CPG items verbatim, in CPG order. Keep the key name yellowFlags so the section renders amber.

### [critical] missing-section · p9 — The whole 'YELLOW FLAG CRITERIA – PAEDIATRICS ONLY' section is missing — the app has no paediatric yellow flags at all.
- **CPG:** Amber header 'YELLOW FLAG CRITERIA – PAEDIATRICS ONLY' with: 'Ingestion of a dangerous foreign body- asymptomatic / normal VSS' → sub-bullet 'Including button batteries and magnets'.
- **App:** No key for paediatric yellow flags. yellowFlags is a flat, undivided array.
- **Fix:** Restructure to yellowFlags: { adultsAndPaediatrics: [...10 items...], paediatricsOnly: ['Ingestion of a dangerous foreign body — asymptomatic / normal VSS (including button batteries and magnets)'] }. The yellowFlags key still drives the amber colour and the nested keys humanise to 'Adults And Paediatrics' / 'Paediatrics Only', mirroring the CPG's two header bars.

### [critical] not-in-cpg · p8 — Eight of the fifteen adult/paediatric red flags in the app do not appear in the CPG's RED FLAG CRITERIA – ADULTS & PAEDIATRICS list.
- **CPG:** The p8 list contains exactly thirteen bullets: 'ANY vital sign outside of the Acceptable Vital Sign Values'; 'Stridor'; 'First presentation seizure'; 'Anaphylaxis'; 'Acute coronary syndrome'; 'Ectopic pregnancy'; 'Primary obstetric issue'; 'Stroke / TIA'; 'Sudden onset headache'; 'Unable to walk (when usually able to walk)'; 'Post-tonsillectomy bleeding (of any amount) of up 14 days post-operation'; 'Acute coronary syndrome' (repeated in the CPG); 'Patients at risk (E.G. domestic violence, elder abuse, child abuse)'. No burns, spinal, sepsis, bleeding, cardiac-arrest, penetrating-trauma or fracture entries appear.
- **App:** redFlags.adultsAndPaediatrics contains 'Burns — partial/full thickness or >9% superficial', 'Altered conscious state (not corrected by treatment)', 'Respiratory distress not relieved by treatment', 'Suspected spinal injury', 'Suspected sepsis', 'Active significant bleeding not controlled', 'Traumatic cardiac arrest', 'Penetrating trauma to head, chest or abdomen', 'Suspected fracture of femur or pelvis'.
- **Fix:** Remove all entries not present on p8. Rebuild redFlags.adultsAndPaediatrics from the CPG's thirteen bullets verbatim (the CPG's duplicated 'Acute coronary syndrome' may be collapsed to one entry, but nothing else may be added).

### [high] missing-section · p8 — Six CPG adult/paediatric red flags are missing from the app, including both obstetric ones and the tonsillectomy-bleeding and safeguarding flags.
- **CPG:** 'Ectopic pregnancy'; 'Primary obstetric issue'; 'Sudden onset headache'; 'Unable to walk (when usually able to walk)'; 'Post-tonsillectomy bleeding (of any amount) of up 14 days post-operation'; 'Patients at risk (E.G. domestic violence, elder abuse, child abuse)'.
- **App:** None of these six appear in redFlags.adultsAndPaediatrics.
- **Fix:** Add all six to redFlags.adultsAndPaediatrics using the CPG's own wording (retain the CPG's 'of up 14 days post-operation' phrasing verbatim even though it reads as a typo).

**Medium/low (unverified):**
- [medium] wrong-value p7 — definition.yellow is paraphrased: it drops 'immediately', drops 'Refcom' from the contact list, and changes 'the patients GP' to 'GP'.  
  _fix:_ Replace definition.yellow with the CPG sentence verbatim, including 'immediately' and 'Refcom'.
- [medium] wrong-value p8 — The app's 'Suspected cardiac chest pain' replaces the CPG's 'Acute coronary syndrome' and loses the CPG's sub-bullet qualifier 'Even if resolved'.  
  _fix:_ Change to 'Acute coronary syndrome — even if resolved' (or a nested item with the sub-bullet) in redFlags.adultsAndPaediatrics.
- [medium] wrong-value p8 — The app narrows the CPG's 'Stroke / TIA' red flag to 'Suspected stroke', dropping TIA.  
  _fix:_ Change the entry to 'Stroke / TIA'.
- [medium] wrong-value p8 — The anaphylaxis red flag qualifier is truncated: the app keeps only 'resolved' and drops 'possible anaphylaxis' and 'the post-adrenaline patient'.  
  _fix:_ Change to 'Anaphylaxis — including resolved anaphylaxis, possible anaphylaxis or the post-adrenaline patient'.
- [medium] wrong-value p9 — The paediatric fever red flag uses the wrong comparison operator and rephrases the age qualifier: the CPG's up-arrow glyph means ABOVE 38°c, not '≥38°C'.  
  _fix:_ Change to 'Febrile ↑38°c in small infants (I.E. up to 3 months old)', preserving the ↑ glyph so it matches the arrow convention used elsewhere in the v6.2 data.
- [medium] render-risk p7 — Once added, the p7 threshold table is 6 columns wide and 19 rows deep; on a 375px phone it will overflow horizontally and the ↑/↓ operator glyphs are small enough to be misread at that width.  
  _fix:_ Confirm the table sits in an overflow-x: auto wrapper with the Age column sticky, and verify the ↑/↓ glyphs render legibly at the app's table font size on a 375px viewport. Consider a non-breaking space between glyph and number ('↓ 110 / ↑ 170') so cells do not wrap mid-operator.
- [low] typo p9 — 'Non-blanching rash' is hyphenated in the app but not in the CPG — trivial, but noted because the surrounding items were rewritten rather than transcribed.  
  _fix:_ Optional: match the CPG's 'Non blanching rash' when transcribing the list verbatim. No clinical impact.
- [low] colour-coding p8 — The CPG's escalation banner on p8 is on a red/pink-tinted ground; if it is added to the app under a generic key such as notes it will render navy instead of red, downgrading its urgency.  
  _fix:_ Place the escalation text under redFlags (or immediateAction) rather than notes so it inherits the red section colour.

## `respiratory-assessment-fr-p16` — p16-17 · coverage: partial

**Graphics not represented in the app:**
- p16: Green 'RECOGNITION' section header band with speech-bubble icon, wrapping the whole grading table; the table's own column-header row is a lighter green band with a magnifier icon on 'Category'.
- p16: CROSSWRAPS mnemonic: green rounded letter tiles down the Category column spelling C-R-O-S-S-W-R-A-P-S (Conscious State, Respiratory Rate, Oxygen, Speech, Skin, Work of breathing, Respiratory rhythm, Appearance, Pulse rate, Sounds). Each category label is underlined on its mnemonic letter.
- p16: Black inline 'OR' chips used as a graphical operator inside cells (Conscious State Moderate/Severe, Respiratory Rate Severe, Appearance Mild/Moderate/Severe, Pulse Rate Severe, Sounds Mild/Moderate/Severe).
- p16: Arrow glyphs used as comparison operators throughout the table: up-arrow = greater than, down-arrow = less than (RR up-20 / down-8; SpO2 up-95% / down-95% / down-90%; Pulse up-120 / down-60).
- p16: Pink/red-tinted footnote band with a red asterisk marker carrying the age-scope caveat for Respiratory Rates and Pulse Rates.
- p17: Green header block 'SIGNS OF REPSIRATORY DISTRESS IN CHILDREN INCLUDE' over a pale-green panel with green asterisk bullets (5 items).
- p17: Green header block 'SIGNS OF HYPOXIA IN CHILDREN INCLUDE' over a two-column comparison table with a green sub-header row: 'Infants (0 - 12 months)' vs 'Children (1 - 15 years)', 5 paired rows.

**Confirmed (verified) findings:**

### [critical] wrong-value · p16 — The Speech row is shifted one column: every app value sits one severity band milder than the CPG, so a patient speaking in short sentences grades Mild in the app but Moderate in the CPG.
- **CPG:** Speech — Normal: 'Clear, Steady'; Mild: 'Full Sentences'; Moderate: 'Short Sentences'; Severe: 'Words -OR- None'.
- **App:** ['Speech', 'Clear, full sentences', 'Short sentences', 'Words only', 'None'] — i.e. Normal='Clear, full sentences', Mild='Short sentences', Moderate='Words only', Severe='None'.
- **Fix:** Replace the row with ['Speech', 'Clear, Steady', 'Full Sentences', 'Short Sentences', 'Words OR None']. Note the CPG merges words-or-none into a single Severe cell; do not split it across Moderate and Severe.

### [critical] missing-section · p16 — The Respiratory Rate row — the only numeric breathing criterion in the whole assessment — is absent from the app table.
- **CPG:** R | Respiratory Rate — Normal: '12 - 16'; Mild: '16 -20'; Moderate: 'up-arrow 20'; Severe: 'up-arrow 20 OR down-arrow 8'.
- **App:** No Respiratory Rate row at all; the table starts at Speech.
- **Fix:** Insert ['Respiratory Rate', '12 - 16', '16 - 20', '↑ 20', '↑ 20 OR ↓ 8'] as the second row (after Conscious State). Preserve the arrow glyphs — in this CPG ↑ means 'greater than' and ↓ means 'less than'; do not convert them to > / < silently and do not 'tidy' the 16 boundary that the CPG places in both the Normal and Mild bands.

### [critical] missing-section · p16 — The Oxygen (SpO2) grading row is absent — the app has invented oxygen targets in its place but not the CPG's actual SpO2 severity thresholds.
- **CPG:** O | Oxygen (SpO2) — Normal: 'up-arrow 95%'; Mild: 'up-arrow 95%'; Moderate: 'down-arrow 95%'; Severe: 'down-arrow 90%'.
- **App:** No Oxygen (SpO2) row in the table.
- **Fix:** Insert ['Oxygen (SpO2)', '↑ 95%', '↑ 95%', '↓ 95%', '↓ 90%'] as the third row. Reproduce Normal and Mild as identical ('↑ 95%') exactly as the CPG prints them — do not invent a distinction.

### [high] missing-section · p16 — The Conscious State row — the first row of the CPG table — is missing.
- **CPG:** C | Conscious State — Normal: 'Alert'; Mild: 'Alert'; Moderate: 'Alert OR Altered'; Severe: 'Altered OR Unconscious'.
- **App:** No Conscious State row.
- **Fix:** Insert ['Conscious State', 'Alert', 'Alert', 'Alert OR Altered', 'Altered OR Unconscious'] as the first data row.

### [high] missing-section · p16 — The Pulse Rate row is missing, including the 'late sign' bradycardia warning in the Severe band.
- **CPG:** P | Pulse Rate — Normal: '60 - 100'; Mild: '60 - 100'; Moderate: '100 - 120'; Severe: 'up-arrow 120 OR down-arrow 60 (late sign)'.
- **App:** No Pulse Rate row.
- **Fix:** Insert ['Pulse Rate', '60 - 100', '60 - 100', '100 - 120', '↑ 120 OR ↓ 60 (late sign)'] between Appearance and Sounds. Keep the parenthetical '(late sign)' — it is the CPG's own flag that bradycardia here is pre-arrest.

**Medium/low (unverified):**
- [medium] missing-section p16 — The Respiratory Rhythm row is missing — the CPG's only rhythm criterion (prolonged expiratory phase at every abnormal band).  
  _fix:_ Insert ['Respiratory Rhythm', 'Regular', 'Prolonged Expiratory Phase', 'Prolonged Expiratory Phase', 'Prolonged Expiratory Phase'] between Work of Breathing and Appearance. Reproduce the three identical cells as printed.
- [medium] not-in-cpg p16 — 'tripod position' is invented in the Work of Breathing Severe cell, and 'increase' is added to Mild/Moderate; the CPG prints Moderate and Severe as the same single word.  
  _fix:_ Replace with ['Work of Breathing', 'Normal', 'Slight', 'Marked', 'Marked']. Remove 'tripod position' entirely — it is not in the CPG and it manufactures a Moderate/Severe distinction the CPG does not draw.
- [medium] colour-coding p16 — The CPG wraps the grading table in a green RECOGNITION block, but the app has no section key for it, so the content renders under the generic navy styling and loses the CPG's colour semantics.  
  _fix:_ Nest the grading table (and the p17 panels) under a `recognition` section, and extend the renderer's green-section list to include `recognition` / `paediatricDistressSigns` / `paediatricHypoxiaSigns` so the app mirrors the CPG's green-for-recognition, red-for-caution coding. The age footnote must be the one red element.
- [medium] render-risk p16 — Restoring all 10 CROSSWRAPS rows gives a 5-column table whose Severe cells run to 60+ characters, which will be unreadable on a 375px phone without horizontal scroll on the table itself.  
  _fix:_ Keep the sticky first column and ensure the table scrolls horizontally in its own container rather than wrapping cells to 5+ lines; verify at 375px after restoring all 10 rows. Consider rendering the CPG's inline 'OR' as a line break within the cell rather than a long single line.
- [low] typo p17 — The CPG's own heading misspells 'RESPIRATORY' as 'REPSIRATORY' — flagged so the re-extraction does not silently 'correct' it or, worse, fail to match on it.  
  _fix:_ When adding the p17 section, title it 'Signs of Respiratory Distress in Children' (corrected spelling is acceptable for a heading) but do not alter any clinical value; normalise '16 -20' to '16 - 20' spacing only, leaving the numbers untouched.

## `weight-calculations-p20` — p20 · coverage: partial

**Graphics not represented in the app:**
- p20: Green section-header band 'PAEDIATRIC WEIGHTS' with a white bulleted-list icon, over a pale-green two-row notes block with green asterisk bullets. Green = definition/notes colour coding.
- p20: Inline black inverted badge glyph reading 'IF' inside the sentence 'However [IF] weight is unknown it can be estimated using the following calculations:' - a deliberate typographic emphasis marker.
- p20: Centred boxed lookup table titled 'PAEDIATRIC WEIGHT CALCULATIONS' with a green header bar and magnifying-glass icon, pale-green striped body, 6 rows, 2 unlabelled centred columns (age band -> weight/formula), thin green row rules.
- p20: Down-arrow glyph in table row 1 ('↓ 24 hours') and up-arrow glyph in table row 6 ('↑ 11 years') - the CPG's own comparison-operator convention for 'under' / 'over'.

**Confirmed (verified) findings:**

### [critical] wrong-value · p20 — App invents a '6-12 years' band with the formula (Age x 3) + 7 that does not exist anywhere in the CPG, overestimating paediatric weight and therefore every weight-based drug dose, defibrillation energy and fluid volume.
- **CPG:** The PAEDIATRIC WEIGHT CALCULATIONS table has no 6-12 year band and no 'x 3 + 7' formula. It reads '1 - 9 years | Age x 2 + 8 kg', '10 - 11 years | Age x 3.3 kg', '↑ 11 years | Estimate based on Pt size'.
- **App:** Row ["6–12 years", "Weight (kg) = (Age × 3) + 7", "8 years = 31 kg"]
- **Fix:** Delete the row. Replace the whole table body with the CPG's six rows: ["↓ 24 hours","3.5 kg"], ["3 months","6 kg"], ["6 months","8 kg"], ["1 - 9 years","Age x 2 + 8 kg"], ["10 - 11 years","Age x 3.3 kg"], ["↑ 11 years","Estimate based on Pt size"].

### [critical] wrong-value · p20 — The 'Age x 2 + 8' formula is scoped to 1-5 years in the app but 1-9 years in the CPG, so children aged 6-9 are routed to the wrong (invented) formula.
- **CPG:** '1 - 9 years | Age x 2 + 8 kg'
- **App:** Row ["1–5 years", "Weight (kg) = (Age × 2) + 8", "3 years = 14 kg"]
- **Fix:** Change the age band to '1 - 9 years'. Worked consequence: an 8-year-old is 8 x 2 + 8 = 24 kg per the CPG, but the app's 6-12 year row yields 8 x 3 + 7 = 31 kg - a 29% overestimate.

### [critical] wrong-value · p20 — App replaces the CPG's fixed infant weights with a '(Age in months + 9) / 2' formula that the CPG does not contain, and its worked value for 6 months contradicts the CPG.
- **CPG:** '6 months | 8 kg' (a fixed value; there is no months-based formula anywhere on the page).
- **App:** Row ["≤12 months", "Weight (kg) = (Age in months + 9) / 2", "6 months = 7.5 kg"]
- **Fix:** Delete the formula row and use the CPG's three fixed infant/neonate values: '↓ 24 hours = 3.5 kg', '3 months = 6 kg', '6 months = 8 kg'.

### [high] missing-section · p20 — The CPG's two oldest age bands are missing, so the app gives no method at all for estimating the weight of a 10-year-old or older child.
- **CPG:** '10 - 11 years | Age x 3.3 kg' and '↑ 11 years | Estimate based on Pt size'
- **App:** Nothing. The app's table stops at '6–12 years' and has no over-11 guidance.
- **Fix:** Add rows ["10 - 11 years","Age x 3.3 kg"] and ["↑ 11 years","Estimate based on Pt size"], keeping the up-arrow operator glyph ('↑ 11 years' = over 11 years).

### [high] missing-section · p20 — The entire 'PAEDIATRIC WEIGHTS' green notes block is missing, including the instruction that it is acceptable to ask a parent for the patient's weight - the CPG's first-choice method before any estimation.
- **CPG:** 'For children various treatments are based on body weight, such as drug doses, defibrillation joules and fluid volume. It is acceptable to ask a parent the patient's weight.' and 'However [IF] weight is unknown it can be estimated using the following calculations:'
- **App:** content has only a 'table' key; no notes, no definition, no prose of any kind.
- **Fix:** Add a green-rendered block, e.g. content.definition or content.paediatricWeights, carrying both sentences verbatim, placed above the table. The CPG sets 'IF' as an inverted black badge - render it as emphasis (e.g. bold/uppercase 'IF').

**Medium/low (unverified):**
- [medium] not-in-cpg p20 — The app adds an 'Example' column with worked answers that appear nowhere in the CPG, and two of the three examples are numerically wrong against the CPG's own values.  
  _fix:_ Remove the 'Example' column. Do not synthesise worked examples that the CPG does not print.
- [medium] table-misrendered p20 — Column headers 'Age / Formula / Example' are invented; the CPG table carries a title bar instead of column headers and has only two columns.  
  _fix:_ Use a two-column table. Either drop headers entirely and title the block 'Paediatric Weight Calculations', or use neutral headers such as ['Age', 'Weight'] - the second column mixes fixed weights, formulas and prose, so 'Formula' is inaccurate for 4 of 6 rows.
- [medium] colour-coding p20 — Both CPG blocks on this page are green (definition/notes coding); the app renders its only section as a bare 'table' key, which the renderer colours navy.  
  _fix:_ Nest the prose under a 'definition' key (green per the renderer's section-colouring rules) and keep the table beneath it, so the page's green coding survives.
- [low] render-risk p20 — A 3-column table with two long-text columns will squeeze or horizontally scroll on a 375px phone; the CPG's own layout is two short centred columns.  
  _fix:_ Reducing to the CPG's two columns (age band | weight/formula) also fixes the mobile rendering; longest cell then fits comfortably at 375px with the sticky first column.

## `airway-obstruction-fr-p37` — p37-38 · coverage: partial

**Graphics not represented in the app:**
- p38: Red 'ESCALATION OF CARE' block with red telephone icon: IF No immediate improvement -> 'SitRep: Hatzolah dispatch + call ambulance'.
- p38: Green 'FURTHER NOTES' block with green asterisk bullets, 3 notes: do NOT place fingers in mouth (bite reflex); if pulse NOT found or lost at any stage manage as per Cardiac Arrest; if patient recovers from basic first aid and ambulance is not attending, a minimum of a VVED must occur.

**Confirmed (verified) findings:**

### [critical] wrong-value · p37 — Box B management is wrong: the app instructs abdominal thrusts (Heimlich); the CPG specifies CHEST thrusts. 'Abdominal', 'Heimlich' and 'abdominal thrusts' appear nowhere on pages 37-38.
- **CPG:** BOX B: 'Back blows, up to 5' / 'IF Still obstructed — Chest thrusts, up to 5' / 'IF Still obstructed — Repeat back blows + chest thrusts, as above'
- **App:** "5 back blows, then 5 abdominal thrusts (Heimlich). Repeat. Paediatric: gravity assist if safe."
- **Fix:** Replace the Box B action with the CPG's own sequence: paediatric gravity assist first if safe, then 'Back blows, up to 5', then 'IF still obstructed: Chest thrusts, up to 5', then 'Monitor for clearance / deterioration', then 'IF still obstructed: repeat back blows + chest thrusts as above'. Remove every reference to abdominal thrusts / Heimlich.

### [critical] missing-section · p38 — The entire green FURTHER NOTES block is absent from the app, including the bite-reflex safety warning and the standing instruction to convert to Cardiac Arrest management if the pulse is lost at any stage.
- **CPG:** FURTHER NOTES: 'Do NOT place fingers in patient's mouth as this may trigger a bite reflex'; 'If pulse is NOT found or is lost at any stage manage as per Cardiac Arrest'; 'If the patient recovers from basic first aid and ambulance is not attending, a minimum of a VVED must occur'
- **App:** content has only 'critical' and 'flowchart'; no notes key at all
- **Fix:** Add a notes array with the three FURTHER NOTES verbatim. The pulse-loss note is a safety-critical standing rule that applies across all three boxes, not just the initial STOP triage.

### [high] missing-section · p38 — The red ESCALATION OF CARE block is missing entirely — the app never tells the responder to SitRep Hatzolah dispatch and call an ambulance.
- **CPG:** ESCALATION OF CARE — 'IF No immediate improvement    SitRep: Hatzolah dispatch + call ambulance'
- **App:** no escalation key
- **Fix:** Add escalation: 'No immediate improvement — SitRep: Hatzolah dispatch + call ambulance' (or an immediateAction/escalation section so it renders red).

### [high] missing-section · p37 — Box A drops the ventilation/oxygenation step, which the CPG highlights in a darker band — the app never mentions Oxygen, BVM or ventilations, nor the repeating 5 compressions : 2 ventilations cycle.
- **CPG:** BOX A: 'IF Nil improvement & Pulse present — Oxygen, BVM (8-15L O2), x2 Ventilations' (highlighted row) and 'AS Required — IF Pulse present — Repeat: 5x compressions + x2 ventilations'
- **App:** "If accredited: laryngoscopy + Magill forceps. If not/fails: external chest compressions x5. Assess for clearance." — stops after the first cycle
- **Fix:** Add to Box A: 'IF nil improvement AND pulse present: Oxygen, BVM (8-15L O2), x2 ventilations' and 'As required, if pulse present: repeat 5x compressions + x2 ventilations'. Keep the 8-15L O2 flow rate verbatim.

### [high] missing-graphic · p37 — The protocol is a colour-coded three-box algorithm (STOP triage table + BOX A/B/C conditional boxes + red escalation + green notes) built from IF / & / AS logic tokens; the app flattens it into a 4-row condition/action list, losing the box structure, the icons, the conditional logic tokens and the CPG's colour coding.
- **CPG:** Pages 37-38 render as six discrete colour-coded blocks (red STOP, navy BOX A, navy BOX B, navy BOX C, red ESCALATION OF CARE, green FURTHER NOTES), each row prefixed by an IF/&/AS token and an assess/act icon.
- **App:** content.flowchart: array of four {condition, action} objects, plus a plain 'critical' string
- **Fix:** Model the CPG's actual shape: a red STOP triage section with the four routing rows, then three named sections (Box A / Box B / Box C) each holding its own ordered step list, then escalation and notes. Optionally a custom '_flowchart' SVG (hidden key) that reproduces the triage-to-box branching.

**Medium/low (unverified):**
- [medium] missing-section p37 — Three Box A steps are missing: patient positioning, the pulse re-check, and airway suction.  
  _fix:_ Add 'Position: Supine' as the first Box A step, 'Reassess: for palpable pulse' after the clearance assessment, and 'If necessary: suction airway'.
- [medium] wrong-value p37 — Box B counts are stated as fixed quantities; the CPG bolds them as maxima ('up to 5'), and the app also drops the interposed 'Monitor for clearance / deterioration' step between cycles.  
  _fix:_ Use 'up to 5' for both back blows and chest thrusts, and insert 'Monitor for clearance / deterioration' before the repeat step.
- [medium] colour-coding p37 — The CPG's STOP block is red and its FURTHER NOTES block is green, but the app's keys ('critical', 'flowchart') are not in SECTION_COLORS, so every section on this protocol renders navy — nothing on the screen reads as danger.  
  _fix:_ Name the newborn exclusion + triage section with a red-mapped key (e.g. immediateAction / dangersAndSafety) or add the new key to SECTION_COLORS, and place the further notes under a green-mapped key so the app mirrors the CPG's red/green coding.
- [low] wrong-value p38 — Box C's monitoring instruction is truncated to a bare 'Monitor.', losing what is being monitored for.  
  _fix:_ Use 'Monitor for clearance / deterioration' verbatim.
- [low] render-risk p37 — The flowchart array will not render as a table on a 375px phone — both 'condition' and 'action' exceed the 22-character long-text threshold, so isUniformObjectArray returns false and the four branches degrade into stacked record cards under a generic 'Flowchart' heading that matches no CPG section name.  
  _fix:_ Split the long Box A/B/C prose out into their own step-list sections and keep the triage rows short ('Unconscious & NO pulse' -> 'Manage as Cardiac Arrest') so the triage renders as a real two-column table.

## `cardiac-arrest-fr-p66` — p66-71 · coverage: partial

**Graphics not represented in the app:**
- p66: DEFINITION block - green section header bar with green-tinted body panel; two-sentence definition of cardiac arrest.
- p66: RECOGNITION block - green header with a DRABC ladder: green letter badges D/R/A/B/C each with an underlined green word (Dangers, Response, Airway, Breathing, Circulation) and a right-hand findings column (No response / No / ineffective breathing (E.G. Agonal) / No pulse). Two-column badge layout, not plain prose.
- p67: Full-page FLOWCHART: red PRIMARY SURVEY panel (mnemonic badge / Assess / Manage columns) -> connector to lavender 'IF Possible / Gain 360 degree access to patient' bar -> connector into navy 'IF TRAUMATIC ARREST' box -> connector fans LEFT and RIGHT into two parallel navy boxes (RESPONDER 1 = Apply Defibrillator, age appropriate pads; RESPONDER 2 with 'IF MULTIPLE CREW' badge = CPR compressions 100-120 per minute) -> both converge into the navy 'DEFIBRILLATOR + CPR' box.
- p67: Red/white D R S A B C letter badges in the PRIMARY SURVEY panel (Dangers, Response, Send for help, Airway, Breathing, Circulation) as inline coloured square glyphs.
- p67: Inline black 'IF', 'OR', '&' badge glyphs and up-arrow / down-arrow comparison glyphs used as greater-than / less-than operators throughout the flowchart.
- p67: Page-bottom continuation strip: full-width row of small down-arrows indicating the DEFIBRILLATOR + CPR box continues onto p68.
- p68: Page-top continuation strip: full-width row of small up-arrows matching the p67 bottom strip (same DEFIBRILLATOR + CPR box).
- p68: ROSC block - red section header with triangle glyph and salmon body panel, containing an embedded navy highlight cell 'Oxygen, BVM (15L O2)' and a five-row age-banded ventilation-rate ladder (every X seconds OR Nx per minute).
- p69: Red/salmon note panel under the table with red asterisk bullets: 'DO pause for ventilations if NO LMA being used' / 'Do NOT pause for ventilations if LMA is being used'.
- p70: PAEDIATRIC CARDIAC ARREST RATES - 12+ Years: green banded table, same 6 columns, 5 rows (12, 13, 14, 15 years Adolescent; 16+ years Adult), each Ratio cell carrying three lines (30 : 2 / 30 : 2 / LMA, 15 : 2 NO pause).
- p70: Red/salmon note panel repeating the LMA pause rules under the 12+ years table.
- p71: FURTHER NOTES block - green header, first row rendered as a solid RED banner (white bold text) for the certify/verify-death rule, followed by green-tinted asterisk-bulleted notes.

**Confirmed (verified) findings:**

### [critical] missing-section · p67 — The entire DEFIBRILLATOR + CPR box - defibrillator application, AED instruction-following, and shock delivery with its safety warning - is absent from the app.
- **CPG:** 'DEFIBRILLATOR + CPR' box: 'Follow instructions: From AED / defibrillator'; 'IF Shock advised' -> 'Ensure NO ONE touching patient prior / during shock (includes stopping compressions)' and 'Press: Shock'. RESPONDER 1 box: 'Apply: Defibrillator - Age appropriate pads'.
- **App:** Nothing. content.cpr contains only compression rate, hand technique and ventilation ratios. The words defibrillator, AED, pads and shock do not appear anywhere in the protocol JSON.
- **Fix:** Add a red-coloured defibrillation section reproducing the box verbatim: apply defibrillator with age appropriate pads; follow instructions from AED/defibrillator; IF shock advised - ensure NO ONE is touching the patient prior to / during shock (includes stopping compressions), then press Shock.

### [critical] missing-section · p68 — The 2-minute resuscitation cycle - repeat from the top of the DEFIBRILLATOR + CPR box, rotate compressors, and pulse check when no shock advised - is missing.
- **CPG:** 'Repeat EVERY 2 minutes - From top of this box (I.E. DEFIBRILLATOR + CPR)'; 'IF Multiple officers - Alternate: CPR responder 2 minutely'; 'IF No shock advised - Check for pulse (in [down-arrow] 10 seconds)'.
- **App:** No mention of a 2-minute cycle, of alternating compressors, or of a pulse check when no shock is advised. content.rosc only says 'Stop CPR when pulse returns'.
- **Fix:** Add these three lines: repeat every 2 minutes from the top of the Defibrillator + CPR sequence; if multiple officers, alternate the CPR responder 2-minutely; if no shock advised, check for a pulse in under 10 seconds.

### [critical] wrong-value · p67 — The compression hand-technique age threshold is wrong: the app puts 4-year-olds on 2-hand technique, while the CPG puts them on 1 hand.
- **CPG:** 'IF [up-arrow] 4 years old - 2 hands technique' and 'IF [down-arrow] 5 years old - 1 hand technique' (over 4 / under 5). Confirmed by the p69 table: '4 years / Small Child / 1 hand' and '5 years / Medium Child / 2 hands'.
- **App:** content.cpr: '≥4 years old → 2-hand technique' and '<5 years old → 1-hand technique' - self-contradictory for a 4-year-old, and ≥4 contradicts the CPG's own table.
- **Fix:** Change to '>4 years old → 2 hands technique' (or '≥5 years old') and '<5 years old → 1 hand technique', matching the p69 table where 4 years = Small Child = 1 hand.

### [critical] missing-section · p71 — The whole FURTHER NOTES page is missing, including the red-banner medico-legal rule that First Responders do not certify or verify death.
- **CPG:** Red banner: 'First Responders do not certify or verify death. IF CPR is being withheld, contact the on-call Clinician immediately for approval.' Plus notes on withholding CPR (Advanced Care Directive; obvious death - injuries where survival is impossible, rigor mortis, postmortem lividity, putrefaction/decomposition, death declared by a medical doctor; 'A consult must still occur in these scenarios'), traumatic cardiac arrest correctable causes (haemorrhage control, airway & breathing management, pelvic splinting), 'Appropriate defibrillation and continuous CPR saves lives / Keep all interruptions to compressions to an absolute minimum', hand position 'middle of the chest (lower half of the sternum)', 'CPR depth should be 1/3 depth of the chest', and 'Effective ventilation is guided by rise and fall of the chest with a minimum pause in CPR'.
- **App:** No notes section of any kind.
- **Fix:** Add a 'furtherNotes' section with the red banner rendered as an urgent/red block and the six green notes as bullets.

### [high] missing-section · p70 — The 'PAEDIATRIC CARDIAC ARREST RATES - 12+ Years' table (adolescent and adult rows) is entirely absent.
- **CPG:** 12, 13, 14, 15 years = Adolescent; 16+ years = Adult. All rows: Rescuers 1 and 2, Rate 100 - 120, Technique 2 hands, Ratio '30 : 2 / 30 : 2 / LMA, 15 : 2 NO pause'.
- **App:** content.paediatricTable stops at '5–11 years / Medium Child'. There is no row for 12-15 years or 16+ years anywhere.
- **Fix:** Add the 12+ years rows: 12-15 years Adolescent and 16+ years Adult, 1 and 2 rescuers both 30 : 2, plus 'LMA, 15 : 2 NO pause'.

**Medium/low (unverified):**
- [medium] missing-section p67 — The step 'IF Possible - Gain 360 degree access to patient' between the primary survey and the traumatic-arrest branch is missing.  
  _fix:_ Add 'If possible → gain 360° access to patient' immediately after the primary survey.
- [medium] missing-section p68 — 'Continuously monitor for re-arrest' is missing from the ROSC section.  
  _fix:_ Add 'Continuously monitor for re-arrest' to the rosc list, before the 12-lead ECG item.
- [medium] wrong-value p66 — The recognition text drops the CPG's agonal-breathing example and collapses the D/R/A/B/C recognition ladder.  
  _fix:_ Restore the CPG structure: the 3-signs sentence, then a D/R/A/B/C list with 'Response: No response', 'Breathing: No / ineffective breathing (E.G. Agonal)', 'Circulation: No pulse'.
- [medium] wrong-value p69 — Two Age cells in the paediatric table use the wrong comparison operator - the CPG's down-arrow means 'less than', not 'less than or equal to'.  
  _fix:_ Change to '<24 hours' and '<3 months' to match the down-arrow convention used elsewhere in this protocol ('<10 seconds', '<1 year').
- [medium] missing-section p67 — The RESPONDER 1 / RESPONDER 2 role split, gated by 'IF MULTIPLE CREW', is absent, so single-responder vs multi-crew task allocation is lost.  
  _fix:_ Add the two responder roles as a distinct subsection, preserving the 'IF MULTIPLE CREW' condition on Responder 2.
- [low] colour-coding p68 — The ROSC block is a RED (critical) block in the CPG but renders navy in the app.  
  _fix:_ Rename to a red-mapped key or extend the renderer's red set to include 'rosc'.
- [low] colour-coding p66 — RECOGNITION is a green block in the CPG but renders navy in the app.  
  _fix:_ Map 'recognition' to green, or restore an explicit 'definition' key so at least the definition block carries the CPG's green.
- [low] missing-section p67 — The primary survey mnemonic words are reduced to bare letters, losing 'Send for help' and the Assess/Manage column split.  
  _fix:_ Use three fields per row (step letter + word, assess, manage) so the mnemonic and the CPG's Assess/Manage columns survive.
- [low] render-risk p69 — The 6-column paediatric table overflows a 375px phone, pushing the clinically decisive Ratio column off-screen.  
  _fix:_ Either split back into explicit 1-rescuer and 2-rescuer rows (the CPG layout, already used for Newborn/Infant) or move the constant 'Rate 100-120' into a note so Ratio fits on screen.

## `general-trauma-fr-p102` — p102-103 · coverage: partial

**Graphics not represented in the app:**
- p102: Green 'DEFINITION' block (? icon) containing two green-asterisk bullets of prose defining trauma (blunt vs penetrating; localised vs undifferentiated).
- p102: Green 'PRINCIPLES OF MAJOR TRAUMA' block (list icon) with five green-asterisk bullets: traumatic cardiac arrest correctable causes, C-spine neutral position, ONLY use airway adjuncts, hypothermia/warming, mechanism of injury.
- p103: Nested age-banded ventilation table indented under 'IF Breathing ineffectively' — 5 rows x 3 columns (age band | seconds between breaths | rate in brackets). Age operators are encoded as arrow glyphs: 'up-arrow 15 years old' (over 15) and 'down-arrow 1 year old' (under 1).
- p103: Navy 'MANAGEMENT – HEAD TO TOE ASSESSMENT' table: six magnifier-icon 'Assess:' rows (Head, Spinal, Chest, Abdominal, Pelvic, Limb) with indented conditional sub-rows carrying 'IF Required' chips and cross-references to other guidelines.
- p102: Icon/badge legend used throughout both pages: black 'IF' and 'ONLY IF' inline badge chips encode conditional logic; green asterisk = note; red plus = perform; red X = avoid; magnifier = assess; pill = medication/oxygen. None of this conditional semantics survives into the app's flat bullet strings.

**Confirmed (verified) findings:**

### [critical] missing-section · p103 — The entire 'MANAGEMENT – HEAD TO TOE ASSESSMENT' section (p103, navy block, 6 assess regions + 4 conditional actions) is absent from the app.
- **CPG:** MANAGEMENT – HEAD TO TOE ASSESSMENT: 'Assess: Head injury' → 'IF Required — Manage as per Head Injury guideline'; 'Assess: Spinal injury' → 'IF Required — Manage as per Spinal Injury guideline'; 'Assess: Chest injury' → 'IF Required — Manage as per Chest Injury guideline'; 'Assess: Abdominal injury'; 'Assess: Pelvic injury' → 'IF Required — Apply pelvic splint'; 'Assess: Limb injury'.
- **App:** Nothing. content has only airway, breathing, circulation arrays.
- **Fix:** Add a headToToeAssessment key rendering as a table: { headers: ['Assess', 'If required'], rows: [['Head injury','Manage as per Head Injury guideline'],['Spinal injury','Manage as per Spinal Injury guideline'],['Chest injury','Manage as per Chest Injury guideline'],['Abdominal injury','—'],['Pelvic injury','Apply pelvic splint'],['Limb injury','—']] }.

### [high] missing-section · p102 — The green 'PRINCIPLES OF MAJOR TRAUMA' block (5 bullets) is entirely absent from the FR entry, including the hypothermia warning and the airway-adjunct restriction.
- **CPG:** 'In cases of Traumatic Cardiac Arrest, managing correctable causes is priority including Haemorrhage control, Airway & Breathing Management, and Pelvic Splinting'; 'If possible C-spine injury, maintain the patients head in a neutral position avoiding neck flexion/extension'; 'ONLY utilise airway adjuncts (OP, NP) if airway not patent and ventilation required'; 'Hypothermia is a significant concern leading to increased mortality. Warming the patient is important and where appropriate should NOT be delayed!'; 'The Mechanism of injury is a significant risk factor. Thorough History taking is important to identify such mechanisms and subsequent care/priority.'
- **App:** No principles key at all in general-trauma-fr (the CB entry has a truncated 2-item principles list; the FR entry has none).
- **Fix:** Add a principles array with all five CPG bullets verbatim, placed before the management sections.

### [high] missing-section · p102 — The green DEFINITION block (two paragraphs) is absent from the FR entry.
- **CPG:** 'Trauma refers to bodily injury caused by a sudden application of significant force (E.G. Fall). Such force may be blunt (E.G. Head strike against concrete) OR penetrating (E.G. Punctured by a knife)...'; 'Trauma may also be localised and occur to a specific body region ... OR it may be undifferentiated (E.G. Car crash, falls, etc). Undifferentiated trauma requires a systematic head to toe assessment to ensure no injuries are missed.'
- **App:** No definition key. summary is only 'Full trauma management protocol'.
- **Fix:** Add a definition key with both CPG paragraphs — the renderer colours definition green, matching the CPG. The second paragraph is the rationale for the missing head-to-toe section, so it must land with it.

### [high] missing-section · p103 — The red ✗ AVOID row in Airway Management is missing — the app lists only the four 'do' rows and drops the one prohibition.
- **CPG:** '✗ AVOID causing patient to gag or vomit' (red X icon, full-width row, second row of the Airway Management table).
- **App:** airway array has 4 items: triple airway manoeuvre, suction, NPA, OPA. No AVOID item.
- **Fix:** Add 'AVOID causing patient to gag or vomit' to the airway array, positioned after the triple airway manoeuvre item, and mark it visually as a prohibition (red) rather than a neutral bullet.

### [high] wrong-value · p102 — The two named haemostatic products are replaced by a generic 'haemostatic dressing' and an invented indication ('junctional wounds').
- **CPG:** Two separate rows: 'IF Uncontrollable life threatening wound — Quickclot trauma pad' and 'IF Uncontrollable life threatening wound — Quickclot rolled gauze'.
- **App:** circulation[2] = 'Haemostatic dressing for junctional wounds'.
- **Fix:** Replace with two entries naming the products the CPG names: 'Uncontrollable life threatening wound → Quickclot trauma pad' and 'Uncontrollable life threatening wound → Quickclot rolled gauze'. The CPG never says 'junctional'.

**Medium/low (unverified):**
- [medium] wrong-value p102 — The tourniquet indication is narrowed to limb bleeding; the CPG places no anatomical restriction on it.  
  _fix:_ Change to 'Uncontrollable life threatening bleed → Combat Application Tourniquet'. The word 'limb' is not in the CPG on this page.
- [medium] wrong-value p103 — The SpO2 titration precondition is wrong: the CPG gates it on adequate perfusion, the app gates it on the patient being 'stable'.  
  _fix:_ Change to 'ONLY IF adequate perfusion → titrate down to SpO2 92 - 96%'. 'Once stable' is not a CPG criterion and is looser than the stated perfusion gate.
- [medium] colour-coding p102 — All three management blocks are red (critical/immediate action) in the CPG but render navy in the app, because the keys airway/breathing/circulation are not in the renderer's red list.  
  _fix:_ Rename or alias these keys so the renderer colours them red (e.g. map them into the immediateAction/primarySurvey red group), and keep the head-to-toe key navy to preserve the CPG's own distinction.
- [medium] wrong-value p102 — Section order is inverted relative to the CPG: the CPG puts Major Haemorrhage Control first (before airway), the app puts circulation last.  
  _fix:_ Reorder content keys to haemorrhageControl, airway, breathing, headToToeAssessment to match the CPG's <C>ABC page order.
- [medium] render-risk p103 — The CPG's condition→action two-column structure and its 'IF' / 'ONLY IF' badge distinction are flattened into prose bullets, losing the strength of the conditional.  
  _fix:_ Render each management block as { headers: ['If', 'Action'], rows: [...] } so the trigger and action stay in separate columns. Both columns are short here, so the table renders correctly at 375px rather than degrading to record cards.
- [low] wrong-value p103 — 'Breathing effectively' in the app vs 'Breathing adequately' in the CPG — the CPG's paired terms are ineffectively/adequately.  
  _fix:_ Use the CPG's word: 'If breathing adequately → Oxygen, NRB (10 - 15L O2)'.
- [low] wrong-value p102 — The airway-adjunct rule loses the CPG's second precondition ('and ventilation required').  
  _fix:_ Carry the full principle across when adding the principles array, so the adjunct rule is not read as 'not patent' alone.
- [low] typo p102 — The CPG's own section header is misspelled 'HAEMORHAGE' (one R); any transcription must not silently normalise it if headers are quoted verbatim elsewhere in the app.  
  _fix:_ Title the block 'Major Haemorrhage Control' (correct spelling, as used in the CPG body text); noted here only so the discrepancy is not later mistaken for an app-side error.

## `spinal-trauma-p105` — p105-107 · coverage: partial

**Graphics not represented in the app:**
- p105: Ambulance icon row with black 'IF' badge: 'IF Meeting time critical criteria | Treat as time critical'
- p105: Blue telephone icon row: 'SitRep: Hatzolah dispatch + call ambulance'
- p105: Three-level indented triangle-bullet hierarchy (criterion > Arms/Legs test method > detail) with vertical rules showing nesting depth
- p105: Blue ✗ cross glyph prohibition: 'Do NOT turn the patient's head for them'
- p106: Up-arrow glyph in 'Age ↑ 65 years old' encoding the greater-than comparison operator
- p106: Blue ✚ plus icons marking the three procedure rows (apply cervical collar, consider self-extrication, spinal immobilisation)
- p106: Blue ✗ cross glyph prohibition: 'NEVER independently restrain the patients head to the stretcher'
- p106: Purple/lavender medication bar with pill icon: 'IF ANY spinal immobilization criteria met | Ondansetron, ODT'
- p107: Ondansetron dosing card 1 - grey header bar with pill icon, white 'ALL' badge and 'Prophylaxis for Spinal Immobilisation'; rows Demographic (people icon) '↑ 11 years old', Route 'ODT', Initial Dose '4 mg', Repeat Dose(s) '4 mg @ 20 mins IF Required', Maximum (⊘ icon) '8 mg (total dose)'
- p107: Ondansetron dosing card 2 - same layout; Demographic '5 to 11 years old', Route 'ODT', Initial Dose '4 mg', Repeat Dose(s) 'Nil', Maximum '4 mg (total dose)'
- p107: Ondansetron dosing card 3 - same layout; Demographic '↓ 5 years old', Route 'ODT', Initial Dose '2 mg', Repeat Dose(s) 'Nil', Maximum '2 mg (total dose)'
- p107: Up-arrow / down-arrow glyphs in the Demographic rows encoding greater-than and less-than age bounds

**Confirmed (verified) findings:**

### [critical] missing-section · p105 — Five of the eleven spinal immobilisation criteria are missing, so a responder using the app could wrongly decide a patient does not need a collar.
- **CPG:** The criteria list under 'IF ANY spinal immobilization criteria met  Apply cervical collar' contains, in order: 'Major trauma following blunt trauma to the head or trunk'; 'ANY new motor deficits / weakness when asked to…'; 'ANY new sensory deficits / reduced sensation when applying light touch to the following'; 'Midline pain / tenderness on palpation'; 'Inability to rotate neck 45° left and right'; 'Altered conscious state'; 'Intoxication'; 'Significant distracting injury'; 'History of vertebral disease / abnormalities'; 'Age ↑ 65 years old'.
- **App:** content.immobilisationCriteria has only 5 entries: major trauma, any new motor deficits/weakness, any new sensory deficits, history of vertebral disease/abnormalities, Age ≥65 years. Missing: midline pain / tenderness on palpation, inability to rotate neck 45° left and right, altered conscious state, intoxication, significant distracting injury.
- **Fix:** Add the five missing criteria to content.immobilisationCriteria in CPG order: 'Midline pain / tenderness on palpation', 'Inability to rotate neck 45° left and right', 'Altered conscious state', 'Intoxication', 'Significant distracting injury'.

### [critical] missing-section · p106 — The spinal immobilisation action step and its absolute prohibition on independently restraining the head are both absent.
- **CPG:** '✚ IF ANY spinal immobilization criteria met  Spinal immobilisation' followed by a blue ✗ line: 'NEVER independently restrain the patients head to the stretcher'.
- **App:** content.management contains only collar, self-extrication, extricate on board/AV, pain relief. No spinal immobilisation step and no restraint prohibition.
- **Fix:** Add to content.management: 'Spinal immobilisation if ANY spinal immobilisation criteria met', and add a red/danger-keyed section (e.g. content.immediateAction or a dedicated warnings list) containing 'NEVER independently restrain the patient's head to the stretcher'.

### [high] missing-section · p105 — The time critical escalation step and the SitRep/ambulance call are missing entirely.
- **CPG:** First rows of MANAGEMENT – SPINAL INJURY: 'IF Meeting time critical criteria  Treat as time critical' and, indented beneath it with a phone icon, 'SitRep: Hatzolah dispatch + call ambulance'.
- **App:** No time critical step and no SitRep/ambulance step anywhere in the protocol.
- **Fix:** Prepend to content.management: 'Treat as time critical if meeting time critical criteria' and 'SitRep: Hatzolah dispatch + call ambulance'.

### [high] missing-section · p105 — The motor and sensory deficit criteria are stated without the CPG's test methods, so the responder is told what to look for but not how to test for it.
- **CPG:** Under 'ANY new motor deficits / weakness when asked to…': 'Arms: Grasp / pull / push' and 'Legs: Push / plantar flex, pull / dorsiflex, leg raise'. Under 'ANY new sensory deficits / reduced sensation when applying light touch to the following': 'Arms: Light touch across the palm and back of hand', 'Legs: Light touch to lateral side of calcaneus', 'The patient should be questioned regarding numbness, tingling, burning or any other altered sensation, anywhere in the body'.
- **App:** Bare bullets 'Any new motor deficits/weakness' and 'Any new sensory deficits' with no sub-detail at all.
- **Fix:** Expand these two criteria into nested objects (e.g. motorDeficits: {arms:'Grasp / pull / push', legs:'Push / plantar flex, pull / dorsiflex, leg raise'} and sensoryDeficits: {arms:'Light touch across the palm and back of hand', legs:'Light touch to lateral side of calcaneus', questioning:'Question re numbness, tingling, burning or any other altered sensation, anywhere in the body'}) so the app renders them as nested subsections.

### [high] missing-section · p105 — The neck-rotation criterion and its safety prohibition against turning the patient's head for them are absent.
- **CPG:** 'Inability to rotate neck 45° left and right', with a blue ✗ 'Do NOT turn the patient's head for them' and 'Ask the patient to turn their head slowly left and right, Approximately 45 degrees each direction, Advised them to stop if they feel ANY pain OR resistance'.
- **App:** Nothing about neck rotation, and no prohibition on turning the patient's head.
- **Fix:** Add the criterion 'Inability to rotate neck 45° left and right' with sub-detail: 'Do NOT turn the patient's head for them'; 'Ask the patient to turn their head slowly left and right, approximately 45 degrees each direction'; 'Advise them to stop if they feel ANY pain OR resistance'. Place the 'Do NOT' line in a red-keyed section.

**Medium/low (unverified):**
- [medium] missing-section p105 — The CPG's NOTE on bilateral simultaneous testing is missing.  
  _fix:_ Add content.notes: ['The left and right sides should be tested simultaneously to compare strength between each side of the body'].
- [medium] missing-section p106 — The Virtual ED disposition option for age-only concern is missing.  
  _fix:_ Add sub-detail to the age criterion: 'Consider Virtual ED if age is the ONLY concerning feature post injury (e.g. fall)'.
- [medium] wrong-value p106 — The age criterion comparison operator is wrong: the app says ≥65 where the CPG's arrow glyph means greater than 65.  
  _fix:_ Change to 'Age >65 years old' (or keep the CPG's own '↑ 65 years old' rendering used elsewhere in the app) so a 65-year-old is not incorrectly captured by the criterion.
- [medium] colour-coding p105 — Both medication rows are purple-highlighted medication bars in the CPG; the app renders pain relief as an ordinary navy management bullet and omits the second bar entirely.  
  _fix:_ Move both medication triggers into a dedicated medication/dosing structure (a 'dosing' key renders dosing cards) so they are visually distinguished from the procedural management steps, matching the CPG's purple medication bars.
- [medium] render-risk p106 — The self-extrication decision is flattened into one long run-on bullet, losing the CPG's explicit ALL-of-four structure and the 'OR changes' qualifier.  
  _fix:_ Replace with a nested structure: selfExtricationCriteria: ['Conscious AND co-operative','NOT intoxicated','NOT prevented by injury','NO neurological deficit OR changes'] introduced by 'Consider self-extrication if patient meets ALL of the following', with 'ELSE: extricate on board/AV' as the fallback.

## `clinical-approach-cb-p119` — p119-120 · coverage: partial

**Graphics not represented in the app:**
- p119: Decision-branch flowchart: the red 'Rapid assessment — Does the patient appear WELL or UNWELL?' row splits via two black arrows into a RED box 'UNWELL / PRIMARY SURVEY' (left) and a GREEN box 'WELL / PROCEED TO ASSESSMENT' (right). This is the central algorithm of the page.
- p119: Vertical black connector spine linking RESPONDER ACTION → ASSESS → PAUSE & PLAN, showing these are sequential stages of one algorithm rather than an unordered list.
- p119: Two-column ASSESS panel: left column 'Ask About' with the SAMPLE mnemonic encoded by bold+underlined initial letters (Situation or Symptoms, Allergies, Medications, Past medical history, Last well (onset time), Extra information); right column 'Assessment Tools' (Perfusion, Respiratory, AVPU, Trauma 2° survey (Head to toe)).
- p119: Bracket connector below PAUSE & PLAN splitting into two parallel end-boxes side by side: TREATMENT (left) and ? ADVICE (right).
- p119: Colour-coded block headers and glyph icons throughout: red STOP / red UNWELL, green WELL, navy RESPONDER ACTION / ASSESS / PAUSE & PLAN / TREATMENT / ADVICE, plus red 'D' badge, black 'IF' badges, magnifier, clipboard, warning triangle, red telephone, ambulance and '+' icons that mark row type.
- p120: Red STOP block containing the Level 1 escalation mandate and the red-telephone 'IF Further clinical advice required → Call clinical operations manager' row.

**Confirmed (verified) findings:**

### [critical] missing-section · p119 — The entire RESPONDER ACTION block is absent from the app, including the deterioration safety-net.
- **CPG:** RESPONDER ACTION — '⚠ IF Patient deteriorates at ANY stage | Return to Primary Survey' and '+ Rapport, reassurance, position'.
- **App:** No step, key or note corresponds to RESPONDER ACTION. content.steps goes straight from 'WELL → Assess' to 'ASSESS — Ask About'. Neither 'deteriorates', 'Return to Primary Survey', nor 'Rapport, reassurance, position' appears anywhere in clinical-approach-cb.json.
- **Fix:** Add a 'responderAction' key (mirroring clinical-approach-fr.json, which already has one) between the WELL/UNWELL branch and ASSESS: ["⚠️ If the patient deteriorates at ANY stage → return to Primary Survey", "Rapport, reassurance, position"].

### [high] missing-graphic · p119 — The WELL/UNWELL decision branch is flattened into two sequential list steps, losing the fork and its red/green colour semantics.
- **CPG:** 'Rapid assessment | Does the patient appear WELL or UNWELL?' with two arrows forking to a RED 'UNWELL → PRIMARY SURVEY' box and a GREEN 'WELL → PROCEED TO ASSESSMENT' box.
- **App:** Two adjacent rows of a flat steps table: {label:'UNWELL → Primary Survey'} then {label:'WELL → Assess'}, rendered as consecutive navy list items, so both paths read as things to do one after the other rather than as mutually exclusive branches.
- **Fix:** Restructure to a branch object as the FR sibling already does: rapidAssessment: { question: 'Does the patient appear WELL or UNWELL?', branches: [{condition:'UNWELL', goTo:'primarySurvey', label:'Primary Survey'}, {condition:'WELL', goTo:'assess', label:'Proceed to Assessment'}] } so the renderer emits tappable decision chips, and consider a '_flowchart' key as used in clinical-approach-fr.json.

### [high] missing-section · p120 — The p120 escalation-advice row 'Further clinical advice required → Call clinical operations manager' is missing.
- **CPG:** '📞 IF Further clinical advice required | Call clinical operations manager' (inside the red STOP block on p120).
- **App:** content.criticalNote carries only the first sentence of the p120 STOP block. The specific escalation route — the clinical operations manager — is nowhere in the file. The app's nearest text is the generic 'If unsure, seek immediate clinical advice' taken from the p119 ADVICE box.
- **Fix:** Add the second row of the p120 STOP block, e.g. escalation: ["All Level 1 incidents must be escalated with Ambulance or a Level 3 FR and higher for further investigation and management.", "If further clinical advice is required → call the clinical operations manager"] (clinical-approach-fr.json already carries the clinical-operations-manager line in pauseAndPlan).

### [high] missing-section · p119 — 'Handover / IMISTAMBO' is missing from the TREATMENT block.
- **CPG:** TREATMENT — '+ Manage as per appropriate CPG' and '🚑 Handover / IMISTAMBO'.
- **App:** The TREATMENT step detail reads only 'Manage as per appropriate CPG. If unsure, seek immediate clinical advice.' The handover mnemonic IMISTAMBO does not appear.
- **Fix:** Add 'Handover / IMISTAMBO' as a second treatment item, e.g. treatment: ["Manage as per appropriate CPG", "Handover / IMISTAMBO"].

### [high] colour-coding · p119 — Every block is packed into one generic 'steps' key, so all content renders navy and none of the CPG's red/green colour coding survives.
- **CPG:** The page is colour-coded: red STOP header with red-tinted 'Dangers & Safety' and 'Rapid assessment' rows; red 'UNWELL / PRIMARY SURVEY' box; green 'WELL / PROCEED TO ASSESSMENT' box; red-tinted 'Call 000 for ALL patients…' row inside PAUSE & PLAN; red STOP block on p120.
- **App:** content = { steps: [...], criticalNote: '...' }. Neither 'steps' nor 'criticalNote' is in the renderer's red list (dangersAndSafety, rapidAssessment, primarySurvey, redFlags, immediateAction) or green list (definition, assessmentTriangle), so the whole protocol — including the STOP/escalation content — renders navy with no urgency signal.
- **Fix:** Split content into renderer-recognised keys so colour follows the CPG: dangersAndSafety (red) for the STOP rows, rapidAssessment (red) for the WELL/UNWELL fork, primarySurvey (red) for the UNWELL branch, and move the p120 escalation from 'criticalNote' into 'immediateAction' (or add a red-coded key) so it is not rendered as ordinary navy prose.

**Medium/low (unverified):**
- [medium] missing-graphic p119 — The SAMPLE mnemonic and the two-column ASSESS layout are lost; the Ask About items are collapsed into one long comma-separated sentence.  
  _fix:_ Use a table so the renderer emits columns and the mnemonic is visible, as clinical-approach-fr.json does: assess: { headers: ['Ask About (SAMPLE)', 'Assessment Tools'], rows: [['Situation or Symptoms','Perfusion'],['Allergies','Respiratory'],['Medications','AVPU'],['Past medical history','Trauma 2° survey (Head to toe)'],['Last well (onset time)',''],['Extra information','']] }.
- [medium] wrong-value p119 — The Call 000 criterion is truncated — 'requiring hospital care' is dropped and the emphasis on ALL is lost.  
  _fix:_ Restore the full CPG sentence verbatim as its own item: 'Call 000 for ALL patients outside of Hatzolah's clinical scope, requiring hospital care', and place it in a red-coded key so it inherits the CPG's red row highlight.
- [medium] render-risk p119 — 'steps' is an array of uniform {label, detail} objects, so it renders as a two-column table whose long detail cells will be badly squeezed on a 375px phone.  
  _fix:_ Drop the generic 'steps' shape and use named sections (dangersAndSafety, rapidAssessment, responderAction, assess, pauseAndPlan, treatment, advice) as clinical-approach-fr.json does — string arrays render as bullet lists that wrap full-width, and the section titles reproduce the CPG's own block headers.
- [low] missing-graphic p119 — TREATMENT and ADVICE are two parallel end-boxes in the CPG but are merged into a single app step.  
  _fix:_ Separate into two keys, treatment and advice, so the two parallel outcomes are distinguishable.
- [low] not-in-cpg p119 — Three step details contain paraphrases/additions that do not appear on p119.  
  _fix:_ Use the CPG's own wording: 'Dangers & Safety', 'Primary Survey', 'Proceed to Assessment'. If a scene-safety expansion is wanted, take it from the FR page (p10) rather than inventing it here.

## `cardiac-arrest-cb-p134` — p134-135 · coverage: partial

**Graphics not represented in the app:**
- p134: Green DEFINITION block with '?' icon and green asterisk bullets containing the two-sentence definition of cardiac arrest.
- p134: Flowchart: heavy black connector drops from the PRIMARY SURVEY block into the lavender 'IF Possible | Gain 360° access to patient' bar, then forks left/right into two parallel boxes and re-merges into the DEFIBRILLATOR + CPR box below.
- p134: Two side-by-side navy role boxes: RESPONDER 1 ('Apply: AED Defibrillator') and RESPONDER 2 with black 'IF' chip 'MULTIPLE CREW' ('CPR compressions | 100-120 per minute') — a crew-role division of labour.
- p134: Navy DEFIBRILLATOR + CPR algorithm box: 'Follow instructions from AED / defibrillator  (If under 8 select child)', black 'IF' chip 'Shock advised', black ✗ glyph warning 'Ensure NO ONE touching patient prior / during shock (includes stopping compressions)', then '+ Press: Shock'.
- p135: Continuation of the DEFIBRILLATOR + CPR box: black 'IF' and '&' condition chips forming the ratio decision grid (Adult (>15) → CPR 30 : 2; Child & Single operator → CPR 30 : 2; Child & Two operators → CPR 15 : 2), plus the loop-back instruction 'Repeat EVERY 2 minutes — From top of this box (I.E. DEFIBRILLATOR + CPR)' with two indented IF branches.
- p135: Green FURTHER NOTES block whose FIRST row is a full-width RED warning bar (white bold text) about not certifying death, followed by five green asterisk notes.

**Confirmed (verified) findings:**

### [critical] missing-section · p135 — The entire 2-minute CPR/defibrillation cycle instruction is absent from the app — nothing tells the responder to repeat the AED analysis and CPR loop.
- **CPG:** '+ Repeat EVERY 2 minutes | From top of this box (I.E. DEFIBRILLATOR + CPR)'
- **App:** content.aed is only 'Apply AED. Follow AED instructions. Ensure no one touching patient during shock.' — no repeat interval, no loop-back, no mention of re-analysis.
- **Fix:** Add the cycle to the AED/defibrillator section, e.g. a step 'Repeat EVERY 2 minutes from the top of DEFIBRILLATOR + CPR (re-analyse, shock if advised, resume CPR)'.

### [high] missing-section · p134 — The paediatric AED setting instruction is missing — the app never tells the responder to select the child mode/pads for patients under 8.
- **CPG:** '+ Follow instructions from AED / defibrillator   (If under 8 select child)'
- **App:** 'Apply AED. Follow AED instructions. Ensure no one touching patient during shock.' — no under-8 / child selection.
- **Fix:** Append '(if under 8 years, select child setting)' to the AED instruction step.

### [high] wrong-value · p135 — The adult age threshold operator is wrong: the CPG uses strictly greater-than 15, the app uses greater-than-or-equal 15, which reclassifies a 15-year-old from child to adult and changes the two-operator ratio from 15:2 to 30:2.
- **CPG:** '[IF] Adult (>15)   CPR 30 : 2'
- **App:** cprRatios[0].condition = 'Adult (≥15 years)'
- **Fix:** Change to 'Adult (>15)' exactly as printed, so a 15-year-old falls under the Child rows.

### [high] missing-section · p135 — Five of the six FURTHER NOTES are missing, including hand position, compression depth, minimising interruptions, compression-only CPR when ventilation is not possible, and how to judge effective ventilation.
- **CPG:** 'Appropriate defibrillation and continuous CPR save lives. Keep all interruptions to compressions to an absolute minimum.' / 'Hands should be positioned in the middle of the chest (lower half of the sternum)' / 'CPR depth should be 1/3 depth of the chest' / 'If single responder and unable to effectively complete ventilations, perform compressions only until further resources arrive' / 'Effective ventilation is guided by rise and fall of the chest with a minimum pause in CPR'
- **App:** Only content.note (the red 'do not certify death' warning). No CPR technique notes at all.
- **Fix:** Add a 'furtherNotes' array carrying all five green asterisk notes verbatim, keeping the red death-certification warning separate and marked urgent.

### [high] missing-section · p135 — The 'no shock advised' branch is missing — the app never tells the responder to check for signs of life / normal breathing, nor the <5 second time limit for that check.
- **CPG:** '[IF] No shock advised | Check for signs of life/normal breathing (<5 seconds)'
- **App:** Nothing — the app only covers the 'shock advised' path ('Ensure no one touching patient during shock').
- **Fix:** Add a branch step: 'IF no shock advised → check for signs of life / normal breathing (<5 seconds)'.

**Medium/low (unverified):**
- [medium] missing-section p135 — The compressor-rotation instruction is missing — nothing tells crews to swap the CPR responder every 2 minutes.  
  _fix:_ Add 'IF multiple officers: alternate the CPR responder every 2 minutes' alongside the 2-minute cycle step.
- [medium] missing-section p134 — The '360° access' step that sits between the primary survey and the resuscitation phase is absent.  
  _fix:_ Add a step 'IF possible — gain 360° access to the patient' after the primary survey.
- [medium] missing-section p134 — The green DEFINITION block is not carried into the app at all.  
  _fix:_ Add content.definition with both sentences verbatim (note the CPG's own missing apostrophe in 'hearts' — reproduce as printed).
- [medium] missing-section p134 — The shock-delivery safety wording is truncated: the app drops 'prior /' , the explicit '(includes stopping compressions)' clarification, and the 'Press: Shock' action step.  
  _fix:_ Restore the full wording 'Ensure NO ONE touching patient prior / during shock (includes stopping compressions)' and add the 'Press: Shock' step.
- [medium] table-misrendered p134 — The Primary Survey table's Assess and Manage columns are collapsed into one 'action' string, and the 'Scene safety?' assessment prompt is lost entirely.  
  _fix:_ Restructure primarySurvey to {step, element, assess, manage} so the app renders the CPG's Assess/Manage split, and restore 'Scene safety?' for D.
- [medium] colour-coding p135 — The RETURN OF SIGNS OF LIFE section is a red (critical) block in the CPG but the app's 'rosc' key falls through to the default navy styling.  
  _fix:_ Rename the key to one the renderer colours red (or add 'rosc'/'returnOfSignsOfLife' to the red map).
- [medium] colour-coding p135 — The death-certification warning is a full-width RED bar in the CPG but renders as an ordinary navy note in the app, losing its critical emphasis.  
  _fix:_ Move it to a red-mapped key (e.g. immediateAction) or add an explicit urgent flag so it renders red.
- [low] render-risk p135 — The 'rosc' key humanises to the section title 'Rosc', which is not the CPG's heading and is meaningless to a Level 1 responder.  
  _fix:_ Rename the key to 'returnOfSignsOfLife' so it humanises to 'Return Of Signs Of Life'.
- [low] render-risk p134 — The 'aed' key humanises to the section title 'Aed' rather than the acronym AED or the CPG's 'DEFIBRILLATOR + CPR'.  
  _fix:_ Rename to 'defibrillatorAndCpr' (renders 'Defibrillator And Cpr') or add a title override so the acronym is not lower-cased.
- [low] wrong-value p135 — The post-ROSC monitoring instruction drops 'effective breathing' — the app only says monitor for re-arrest.  
  _fix:_ Restore to 'Continuously monitor for re-arrest / effective breathing'.
- [low] colour-coding p134 — RECOGNITION is a green (definition/notes) block in the CPG but renders navy in the app.  
  _fix:_ Add 'recognition' to the green-mapped section keys.

## `alcohol-intoxication-p39` — p39-40 · coverage: partial

**Graphics not represented in the app:**
- p39: Green DEFINITION header bar with '?' icon glyph, over a pale-green body block with a green asterisk bullet.
- p40: Green FURTHER NOTES header bar with list icon over a pale-green two-row body block.

**Confirmed (verified) findings:**

### [critical] missing-section · p40 — The entire FURTHER NOTES block is absent from the app — both the 16-years-and-over age restriction and the mandatory escalation of non-low-risk patients to Ambulance Victoria are missing.
- **CPG:** FURTHER NOTES — "This CPG is for 16 years and over"; "Patients who do not fit the low-risk criteria (i.e. moderate and high Risk) will be escalated to Ambulance Victoria"
- **App:** content has only `recognition` and `management`. No notes, no escalation, no age restriction anywhere in the entry.
- **Fix:** Add `notes: ['This CPG is for 16 years and over', 'Patients who do not fit the low-risk criteria (i.e. moderate and high Risk) will be escalated to Ambulance Victoria']` and set `escalation: 'Patients who do not fit the low-risk criteria (i.e. moderate and high Risk) will be escalated to Ambulance Victoria'`.

### [high] wrong-value · p39 — The low-risk criteria list is truncated: two of the CPG's six criteria are dropped, and the remaining four are compressed into one prose string rather than a sub-list.
- **CPG:** "Patient is considered low risk (as per following criteria)" with six sub-bullets: "Has capacity to make their own decisions", "Able to ambulate", "Competent sober adult able to care for patient in a safe place", "NO red flag criteria met", "Patient is normothermic", "Exclusion of other differentials (AEIOUTIPS)"
- **App:** "Patient is low risk: has capacity, able to ambulate, competent sober adult carer available, no red flags" — a single flattened string. "Patient is normothermic" and "Exclusion of other differentials (AEIOUTIPS)" do not appear at all.
- **Fix:** Replace the flattened string with a nested structure, e.g. `lowRiskCriteria: ['Has capacity to make their own decisions', 'Able to ambulate', 'Competent sober adult able to care for patient in a safe place', 'NO red flag criteria met', 'Patient is normothermic', 'Exclusion of other differentials (AEIOUTIPS)']`, keeping the lead-in bullet "Patient is considered low risk (as per following criteria)" in recognition.

### [high] wrong-value · p40 — The call-for-ambulance criteria are paraphrased and shortened — the specific vomiting/airway wording is replaced with "airway compromise" and the 2-to-4-hour no-improvement trigger is dropped.
- **CPG:** "Call for ambulance if conscious state deteriorates, patient vomits when laying supine and does not clear their own airway, severe pain occurs, there is no improvement in conscious state over the next 2 to 4 hours"
- **App:** "Call ambulance if conscious state deteriorates, airway compromise, severe pain"
- **Fix:** Use the CPG wording verbatim: 'Call for ambulance if conscious state deteriorates, patient vomits when laying supine and does not clear their own airway, severe pain occurs, there is no improvement in conscious state over the next 2 to 4 hours'.

### [high] wrong-value · p40 — The Safety Netting sub-list is collapsed into one sentence, losing three of the CPG's seven advice items and part of a fourth.
- **CPG:** "Safety Netting" with seven sub-bullets: "Advise not to drink any more alcohol until recovered from symptoms", "Sleep on side, keep warm and remain in a low stimulus environment", "Avoid mobilising", "Maintain hydration and nutrition", "Take paracetamol for mild pain as required", "Have sober adult stay with the patient", "Call for ambulance if..."
- **App:** "Safety netting: advise no more alcohol, sleep on side, keep warm, maintain hydration" — omits "remain in a low stimulus environment", "Avoid mobilising", "and nutrition", "Take paracetamol for mild pain as required" and "Have sober adult stay with the patient".
- **Fix:** Make Safety Netting a nested list under management with all seven CPG bullets verbatim, e.g. `safetyNetting: ['Advise not to drink any more alcohol until recovered from symptoms', 'Sleep on side, keep warm and remain in a low stimulus environment', 'Avoid mobilising', 'Maintain hydration and nutrition', 'Take paracetamol for mild pain as required', 'Have sober adult stay with the patient', 'Call for ambulance if ...']`.

**Medium/low (unverified):**
- [medium] missing-section p39 — The green DEFINITION block on p39 is not represented in the app at all.  
  _fix:_ Add `definition: 'A disturbance in behaviour or mental function during or after alcohol consumption. Alcohol is a depressant that affects the CNS. Intoxication may lead to inhibition of behaviours, slurred speech, reduction in conscious state, HR and RR.'` — the key name `definition` also gives it the green colour the CPG uses.
- [low] colour-coding p39 — The CPG colours RECOGNITION green (definition/notes colour) and MANAGEMENT navy; the app's renderer gives `recognition` navy, so the p39 green / p40 navy contrast is lost.  
  _fix:_ Either add the missing `definition` and `notes` keys (which do render green) so the protocol still reads green-then-navy, or extend the renderer's green key list to include `recognition`. No red/urgent block exists on these pages, so no urgency colour is lost.
- [low] typo p39 — First recognition bullet drops the CPG's parenthetical clarifier.  
  _fix:_ Restore to 'Recent ingestion of ethanol (alcohol)'.
- [low] render-risk p40 — The two conditional management rows are a two-column construct in the CPG (black "IF" badge + condition | action); the app flattens them to single strings that wrap mid-clause on a 375px phone.  
  _fix:_ Acceptable as bullets, but match the CPG's capitalisation and spacing ('IF necessary → Manage as per Altered Conscious State (Acute)', '... Manage as per Nausea / Vomiting'); consider a two-column conditional table so the IF-condition and the action stay visually paired on a narrow screen.

## `cardiac-chest-pain-p72` — p72-74 · coverage: partial

**Graphics not represented in the app:**
- p72: RECOGNITION block — green section header bar with speech-bubble glyph over a pale-green body panel; three icon-led rows (green ✱ = key statement, green magnifier = 'suspicion' list, green magnifier = 'risk factors' list), each with an indented triangle-bullet sub-list. Entire coloured block is absent from the app.
- p73: MANAGEMENT coloured algorithm table — navy header bar with clipboard glyph; left column encodes meaning by icon (ambulance = SitRep/dispatch, blue '+' = procedure, pill = medication, clock = repeat, ⊘ = maximum, '?' = reassess, ✖ = stop/exclude) plus inline inverse-video logic badges (IF, &, OR); right column carries purple/lavender medication bands (Oxygen PRN, Aspirin Oral, GTN Sublingual/Buccal, Methoxyflurane Whistle). The app flattens this to a plain bullet list, dropping the icon vocabulary, both ✖ stop rows and the AND/OR logic badges.
- p74: Aspirin drug dosing table — grey header ('Aspirin' | IF Cardiac chest pain / Discomfort) over 5 icon-led rows (people glyph = Demographic, triangle bullets = Route/Initial Dose/Repeat Dose(s), ⊘ = Maximum). Not present in the app in any form.
- p74: Glyceryl Trinitrate (GTN) drug dosing table — grey header ('Glyceryl Trinitrate' | 'GTN' | IF Cardiac Chest Pain / Discomfort) over the same 5 icon-led rows. Not present in the app in any form.
- p74: Methoxyflurane drug dosing table — grey header ('Methoxyflurane' | IF Pain score ↑ 3) over the same 5 icon-led rows, with a second value column on the Repeat Dose(s) row. Not present in the app in any form.

**Confirmed (verified) findings:**

### [critical] missing-section · p72 — The entire RECOGNITION section (payoff principle, 6 cardiac-suspicion characteristics, 6 risk factors) is absent from the app.
- **CPG:** RECOGNITION — "Cardiac chest pain can be notoriously difficult to differentiate from non-cardiac chest pain - when in doubt, the “payoff principle” advised is to treat as if it were cardiac". "Chest pain associated with ANY of the following characteristics or symptoms should markedly increase suspicion that it may be of a cardiac nature": History of experiencing similar pain during previous cardiac events; Pain that radiates to the shoulder, arm, jaw, neck, upper abdomen OR back; Pain that is worse on exertion (E.G. walking, climbing stairs); Pain that is constant at rest (E.G. not significantly worse when coughing); Pain associated with active vomiting; Pain associated with sweating. "The following risk factors also increase the likelihood of chest pain having a cardiac origin": Age ↑ 40; Obesity / poor diet / poor exercise; Smoking; History of hypertension / high cholesterol; History of diabetes; Family / genetic history of cardiac events.
- **App:** content has only definition, management and gtnContraindications. There is no recognition key at all — no payoff principle, no characteristics list, no risk-factor list.
- **Fix:** Add content.recognition as a nested object: { keyPoint: '…payoff principle…', suspicionFeatures: [6 strings], riskFactors: [6 strings] } so the whole green RECOGNITION block is restored verbatim, including 'Age ↑ 40' as 'Age >40'.

### [critical] missing-section · p73 — The Aspirin exclusion row '✖ Associated back pain' is omitted, so the app tells the responder to give Aspirin with no stated exclusion.
- **CPG:** Under the purple 'Aspirin, Oral' band: "▸ 300 mg" then a ✖ (stop/exclude) row: "✖ Associated back pain".
- **App:** "💊 If suspected cardiac chest pain → Aspirin 300 mg oral" — no exclusion of any kind.
- **Fix:** Add the ✖ row to the Aspirin management step, e.g. an 'exclusions' entry reading 'Do NOT give Aspirin if there is associated back pain', and render ✖ rows in red per the CPG's stop-marker convention.

### [critical] missing-section · p73 — The GTN stop/rescue row is omitted — the app never tells the responder to remove the tablet and rinse the mouth if sBP drops below 100 or side effects occur.
- **CPG:** Under the GTN band: "✖ IF sBP ↓ 100  OR  Side effects → Remove tablet + rinse mouth".
- **App:** "Reassess BP and side effects after each dose" — the app states the reassessment but gives no action when the reassessment is abnormal.
- **Fix:** Add a management/exclusion entry: 'If sBP <100 OR side effects → remove tablet + rinse mouth', marked as a stop (red) action.

### [high] missing-section · p73 — Methoxyflurane has no dose, repeat interval or maximum in the app's management list.
- **CPG:** "💊 IF Pain remains ↑ 3 → Methoxyflurane, Whistle"; "▸ 3 mL   1 vial"; "🕐 IF Required — Repeat: @ 25 mins (continuous use)"; "⊘ IF No side effects — Maximum: 6 mL (2 vials) in 24 hour period".
- **App:** "💊 If pain ≥3 → Methoxyflurane inhaled" — no volume, no vial count, no repeat interval, no maximum.
- **Fix:** Extend the Methoxyflurane management step to '3 mL (1 vial) inhaled via whistle; repeat @ 25 mins if required (continuous use); maximum 6 mL (2 vials) in 24 hours'.

**Medium/low (unverified):**
- [medium] wrong-value p73 — The CPG's ↑ (greater-than) arrow glyphs are rendered as ≥ in the app, and inconsistently with the app's own handling of ↓ as <.  
  _fix:_ Apply the CPG arrow convention consistently: ↑ → '>' and ↓ → '<'. Change to 'pain >2', 'sBP >100', 'pain remains >3', keeping 'SpO2 <92%' as is.
