- [medium] not-in-cpg p73 — content.gtnContraindications (6 items) does not appear anywhere on pages 72–74 of the CPG.  
  _fix:_ Either verify this list verbatim against the GTN pharmacology monograph (p145–146) and label it as sourced from there, or remove it from this protocol and rely on the GTN medication entry. Do not present unsourced contraindications as part of the p72–74 protocol.
- [medium] wrong-value p72 — The definition is paraphrased and truncated — the CPG's closing time-critical/life-threatening statement is dropped.  
  _fix:_ Replace content.definition with the CPG's full four-sentence text verbatim.
- [medium] colour-coding p72 — Nothing in this protocol renders red despite the CPG's red title banner and its explicit time-critical/life-threatening framing.  
  _fix:_ Add an immediateAction (red) key carrying 'Treat as TIME CRITICAL — SitRep: Hatzolah dispatch + call ambulance' and move the ✖ stop rules (Aspirin back pain; GTN sBP<100/side effects) into a red-rendered key.
- [low] wrong-value p73 — Drug routes are narrowed relative to the CPG's MANAGEMENT bands.  
  _fix:_ Use 'GTN 300 mcg sublingual / buccal' and 'Methoxyflurane, inhaled via whistle'.
- [low] colour-coding p72 — A restored recognition key would render navy, but the CPG colours RECOGNITION green like DEFINITION.  
  _fix:_ Add 'recognition' to the green section-colour list in the renderer so the restored block matches the CPG's green coding.
- [low] render-risk p74 — A dosing array built directly from the p74 tables will degrade to stacked record cards on a 375px phone because two columns hold long text.  
  _fix:_ Keep the long Methoxyflurane repeat note in a single separate 'note' field (one long-text column only) so the dosing objects still render as dosing cards rather than degrading.

## `stroke-p99` — p99-100 · coverage: partial

**Graphics not represented in the app:**
- p100: Red STOP banner block with a red ✚ icon and a black 'IF' badge — 'IF Unconscious | Manage per unconscious patient guideline'. A standalone red gate sitting ABOVE the Management block.
- p100: FURTHER NOTES green block containing two two-column bulleted lists: 'symptoms that should prompt a stroke assessment' (5 items) and 'possible stroke mimics' (6 items), plus two free-text clinical rules.

**Confirmed (verified) findings:**

### [critical] missing-section · p100 — The wake-up stroke time-of-onset rule from FURTHER NOTES is absent from the app.
- **CPG:** "In cases where a patient wakes up with stroke symptoms the time of onset is taken from when the patient was last seen well and NOT from the time of awakening"
- **App:** Nothing. The app has no notes/further-notes key at all; content is definition, fast, recognition, history, management only.
- **Fix:** Add a notes array to content with this rule verbatim, e.g. 'In cases where a patient wakes up with stroke symptoms the time of onset is taken from when the patient was last seen well and NOT from the time of awakening'.

### [high] missing-section · p100 — Whole FURTHER NOTES section (p100) missing — including the list of symptoms that should trigger a stroke assessment.
- **CPG:** "The following symptoms may be caused by strokes and should therefore prompt you to perform a stroke assessment" — Headache (severe / sudden onset); Vertigo (I.E. dizziness, nausea, vomiting); Hypertension (severe / sudden onset); Impaired gait (sudden onset); Altered conscious state
- **App:** No equivalent. The app's only recognition trigger is the F/A/S signs, so a patient with sudden severe headache or sudden impaired gait would not be assessed for stroke.
- **Fix:** Add a section (e.g. content.promptsStrokeAssessment) listing all five items exactly as printed.

### [high] colour-coding · p100 — The red STOP gate 'IF Unconscious → manage per unconscious patient guideline' is rendered as an ordinary navy management bullet.
- **CPG:** Red-headed block titled "STOP" above MANAGEMENT: "✚ IF Unconscious | Manage per unconscious patient guideline"
- **App:** management[0] = 'If unconscious → manage as unconscious patient guideline' — first bullet of the navy Management list, no red styling, no STOP framing.
- **Fix:** Move it out of management into its own key rendered red (e.g. immediateAction: ['IF Unconscious — manage per unconscious patient guideline']), since SECTION_COLORS already maps immediateAction to CPG red.

### [high] missing-section · p100 — FURTHER NOTES rule that all potential stroke patients are transported even if symptoms resolve is missing.
- **CPG:** "Stroke symptoms can sometimes fluctuate / be intermittent – for this reason all potential stroke patients should be transported to hospital regardless of symptom improvement OR resolution"
- **App:** Only the partial management bullet '🚑 SitRep: Hatzolah dispatch + call ambulance regardless of severity' — 'severity' only; no mention of fluctuation, improvement or full resolution.
- **Fix:** Add the note verbatim, and correct the management bullet to the CPG's 'REGARDLESS of severity / improvement'.

**Medium/low (unverified):**
- [medium] missing-section p100 — The stroke-mimics list from FURTHER NOTES is absent.  
  _fix:_ Add a strokeMimics array with all six entries as printed.
- [medium] missing-section p99 — Each F/A/S row's 'E.G.' descriptor of the abnormal finding was dropped; only the assessment technique survives.  
  _fix:_ Give each fast entry both fields, e.g. { letter:'A', sign:'One sided limb weakness OR arm drift', assess:'Ask patient to hold their arms straight for 10s & squeeze your fingers' } — three short-ish columns still render as a table.
- [medium] missing-section p99 — Stroke-history questions are paraphrased and lose their parenthetical qualifiers, including the onset-time precision instruction.  
  _fix:_ Restore the five history questions verbatim from p99.
- [medium] wrong-value p100 — The MANAGEMENT 'REGARDLESS' row is truncated: '/ improvement' is dropped and the CPG's two separate SitRep rows are merged into one.  
  _fix:_ Emit both rows; the second as 'REGARDLESS of severity / improvement — SitRep: Hatzolah dispatch + call ambulance'.
- [low] missing-section p99 — The definition is compressed and loses the mechanism and the 'potentially irreversible' wording.  
  _fix:_ Use the CPG sentence verbatim — it fits the green definition block and conveys the time-critical rationale.
- [low] colour-coding p99 — RECOGNITION, STROKE HISTORY and FURTHER NOTES are green-headed blocks in the CPG but render navy in the app.  
  _fix:_ Either add recognition/history/notes to the green group for this protocol family, or nest them so they inherit green — but only after checking other protocols, since 'recognition' is navy-correct on some CPG pages.
- [low] not-in-cpg p99 — The app labels the section 'FAST' and calls them 'FAST symptoms'; the acronym FAST never appears on p99–100.  
  _fix:_ Rename the section to 'Recognition' (matching the CPG heading) and put the lead-in sentence above the F/A/S table rather than after it — currently key order renders 'Fast' before 'Recognition'.
- [low] typo p100 — The CPG itself misspells 're-evaluate' as 'revaluate'; the app silently corrects it.  
  _fix:_ No clinical change needed — recorded so the deviation from source text is deliberate and traceable. Values verified digit by digit: SpO2 ↓92% and BGL ↓4 both match the app; ↓24 hours and ↑4 mmol/L on p99 match '<24 hours' and '≥4 mmol/L'.

## `altered-consciousness-cb-p125` — p125 · coverage: partial

**Graphics not represented in the app:**
- p125: Colour-coded section block system: two green blocks (DEFINITION with '?' icon, RECOGNITION with speech-bubble icon), a red STOP block (▶ flag icon) running continuously into a red 'ALL ALTERED CONSCIOUS PATIENTS – GENERAL MANAGEMENT' block (clipboard icon), then two navy blocks (HEAD INJURY – ADDITIONAL MANAGEMENT, SEIZURE – ADDITIONAL MANAGEMENT). Row bullets are coloured glyphs: green ✳/magnifier in green blocks, red magnifier and red phone handset in red blocks, navy ✚ in navy blocks.
- p125: Nested/indented branch structure under '[IF] Breathing effectively': first-level action '✚ Recovery position', and a second-level nested condition '[IF] Neck injury possible ✚ Recovery position (Support head in neutral position)' indented inside the same branch row.
- p125: Red bold cross-reference 'Cardiac Arrest' styled as a link/pointer to the separate Cardiac Arrest (L1) guideline.
- p125: Two-column condition→action tabular layout in the HEAD INJURY and SEIZURE navy blocks: left column holds the condition/instruction ('[IF] Life threatening bleeding', 'Protect patient from injury', '✚ [IF] Necessary'), right column the corresponding action ('Control bleeding + dress wound', 'Do NOT forcibly restrict the patient from convulsing', 'Pillow / pad head').

**Confirmed (verified) findings:**

### [critical] wrong-value · p125 — The app changes the trigger for treating as Cardiac Arrest from 'unresponsive AND ineffective breathing' to 'unconscious and no pulse' — a pulse check the CPG does not ask a Level 1 responder to perform.
- **CPG:** Under ALL ALTERED CONSCIOUS PATIENTS – GENERAL MANAGEMENT: "[IF] Patient becomes unresponsive  [&] ineffective breathing  ✚ Manage as Cardiac Arrest". The word 'pulse' does not appear anywhere on p125.
- **App:** content.management[1] = "If unconscious and no pulse → Manage as Cardiac Arrest"
- **Fix:** Replace with "If patient becomes unresponsive AND ineffective breathing → Manage as Cardiac Arrest". Remove the pulse criterion entirely.

### [high] missing-section · p125 — The entire 'SEIZURE – ADDITIONAL MANAGEMENT' block is absent from the app, including the explicit 'Do NOT' safety directive.
- **CPG:** SEIZURE – ADDITIONAL MANAGEMENT: "✚ Protect patient from injury | Do NOT forcibly restrict the patient from convulsing"; "✚ [IF] Necessary | Pillow / pad head".
- **App:** No seizure section, key or wording of any kind in content.
- **Fix:** Add content.seizureAdditionalManagement = ["Protect patient from injury — do NOT forcibly restrict the patient from convulsing", "If necessary → pillow / pad head"].

### [high] missing-section · p125 — The entire 'HEAD INJURY – ADDITIONAL MANAGEMENT' block is absent from the app.
- **CPG:** HEAD INJURY – ADDITIONAL MANAGEMENT: "✚ Maintain neutral spine alignment"; "✚ [IF] Life threatening bleeding | Control bleeding + dress wound".
- **App:** No head-injury section; 'spine alignment' and 'bleeding' appear nowhere in content.
- **Fix:** Add content.headInjuryAdditionalManagement = ["Maintain neutral spine alignment", "If life threatening bleeding → control bleeding + dress wound"].

### [high] missing-section · p125 — Two of the three red STOP items are missing — responder-safety (patient volatility) and the hypoxia-presents-as-agitation caution.
- **CPG:** STOP: "Consider risk of potential patient volatility when drug overdose is suspected" and "Consider that low oxygen may present with agitation until oxygen levels are corrected".
- **App:** content.critical carries only the needlestick item; the other two STOP rows are absent.
- **Fix:** Carry all three STOP rows verbatim as a list under a red-coloured key (e.g. dangersAndSafety), not a single string.

**Medium/low (unverified):**
- [medium] missing-section p125 — The final General Management step — ongoing assessment and breathing monitoring — is missing from the app's management list.  
  _fix:_ Append "Assess patient, monitor breathing closely, AVPU" to content.management.
- [medium] colour-coding p125 — The CPG's red 'ALL ALTERED CONSCIOUS PATIENTS – GENERAL MANAGEMENT' block renders navy in the app, dropping its urgency signalling.  
  _fix:_ Rename the key to one of the red-mapped keys (e.g. immediateAction / primarySurvey) or add a red entry for this section so it matches the CPG.
- [medium] colour-coding p125 — The CPG's red STOP block renders navy in the app because the key 'critical' is not in the red section map.  
  _fix:_ Rename to a red-mapped key such as dangersAndSafety and make it an array of the three STOP strings.
- [medium] render-risk p125 — The CPG's nested branch structure is flattened: the neck-injury instruction is a sub-branch of 'IF Breathing effectively' but the app lists it as an independent sibling step.  
  _fix:_ Nest the neck-injury condition inside the breathing-effectively branch (e.g. a branches object, or indent the string) so the dependency is visible on a 375px phone.
- [low] wrong-value p125 — The needlestick STOP item is paraphrased — 'from used sharps' is dropped and 'whenever' is weakened to 'when'.  
  _fix:_ Restore the CPG wording verbatim, including 'from used sharps' and 'whenever'.

## `anaphylaxis-cb-p127` — p127-129 · coverage: partial

**Graphics not represented in the app:**
- p128: Red 'STOP' header bar over a pink alert block containing the deterioration rule: unconscious & breathing ineffectively -> manage as cardiac arrest + SitRep Hatzolah + call ambulance.
- p128: Green 'FURTHER NOTES' block with six asterisk-bulleted clinical notes.
- p129: Photograph of the YELLOW adult Epi-Pen auto-injector beside the 0.3 mg initial-dose row — colour is the field cue for picking the adult device.
- p129: Photograph of the GREEN Epi-Pen Jr auto-injector beside the 0.15 mg initial-dose row — colour is the field cue for picking the paediatric device.

**Confirmed (verified) findings:**

### [critical] missing-section · p128 — The entire red STOP block (deterioration to cardiac arrest) is absent from the app.
- **CPG:** STOP — 'IF Patient becomes unconscious & Breathing ineffectively' / '+ Manage as per cardiac arrest' / 'SitRep: Hatzolah + call ambulance'
- **App:** No STOP / immediateAction section at all. content has only definition, recognition, management, dosing.
- **Fix:** Add a red-coloured section, e.g. content.immediateAction = { trigger: 'Patient becomes unconscious AND breathing ineffectively', actions: ['Manage as per Cardiac Arrest (L1)', 'SitRep: Hatzolah + call ambulance'] }. Key name must be one of the red-coloured keys (immediateAction) so it renders red like the CPG.

### [high] wrong-value · p127 — One of the two stand-alone recognition criteria is wrong: the app lists hypotension twice and omits 'Isolated breathing difficulty', so an isolated-breathing-difficulty anaphylaxis with a known allergen will not be recognised.
- **CPG:** 'OR Signs of isolated hypotension & Exposure to a KNOWN allergen' and 'OR Isolated breathing difficulty & Exposure to a KNOWN allergen'
- **App:** content.recognition.trigger: 'OR: Isolated hypotension + known allergen\nOR: Signs of isolated hypotension + known allergen' — both lines are hypotension; 'Isolated breathing difficulty' appears nowhere.
- **Fix:** Replace the second line with 'OR: Isolated breathing difficulty + exposure to a KNOWN allergen' so the two criteria read: signs of isolated hypotension + known allergen; isolated breathing difficulty + known allergen.

### [high] missing-section · p128 — The whole FURTHER NOTES block (6 notes) is missing, including the 'NEVER delay adrenaline' warning, the definition of 'known allergen', and the 4-hour hospital observation requirement.
- **CPG:** FURTHER NOTES: 'Anaphylaxis can be difficult to identify, while rash / itch is common they are NOT mandatory'; "For the purposes of this guideline a 'known allergen' refers to any substance that has previously caused an anaphylactic reaction in this patient"; 'Adrenaline saves lives in anaphylaxis. NEVER delay adrenaline in favour of other medications'; 'Advise patient of possible adrenaline side effects including heart racing / palpitations / anxiety'; 'Hospital based observations are required for a minimum of 4 hours'; 'Hypotension is an abnormally low blood pressure. Symptoms may include feeling dizzy, lightheaded or being in an altered conscious state.'
- **App:** No notes key on this protocol.
- **Fix:** Add content.notes as an array of the six CPG note strings, verbatim.

### [high] wrong-value · p129 — Dosing demographic comparison operators are wrong: the CPG uses '>= 6 years old' and '< 6 years old' for age, but the app renders both as ambiguous up/down arrows, losing the inclusive boundary at exactly 6 years.
- **CPG:** Card 1 Demographic: '≥ 6 years old  &  ↑ 20 kgs'. Card 2 Demographic: '< 6 years old  OR  ↓ 20 kgs'.
- **App:** dosing[0].demo: '↑6 years AND ↑20 kg'; dosing[1].demo: '↓6 years OR ↓20 kg'
- **Fix:** Set dosing[0].demo = '≥6 years old AND ↑20 kg' and dosing[1].demo = '<6 years old OR ↓20 kg'. Keep the ↑/↓ arrows only for the weight term, which is how the CPG prints it; age must use ≥ and <.

**Medium/low (unverified):**
- [medium] missing-graphic p129 — Both Epi-Pen device photographs are missing, so the app loses the yellow-adult / green-Junior colour cue used to pick the right auto-injector.  
  _fix:_ Add a device/colour field to each dosing card (e.g. device: 'Epi-Pen (yellow)' / 'Epi-Pen Jr (green)') or an inline SVG of the two auto-injectors under a '_' prefixed key, matching the CPG colours.
- [medium] missing-section p127 — The 'Example antigens' list in RECOGNITION is entirely absent from the app.  
  _fix:_ Add content.recognition.exampleAntigens as an array of the five CPG strings (renders as a nested subsection titled 'Example Antigens').
- [medium] wrong-value p127 — The definition is truncated — the second sentence explaining the immune mechanism is dropped.  
  _fix:_ Append the second sentence verbatim: 'Typically caused by an excessive immune system response to an allergen that is normally harmless to humans.'
- [low] colour-coding p127 — RECOGNITION is a green block in the CPG but renders navy in the app, because only keys named definition/assessmentTriangle get green.  
  _fix:_ Extend the green-section key list in the renderer to include 'recognition' (and 'notes' for FURTHER NOTES) so the app's section colours match the CPG's own colour-coding.
- [low] render-risk p127 — recognition.trigger packs three separate criteria into one string with embedded \n newlines, which will collapse into one run-on line on a 375px phone.  
  _fix:_ Split into an array of three strings (renders as a bullet list, one criterion per line) instead of one newline-joined string.

## `general-trauma-cb-p136` — p136 · coverage: partial

**Graphics not represented in the app:**
- p136: Green PRINCIPLES OF MAJOR TRAUMA header bar with a bulleted-list icon, over a pale-green four-row block, each row prefixed by a green asterisk glyph.
- p136: Red MANAGEMENT – MAJOR HAEMORHAGE CONTROL header bar with white flag/play icon, over a two-row IF→ACTION conditional table on pink background. Left column: red '+' cross glyph + black 'IF' badge + condition. Right column: the action. Row 1 'Life threatening bleed' → 'Apply pad and pressure'. Row 2 'Uncontrollable life-threatening bleed' → 'Combat Application Tourniquet'.
- p136: Red MANAGEMENT – BREATHING MANAGEMENT header bar with white flag/play icon, over a single IF→ACTION row: pink cell with a pill/capsule icon, black 'IF' badge and 'Breathing ineffectively & unconscious'; the right half is a distinct purple/violet cross-reference block reading 'Manage as per cardiac arrest' (purple = jump to another protocol).

**Confirmed (verified) findings:**

### [critical] missing-section · p136 — The entire red 'MANAGEMENT – MAJOR HAEMORHAGE CONTROL' IF→action block is missing; the app never mentions applying a pad and pressure, and never mentions the Combat Application Tourniquet for an uncontrollable life-threatening bleed.
- **CPG:** MANAGEMENT – MAJOR HAEMORHAGE CONTROL / IF 'Life threatening bleed' → 'Apply pad and pressure' / IF 'Uncontrollable life-threatening bleed' → 'Combat Application Tourniquet'
- **App:** management: ["Primary survey", "Control major haemorrhage", "If spinal injury suspected → apply cervical collar", "Call ambulance"] — a generic phrase 'Control major haemorrhage' with no technique and no escalation to a tourniquet.
- **Fix:** Replace the generic management array with the CPG's conditional block, keyed so it renders red: "immediateAction": { "majorHaemorrhageControl": [ { "if": "Life threatening bleed", "then": "Apply pad and pressure" }, { "if": "Uncontrollable life-threatening bleed", "then": "Combat Application Tourniquet" } ] }. Both columns are short, so the uniform-object array renders as a clean 2-column table at 375px.

### [high] not-in-cpg · p136 — Three of the four app management steps do not appear anywhere on p136, and one of them ('apply cervical collar') contradicts what the CPG actually instructs for suspected C-spine injury at Level 1.
- **CPG:** p136 contains no 'Primary survey', no 'cervical collar' and no 'Call ambulance' step. For C-spine the CPG says only: 'If possible C-spine injury, maintain the patients head in a neutral position avoiding neck flexion/extension'. The only management on this page is the two red blocks (haemorrhage control, breathing management).
- **App:** management: ["Primary survey", "Control major haemorrhage", "If spinal injury suspected → apply cervical collar", "Call ambulance"]
- **Fix:** Delete 'Primary survey', 'If spinal injury suspected → apply cervical collar' and 'Call ambulance'. Collar application is invented content for the L1 page — replace with the CPG's own manual in-line neutral-head wording under principles.

### [high] missing-section · p136 — Two of the four 'PRINCIPLES OF MAJOR TRAUMA' bullets are missing entirely — the hypothermia/warming principle and the mechanism-of-injury/history principle.
- **CPG:** 'Hypothermia is a significant concern leading to increased mortality. Warming the patient is important and where appropriate should NOT be delayed' and 'The Mechanism of injury is a significant risk factor. Thorough History taking is important to identify such mechanisms and subsequent care/priority.'
- **App:** principles: only 2 entries — traumatic cardiac arrest / correctable causes, and the C-spine bullet. No hypothermia bullet, no mechanism-of-injury bullet.
- **Fix:** Restore both bullets verbatim into the principles array, keeping the CPG's bolded 'NOT' emphasis in the hypothermia line ('...where appropriate should NOT be delayed').

### [high] missing-section · p136 — The DEFINITION block is reduced to a single 9-word sentence; the CPG's two full paragraphs are almost entirely dropped, including the whole second paragraph on localised vs undifferentiated trauma and the systematic head-to-toe assessment requirement.
- **CPG:** Para 1: 'Trauma refers to bodily injury caused by a sudden application of significant force (E.G. Fall). Such force may be blunt (E.G. Head strike against concrete) OR penetrating (E.G. Punctured by a knife). In either case it results in damage to tissue and organs, ranging from minor cuts to life threatening injuries.' Para 2: 'Trauma may also be localised and occur to a specific body region (E.G. Heavy object falling on a foot) OR it may be undifferentiated (E.G. Car crash, falls, etc). Undifferentiated trauma requires a systematic head to toe assessment to ensure no injuries are missed.'
- **App:** definition: "Bodily injury from sudden significant force — blunt or penetrating."
- **Fix:** Make definition an array of the two CPG paragraphs verbatim. The instruction that undifferentiated trauma 'requires a systematic head to toe assessment to ensure no injuries are missed' is actionable guidance and must not be lost.

**Medium/low (unverified):**
- [medium] colour-coding p136 — The CPG's two management blocks carry red headers (immediate/critical action) but the app stores them under a plain key named 'management', which the renderer colours navy — the urgency signal is lost.  
  _fix:_ Nest both blocks under the key 'immediateAction' (which the renderer colours red) rather than 'management', so the CB responder sees the same danger colouring as the printed CPG.
- [low] colour-coding p136 — 'PRINCIPLES OF MAJOR TRAUMA' is a green (definition/notes) block in the CPG, but the app's 'principles' key renders navy.  
  _fix:_ Either extend the renderer's green key list to include 'principles', or nest the principles under the green-rendering definition group, so the green/navy split matches the page.
- [low] typo p136 — The CPG's own heading misspells haemorrhage as 'HAEMORHAGE' (one r) while its body text on the same page spells it 'Haemorrhage' — recorded so the app's wording choice is a deliberate decision, not a silent drift.  
  _fix:_ When adding the missing block, title it 'Major Haemorrhage Control' (correct spelling, matching the CPG's own body text) rather than reproducing the header's typo.
- [low] wrong-value p136 — The C-spine principle is paraphrased in a way that drops the specific caution the CPG gives about neck movement.  
  _fix:_ Use the CPG wording verbatim: head in a neutral position, avoiding neck flexion/extension. 'Neutral spinal alignment' is a different (whole-spine) instruction and omits the flexion/extension warning.
- [low] render-risk p136 — The CPG's IF→action rows are a two-column conditional table; flattening them into the existing plain string array would lose the condition/action split that makes the block scannable under pressure.  
  _fix:_ Model each row as a uniform object { if, then }. Both fields are short (max ~36 chars), so the renderer keeps a real 2-column table at 375px instead of degrading to stacked record cards.

## `vital-signs-p5` — p5-6 · coverage: partial

**Graphics not represented in the app:**
- p5: Red/pink highlighted callout band immediately under the table, two rows deep, each with a red icon in the left gutter: a red ✗ cross for 'ANY deviation from normal VSS…' and a red telephone-handset glyph for 'Patients with ANY vital signs… put through to VVED as a minimum'. The pink fill and red icons mark both lines as critical.
- p6: Green 'FURTHER NOTES' header bar (white text, list icon) over a pale-green body block, with a green asterisk (✱) bullet glyph marking the note.

**Confirmed (verified) findings:**

### [critical] wrong-value · p5 — Adult AVPU/GCS is widened from the CPG's '15' to '13–15', so a GCS 13 or 14 adult reads as having acceptable vital signs.
- **CPG:** Adult row, AVPU/GCS column: "15" (bare, with no 'A /' prefix — every paediatric row above it reads "A / 15").
- **App:** Adult row AVPU/GCS = "13–15".
- **Fix:** Change the Adult row's AVPU/GCS cell from "13–15" to "15".

### [high] wrong-value · p5 — The escalation destination is invented: the CPG routes borderline patients to VVED, the app routes them to Ambulance Victoria.
- **CPG:** Red callout, second row: "…must be put through to VVED as a minimum".
- **App:** notes[2]: "…must be escalated to Ambulance Victoria". 'VVED' appears nowhere in the app content.
- **Fix:** Restore the CPG wording verbatim, including the destination VVED and the qualifier 'as a minimum'.

### [high] wrong-value · p5 — The escalation trigger is loosened from 'persistently borderline / close to being outside' the ranges to 'persistently outside' them.
- **CPG:** Red callout, second row: "Patients with ANY vital signs that are persistently borderline / close to being outside of the above ranges must be put through to VVED as a minimum".
- **App:** notes[2]: "Patients with ANY vital signs persistently outside ranges must be escalated…" — the 'borderline / close to being outside' trigger is deleted.
- **Fix:** Replace notes[2] with the CPG sentence verbatim: "Patients with ANY vital signs that are persistently borderline / close to being outside of the above ranges must be put through to VVED as a minimum".

**Medium/low (unverified):**
- [medium] colour-coding p5 — The two red-highlighted callout notes are flattened into the generic `notes` array, so they render navy instead of red and lose their critical framing.  
  _fix:_ Split the two p5 callouts into a red-rendering key (e.g. `immediateAction` or `redFlags`) and keep the p6 child-definition text in a separate green-rendering key (e.g. `definition`), preserving CPG order: red note 1, red VVED note, then the p6 further note.
- [medium] missing-section p6 — The p6 FURTHER NOTES block is truncated to its first clause — the rationale and the explicit 'does NOT relate to…' carve-out are missing.  
  _fix:_ Restore the full FURTHER NOTES paragraph verbatim, including the rationale sentence and the 'does NOT relate to emotional care, mental health, OR legal obligations of caring for a person under the age of 18' clause.
- [medium] wrong-value p5 — The two youngest age bands use ≤ where the CPG prints a ↓ (less than) arrow; '≤ 3 months' then overlaps the '3 – 12 months' row.  
  _fix:_ Render these as "< 24 hours" and "< 3 months" to match the CPG's ↓ glyph and remove the overlap with the 3–12 months band.
- [medium] wrong-value p5 — Every ↑ arrow in the SPO2 and BP columns is silently converted to ≥, an inclusive operator the CPG page does not print.  
  _fix:_ Adopt one documented convention for the CPG's ↑/↓ glyphs and apply it to every column, or reproduce the arrows as printed. Do not upgrade ↑ to ≥ in some columns while treating the same glyph as a boundary in the Age column.
- [low] not-in-cpg p5 — The BP column header is relabelled 'BP sys'; the CPG header is just 'BP'.  
  _fix:_ Use the CPG's header "BP". If a systolic clarification is wanted, add it as an app-authored note rather than altering the CPG's column label.
- [low] render-risk p5 — Eight columns of a 19-row table on a 375px phone push SPO2, HR and BP off-screen behind horizontal scroll.  
  _fix:_ Scrolling with a sticky Age column is workable, but consider a phone layout that keeps Category out of the scroll region or renders each age band as a compact card so RR / SPO2 / HR / BP are readable without lateral scrolling.

## `conscious-status-fr-p13` — p13-15 · coverage: partial

**Graphics not represented in the app:**
- p14: Colour-banded Glasgow Coma Scale scoring table (magnifier icon header). Three data columns: '↑ 4 Years Old', '↓ 5 Years Old', score. Mid-green banded sub-headers split it into Eye Opening (Score "E"), Verbal Response (Score "V") and Motor Response (Score "M"), and a closing green Total band reading '= "E" + "V" + "M"'. The whole age-split structure and the Total band carry the clinical meaning.
- p13: Green section-header bars with glyph icons ('?' = DEFINITION, list icon = AVPU) that colour-code the two narrative blocks as definition/notes content.
- p15: Green section-header bar with list icon: FURTHER NOTES, above two bullets on the use of painful stimuli.

**Confirmed (verified) findings:**

### [critical] table-misrendered · p14 — The GCS table's entire paediatric column ('↓ 5 Years Old') and both age-column headers are missing — the app shows one unlabelled descriptor set, so a young child would be scored against the older-child/adult wording.
- **CPG:** The GCS table has two descriptor columns headed '↑ 4 Years Old' and '↓ 5 Years Old' plus a score column. The paediatric column reads: Eye Opening — 'Spontaneous' 4, 'Reacts to speech' 3, 'Reacts to pain' 2, 'None' 1; Verbal Response — 'Appropriate words / smile' 5, 'Cries but consolable' 4, 'Persistently irritable' 3, 'Moans to pain' 2, 'None' 1; Motor Response — 'Spontaneous' 6, 'Localises to pain' 5, 'Withdraws to pain' 4, 'Flexion response' 3, 'Extension response' 2, 'None' 1.
- **App:** content.gcs has only eyeOpening / verbalResponse / motorResponse, each an array of {score, response} with a single descriptor per score and no age label anywhere: e.g. { score: 4, response: 'Withdraws from pain' }.
- **Fix:** Restructure content.gcs into an explicit { headers, rows } table per component, e.g. headers ['Score','↑ 4 Years Old','↓ 5 Years Old'] and rows ['4','Spontaneous + tracking','Spontaneous'] … so both age bands are present. Carry the ↑/↓ glyphs through exactly as printed; note the app elsewhere converts ↑→≥ and ↓→≤ (vital signs), but ≥4 and ≤5 would overlap at ages 4–5 here, so confirm the intended operator with the clinical owner rather than inventing one.

### [high] missing-section · p14 — The whole green 'Glasgow Coma Scale (GCS)' notes block is absent, including the instruction that the score must not be estimated.
- **CPG:** 'A formal GCS should be undertaken in more complex patient presentations'; 'The GCS is an objective measure of consciousness. The score should NOT be estimated.'; 'The maximum GCS score is 15 and represents a patient that is fully alert and orientated to person, place and time. The minimum GCS score is 3 and represents a patient that is completely unresponsive to stimuli.'
- **App:** A single compressed string: gcs.description = 'Glasgow Coma Scale — scores 3 to 15'.
- **Fix:** Add a gcs.notes array carrying all three bullets verbatim, keeping the emphasis on 'NOT be estimated' and on what 15 and 3 each represent.

### [high] missing-section · p13 — The entire green AVPU notes block (4 bullets) is missing, including the rule that a child must be woken before a conscious state assessment and how to record the result.
- **CPG:** 'AVPU is quick and simple to apply and is appropriate for determining conscious state whilst the initial assessment (E.G. Primary Survey) is conducted'; 'AVPU is also the preferred tool for assessing conscious state in children where adapting the GCS can be problematic. It is widely used and consistent with practice at the Royal Children's Hospital.'; 'A child cannot have a conscious state assessment done while asleep. They must be woken first. If the child wakes and remains awake and alert, record this as an "A" for AVPU. If the child wakes but remains drowsy and appears inattentive, record this as a "V".'; 'If a patient has clinical or social issues such as aphasia / dysphasia, facial injuries or language Barriers then AVPU is an appropriate tool to assess consciousness'
- **App:** content.avpu is only the four {letter, meaning} rows — no notes at all. (The CB sibling 'conscious-status-cb' does carry a shortened version of the child-asleep rule, so the FR user sees less than the CB user.)
- **Fix:** Add an avpuNotes array with all four bullets verbatim, above or beside the AVPU letter table.

**Medium/low (unverified):**
- [medium] missing-section p14 — The GCS table's closing Total band is missing — nothing in the app tells the user the total is the sum of the three components.  
  _fix:_ Add a total row/line reading 'Total = "E" + "V" + "M"' and label the three subsections 'Eye Opening (Score "E")', 'Verbal Response (Score "V")', 'Motor Response (Score "M")' as the CPG does.
- [medium] wrong-value p14 — Eye Opening descriptors are altered: score 4 drops '+ tracking' (and matches the paediatric column instead), and score 2 is shortened.  
  _fix:_ Set the older-child/adult column to 'Spontaneous + tracking' (4) and 'To painful stimuli' (2), and put 'Spontaneous' / 'Reacts to pain' in the paediatric column where the CPG has them.
- [medium] wrong-value p14 — Verbal 5 loses its orientation qualifier and Motor 5 is reworded.  
  _fix:_ Restore 'Orientated (person, place, time)' and 'Localises to pain' verbatim — the '(person, place, time)' qualifier is what the GCS notes block on the same page refers to when defining a score of 15.
- [medium] missing-section p15 — The FURTHER NOTES block on painful stimuli is missing entirely.  
  _fix:_ Add a furtherNotes array with both bullets verbatim, preserving the emphasised 'NOT'.
- [medium] render-risk p14 — If the paediatric column is added as a third field on the {score, response} objects, the renderer will degrade the GCS from a table to stacked record cards on a 375px phone.  
  _fix:_ Use the explicit { headers, rows } table form for each GCS component so it renders as a real table with a sticky first column; put the short 'Score' column first so the sticky column stays narrow.
- [low] table-misrendered p13 — The AVPU table's framing prompt and its response→letter direction are not carried into the app.  
  _fix:_ Keep the CPG's framing: title the block 'AVPU – When assessed is the patient…' and order the columns response → '=' → letter.
- [low] colour-coding p13 — Every content block across p13–15 is green (definition/notes) in the CPG, but the app renders all sections navy because it has no green-keyed section.  
  _fix:_ Add the missing 'definition' key (see the p13 DEFINITION finding) so the opening block renders green as the CPG does; leave the remainder non-urgent.

## `perfusion-assessment-fr-p18` — p18 · coverage: partial

**Graphics not represented in the app:**
- p18: Red/pink highlighted footnote strip immediately under the table: '✱ Pulse and sBP in this table specifically refer to ↑ 15 year olds only'. Red background = age restriction on the whole table.
- p18: MANAGEMENT block (navy header) rendered as a 3-row icon table: blue ✕ (avoid), blue + (do/position), blue telephone handset (call), each followed by a black inverted 'IF' badge, the condition '↓ Adequate perfusion', and the action in a second column.
- p18: Inline glyph notation used as operators: black inverted 'IF' badges and black inverted 'OR' badges inside prose and inside table cells (e.g. '↓ 50 OR ↑ 100', 'Alert OR Altered'), plus ↑/↓ arrows used throughout the CPG as its own greater-than/less-than notation (confirmed on p5, where the same ↑ glyph is used, e.g. '↑ 95%', '↑ 70').
- p18: Four colour-coded section header bars with icons: green '? DEFINITION', green 'RECOGNITION', navy 'MANAGEMENT', green 'FURTHER NOTES'. The colour tells the reader definition/notes vs action.

**Confirmed (verified) findings:**

### [critical] missing-section · p18 — The rule that defines when the table means '↓ Adequate perfusion' (2 or more criteria abnormal) is entirely absent from the app.
- **CPG:** RECOGNITION: "The patient is considered to have less than adequate (↓ Adequate) perfusion IF 2 or more of the below criteria are outside of the Adequate criteria"
- **App:** content contains only the `table` key. No recognition sentence, no '2 or more' threshold anywhere in the protocol.
- **Fix:** Add above the table, e.g. `recognition: ["The patient is considered to have less than adequate (↓ Adequate) perfusion IF 2 or more of the below criteria are outside of the Adequate criteria"]`. Without it the app's table implies a single abnormal column is enough to declare ↓ Adequate perfusion.

### [critical] missing-section · p18 — The red-highlighted age restriction limiting the Pulse and sBP columns to over-15s is missing, so the adult thresholds appear to apply to children.
- **CPG:** Red/pink highlighted strip directly under the table: "✱ Pulse and sBP in this table specifically refer to ↑ 15 year olds only"
- **App:** Nothing. The table is presented with no age qualifier, so 60–100 pulse and ≥100 sBP read as universal.
- **Fix:** Add the note verbatim and render it red (the renderer colours `redFlags`/`immediateAction` red — either place it there or extend the red key list): e.g. `redFlags: ["Pulse and sBP in this table specifically refer to ↑ 15 year olds only"]`.

### [high] missing-section · p18 — The entire MANAGEMENT section (3 actions, including calling an ambulance) is missing from the app.
- **CPG:** MANAGEMENT: "✕ IF ↓ Adequate perfusion — Avoid: Standing / walking patient"; "+ IF ↓ Adequate perfusion — Position: Supine / legs raised"; "📞 IF ↓ Adequate perfusion — SitRep: Hatzolah dispatch + call ambulance"
- **App:** No management key at all. (Note: the CB sibling entry in contentData.js line 337 does carry a management string; the FR entry does not.)
- **Fix:** Add `management: ["IF ↓ Adequate perfusion → Avoid: Standing / walking patient", "IF ↓ Adequate perfusion → Position: Supine / legs raised", "IF ↓ Adequate perfusion → SitRep: Hatzolah dispatch + call ambulance"]`.

**Medium/low (unverified):**
- [medium] not-in-cpg p18 — The '↓ Adequate' Conscious State cell adds 'Dizzy, Lightheaded', which does not appear anywhere on the CPG page.  
  _fix:_ Change the cell to "Alert OR Altered". The same invented text also appears in the CB version (contentData.js line 333) and should be corrected there too.
- [medium] missing-section p18 — The whole FURTHER NOTES section (4 bullets, including the two 'may be normal' caveats) is missing.  
  _fix:_ Add `notes: ["Always assume abnormal vital signs are due to illness in any patient that appears unwell", "An isolated pulse between 50 and 60 BPM may be normal for some adult patients — particularly if they are very fit (E.G. marathon runner)", "An isolated systolic BP below 100 mmHg may be normal for some adult patients — particularly if they are very small or have paraplegia / quadriplegia"]`.
- [medium] missing-section p18 — The green DEFINITION block (2 bullets) is missing, including the fact that the assessment is four vital signs.  
  _fix:_ Add `definition: [ ...the two bullets verbatim... ]`. A key named `definition` is rendered green by the app, matching the CPG's green block.
- [medium] wrong-value p18 — The CPG's ↑ glyph is translated inconsistently: the same '↑ 100' becomes '≥100' in the sBP column but '>100' in the Pulse column.  
  _fix:_ Use one convention for the whole table. Preferred: keep the CPG's own glyphs as the rest of contentData.js does ('↑50 years old', '↑6 years AND ↑20 kg'), i.e. sBP Adequate "↑ 100", ↓ Adequate "↓ 100", Pulse ↓ Adequate "↓ 50 OR ↑ 100". If ASCII operators are kept, do not mix ≥ and > for the same glyph.
- [medium] colour-coding p18 — The page's four colour-coded blocks collapse into a single navy table section in the app, losing the green/navy/red signalling.  
  _fix:_ When restoring the missing sections, name the keys so the colour rules fire: `definition` (green), the age-restriction note under a red-rendered key, `management` and the table navy. Verify the red note renders red on device.
- [low] wrong-value p18 — Two 'No Perfusion' cells are paraphrased rather than quoted from the CPG.  
  _fix:_ Restore the CPG wording: "No pulse" and "Unable to record".
- [low] wrong-value p18 — The tile summary describes the assessment as 'BP and pulse criteria', omitting two of the four vital signs the CPG names.  
  _fix:_ Reword to cover all four, e.g. "Four vital signs: skin, pulse, sBP and conscious state".
- [low] render-risk p18 — A 5-column table with three long free-text cells per row will need horizontal scrolling on a 375px phone, and the sticky first column has an empty header.  
  _fix:_ Confirm the table scrolls horizontally inside its own container at 375px and that the sticky first column (Adequate / ↓ Adequate / No Perfusion) stays readable with a blank header cell.
- [low] colour-coding p18 — Protocol is categorised 'cardiac' although the CPG files it under ASSESSMENTS, which the app maps to the 'primary' category/tile colour.  
  _fix:_ Set category to "primary" per the project's Protocol → Category mapping, so it groups with the other assessment protocols; check the CB sibling for the same issue.

## `time-critical-guidelines-p21` — p21-26 · coverage: missing
_Full replacement content was extracted by the audit agent._

**Graphics not represented in the app:**
- p21: Red title banner "TIME CRITICAL GUIDELINES" between two red rules, followed by a green section-header bar with a list icon reading "PRINCIPLES OF THE TIME CRITICAL GUIDELINES". The 10 principle rows form a two-tone colour-coded block: rows 1-3 on a red/pink band with red asterisk bullets (the mandatory MUST rows), rows 4-10 on a green band with green asterisk bullets. Inline up/down arrow glyphs are used for thresholds ("Patients down-arrow 16 or up-arrow 55 years").
- p22: Green section-header bar with a list icon reading "TIME CRITICAL DEFINITIONS", followed by three green-banded definition blocks each introduced by a green magnifying-glass icon and an underlined bold caption: ACTUAL, EMERGENT, POTENTIAL.
- p23: Red section-header bar with magnifying-glass icon "ACTUAL TIME CRITICAL - VSS", above a colour-coded 7-row table on a peach/salmon zebra background. Header row (darker salmon) has merged cells: Age | SpO2 | Respiratory Rate (spanning two sub-columns) | Heart Rate (spanning two sub-columns) | BP | GCS. Every threshold cell carries a bold down-arrow or up-arrow glyph rather than a < or > operator; the Age column itself uses down-arrows for the two youngest bands ("down-arrow 3 months", "down-arrow 24 hours").
- p23: Lavender/blue action block introduced by a blue cross icon and a black [IF] badge: "IF ANY of the above criteria", then a blue telephone icon line "SitRep: Hatzolah dispatch + call ambulance", then a blue ambulance icon line "Expedite transport", then a three-row two-column conditional grid pairing black [IF] badges (Trauma / Medical / Possible) with destinations (Highest trauma service within 60 mins / Closest appropriate hospital / Rendezvous with paramedics).
- p24: Amber/gold section-header bar with magnifying-glass icon "EMERGENT TIME CRITICAL - PATTERN OF INJURY", above a pale-yellow banded panel with amber asterisk bullets for the three group headings (Penetrating Trauma, Blunt Trauma, Specific Injuries) and small grey triangle sub-bullets for the criteria. Up-arrow glyphs encode the burn thresholds (10% TBSA paediatrics, 20% TBSA adults, 1000 volts).
- p24: Lavender/blue action block: blue cross icon + black [IF] badge "ANY of the above criteria", telephone icon "SitRep: Hatzolah dispatch + call ambulance", ambulance icon "Expedite transport", sub-bullets "Highest trauma service within 60 mins" and black [IF] badge "Possible - Rendezvous with paramedics".
- p25: Amber/gold section-header bar with magnifying-glass icon "EMERGENT TIME CRITICAL - PATTERN OF ILLNESS", above a pale-yellow panel with one amber asterisk heading "Medical symptoms / Conditions" and 13 grey triangle sub-bullets.
- p25: Lavender/blue action block: blue cross icon + black [IF] badge "ANY of the above criteria", telephone icon "SitRep: Hatzolah dispatch + call ambulance", ambulance icon "Expedite transport", sub-bullets "Closest appropriate hospital" and black [IF] badge "Possible - Rendezvous with paramedics".
- p26: Green section-header bar with magnifying-glass icon "POTENTIALLY TIME CRITICAL - MECHAISM OF INJURY [&] VULNERABILITY" containing an inline black badge with a white ampersand - a logical AND operator rendered as a graphic. The green panel below has two asterisk headings, "At least 1 Mechanism of injury" (8 sub-bullets, up-arrow glyphs for 30 km/hr, 60 km/hr, 3 m, 3 m) and a second heading prefixed by the same black [&] badge, "At least 1 vulnerability" (11 sub-bullets, up-arrow 55 / down-arrow 16 years).
- p26: Lavender/blue action block: blue cross icon + black [IF] badge "At least 1 mechanism of injury" + black [&] badge + "At least 1 vulnerability", telephone icon "SitRep: Hatzolah dispatch + call ambulance", ambulance icon "Expedite transport", sub-bullets "Nearest trauma service" and black [IF] badge "Possible - Rendezvous with paramedics".

**Confirmed (verified) findings:**

### [critical] missing-section · p21 — The entire six-page Time Critical Guidelines section (CPG p21-26) has no entry in the app - no key exists in assessmentsContent, so responders cannot reach any time critical criteria, thresholds or escalation actions offline.
- **CPG:** "TIME CRITICAL GUIDELINES" p21-26, comprising: "PRINCIPLES OF THE TIME CRITICAL GUIDELINES" (10 rows, 3 red MUST rows + 7 green rows), "TIME CRITICAL DEFINITIONS" (ACTUAL / EMERGENT / POTENTIAL), "ACTUAL TIME CRITICAL - VSS" (7-row age-banded threshold table), "EMERGENT TIME CRITICAL - PATTERN OF INJURY", "EMERGENT TIME CRITICAL - PATTERN OF ILLNESS", and "POTENTIALLY TIME CRITICAL - MECHAISM OF INJURY & VULNERABILITY", each closed by an "IF ANY of the above criteria" action block.
- **App:** Nothing. Object.keys(assessmentsContent) returns vital-signs, clinical-flags, clinical-approach-cb/-fr, conscious-status-cb/-fr, respiratory-assessment-cb/-fr, perfusion-assessment-cb/-fr, pain-assessment, weight-calculations, ventilation-rates, paediatric-assessment-triangle. There is no time-critical* key anywhere in src/data/contentData.js; the only hits for "time critical" are two cells inside the clinical-approach-fr assessment table ('Medical time critical', 'Trauma time critical') which point at a guideline that does not exist in the app.
- **Fix:** Add a new assessmentsContent entry keyed 'time-critical-guidelines' using the object supplied in proposedContent. Recommended wrapper: { title: 'Time Critical Guidelines', level: 'ALL', universal: true, category: 'primary', summary: 'Actual, Emergent and Potentially time critical criteria plus mandatory SitRep and transport actions', content: <proposedContent> }. Level 'ALL' / universal:true matches the precedent already used for the other non-L1 foundational assessments vital-signs (p5) and clinical-flags (p7).

### [critical] missing-graphic · p23 — The ACTUAL TIME CRITICAL - VSS threshold table is absent. It is a distinct table from the app's existing 'vital-signs' entry (normal ranges) - it gives the point at which a patient becomes time critical - and every one of its 7 age bands x 8 threshold values is missing.
- **CPG:** Red-headed table "ACTUAL TIME CRITICAL - VSS", columns Age | SpO2 | Respiratory Rate (two sub-columns) | Heart Rate (two sub-columns) | BP | GCS. Rows: Adult (SpO2 down-arrow 90%, RR down 10 / up 30, HR down 60 / up 120, BP down 90, GCS down 13); 12-15 years (96%, 14/26, 60/120, 90, 15); 5-11 years (96%, 16/34, 70/135, 80, 15); 1-4 years (96%, 20/40, 85/150, 70, 15); 3-12 months (96%, 25/55, 105/165, 65, 15); down-arrow 3 months (96%, 25/60, 110/170, 60, 15); down-arrow 24 hours (96%, 25/60, 110/170, 60, 15).
- **App:** Nothing. The app's 'vital-signs' table (contentData.js line 27) holds ACCEPTABLE ranges from CPG p5 (e.g. adult RR 12-30, SpO2 >=92%, HR 60-100, BP >=100, GCS 13-15) - the opposite semantics. A responder looking for the time critical trigger points has no source in the app.
- **Fix:** Render actualTimeCriticalVss from proposedContent as a table. Note the CPG splits Respiratory Rate and Heart Rate into two unlabelled sub-columns (low threshold, high threshold); the proposal merges each into one cell as 'down 10 / up 30' to keep 6 columns readable on a 375px phone. The header block is RED in the CPG - this section must be rendered urgent/red, not navy.

### [high] missing-graphic · p23 — All four lavender 'IF ANY of the above criteria' action blocks (p23, p24, p25, p26) are absent. These are the actual instructions - SitRep, call ambulance, and a destination that differs per block - and they are the operational payload of the whole section.
- **CPG:** p23: "[IF] ANY of the above criteria" then phone-icon "SitRep: Hatzolah dispatch + call ambulance", ambulance-icon "Expedite transport", then a two-column conditional layout: "[IF] Trauma - Highest trauma service within 60 mins", "[IF] Medical - Closest appropriate hospital", "[IF] Possible - Rendezvous with paramedics". p24 destination is "Highest trauma service within 60 mins"; p25 is "Closest appropriate hospital"; p26 is "Nearest trauma service" - the destinations differ per block and must not be conflated.
- **App:** Nothing.
- **Fix:** Add the four *Action arrays from proposedContent, keeping each block's own destination. These are the highest-value strings on the pages; keep the conditional prefix ('IF Trauma -', 'IF Medical -', 'IF Possible -') inline so the branch logic survives a plain bullet render.

**Medium/low (unverified):**
- [medium] colour-coding p21 — The CPG colour-codes this section into four distinct urgency bands (red / amber / green) and the app's key-name based section colouring will render every proposed key navy unless the mapping is extended.  
  _fix:_ Extend the section colour mapping so actualTimeCriticalVss + actualTimeCriticalAction render red, emergentTimeCriticalPatternOfInjury / emergentTimeCriticalPatternOfIllness (and their action blocks) render amber, and potentiallyTimeCritical renders green - matching the CPG bands exactly.
- [medium] render-risk p23 — The ACTUAL TIME CRITICAL VSS table has 8 value columns in the CPG (Age, SpO2, RR-low, RR-high, HR-low, HR-high, BP, GCS) which will overflow a 375px phone if transcribed column-for-column.  
  _fix:_ Use the 6-column form in proposedContent (merged 'down 10 / up 30' cells). Keep Age as the sticky first column. Do not add a second header row - the app's table renderer supports only one.
- [low] typo p21 — The CPG misspells 'Victoria' as 'Vicotria' in the third principle. Transcribe as printed but flag to the CPG authors.  
  _fix:_ proposedContent transcribes this verbatim as 'Ambulance Vicotria'. Raise the misspelling with the CPG owner rather than silently correcting it in the app.
- [low] typo p26 — The p26 section header misspells 'MECHANISM' as 'MECHAISM'.  
  _fix:_ The proposed key potentiallyTimeCritical renders as 'Potentially Time Critical'; if a literal heading is used, transcribe 'MECHAISM' as printed and flag it to the CPG owner.
- [low] typo p21 — The last principle has a mid-sentence capital and a missing word: 'even though does Not meet stated parameters'.  
  _fix:_ Transcribed verbatim in proposedContent. Do not silently repair to 'even though they do not meet'; flag to the CPG owner.

## `altered-consciousness-fr-p33` — p33-36 · coverage: partial

**Graphics not represented in the app:**
- p34: Red 'STOP' banner with white triangle icon over three red-tinted safety rows (needlestick risk, patient volatility, hypoxia-induced agitation).
- p34: Red 'ALL ALTERED CONSCIOUS PATIENTS – GENERAL MANAGEMENT' clipboard banner; the whole block is red-tinted (critical/immediate action colour coding).
- p34: Nested conditional-logic structure built from black 'IF' and '&' glyph chips, red '+' do-this glyphs and black '✕' do-not glyphs: unresponsive & no pulse -> Cardiac Arrest; inadequate ventilations & has pulse -> supine/suction/BVM; adequate ventilations -> c-spine YES/NO positioning.
- p34: Purple/periwinkle medication callout box with pill icon: 'Oxygen, BVM (8-15L O2)'.
- p34: Age-banded ventilation rate table (5 rows) with ↑ / ↓ arrow glyphs for over/under: ↑15 yrs 5 s or 12x/min; 12-15 yrs 3-4 s or 14-26x/min; 5-11 yrs 2-3 s or 16-34x/min; 1-4 yrs 2-3 s or 20-40x/min; ↓1 yr 2 s or 25-55x/min.
- p34: Purple/periwinkle medication callout box with pill icon: 'Oxygen, NRB (10-15L O2)'.
- p34: Red telephone glyph row 'SitRep: Hatzolah dispatch + call ambulance' and red speech-bubble glyph row 'Consider possible causes (AEIOUTIPS) + manage appropriately where possible'.
- p35: Navy 'HEAD INJURY – ADDITIONAL MANAGEMENT' clipboard banner with black 'ONLY IF' chip and '✕ Avoid' glyph row.
- p35: Navy 'OVERDOSE (DRUGS / MEDICATIONS) – ADDITIONAL MANAGEMENT' block; the history-taking list conveys the mnemonic TRADIES purely by underlining the first letter of Time / Route / Agent / Dose / Intent / Emesis / Symptoms — the mnemonic is a typographic graphic that the text layer drops.
- p35: Navy 'SEIZURE – ADDITIONAL MANAGEMENT' clipboard banner with red '+' do glyphs and black '✕' do-not glyph ('Do NOT forcibly restrict...').
- p36: Green 'FURTHER NOTES' banner with list icon over four green-tinted asterisk-bulleted note rows, including the Poisons Information number 13 11 26 in bold.

**Confirmed (verified) findings:**

### [critical] wrong-value · p34 — App tells the responder to use the recovery position unconditionally, dropping the CPG's c-spine exception that requires supine positioning.
- **CPG:** 'IF Adequate ventilations' → 'IF NO c-spine concerns … Position: Lateral' / 'IF YES C-spine concerns … Position: Supine'.
- **App:** 'If breathing effectively → Recovery position' — no mention of c-spine, and no supine alternative.
- **Fix:** Replace with two branch steps: 'IF adequate ventilations AND NO c-spine concerns → Position: Lateral' and 'IF adequate ventilations AND c-spine concerns → Position: Supine'. Do not use the phrase 'recovery position'; the CPG says 'Position: Lateral'.

### [critical] missing-section · p34 — The whole red STOP safety block is missing from the app.
- **CPG:** STOP: 'Consider risk of needlestick injuries from used sharps whenever drug overdose is suspected'; 'Consider risk of potential patient volatility when drug overdose is suspected'; 'Consider that hypoxic patients may present with agitation until oxygen levels are corrected'.
- **App:** No STOP / dangers / responder-safety content anywhere in the protocol.
- **Fix:** Add content.dangersAndSafety as an array of the three STOP strings verbatim. The key name dangersAndSafety makes the renderer colour it red, matching the CPG's red STOP banner.

### [critical] missing-section · p35 — The entire 'OVERDOSE (DRUGS / MEDICATIONS) – ADDITIONAL MANAGEMENT' section, including the TRADIES history, is missing.
- **CPG:** 'Evidence suggestive of illicit drug use may include' Paraphernalia (E.G. Used needles, empty packets, etc.), Track marks, 'Pinpoint pupils OR dilated pupils', 'Hypothermia OR hyperthermia'; then 'Where safe and possible to do so gain the following information': Time (Time of consumption), Route (Method of consumption (E.G. oral, injected)), Agent (Drug(s) and / or medication(s) consumed), Dose (Quantity consumed), Intent (Intended self-harm OR accidental overdose), Emesis (Have they vomited since consumption), Symptoms (Any other symptoms since consumption).
- **App:** Only the word 'overdose' inside the invented step 'Consider head injury, overdose as differentials'. No overdose section at all.
- **Fix:** Add content.overdoseAdditionalManagement = { evidenceOfIllicitDrugUse: [...4 items with their E.G. text...], historyToGain: { headers: ['TRADIES','What to ask'], rows: [['Time','Time of consumption'],['Route','Method of consumption (E.G. oral, injected)'],['Agent','Drug(s) and / or medication(s) consumed'],['Dose','Quantity consumed'],['Intent','Intended self-harm OR accidental overdose'],['Emesis','Have they vomited since consumption'],['Symptoms','Any other symptoms since consumption']] } }. Name the mnemonic TRADIES explicitly — in the PDF it exists only as underlined initials and the text layer loses it.

**Medium/low (unverified):**
- [medium] colour-coding p34 — The CPG's red-coded general-management block renders navy in the app, losing its critical/immediate-action signal.  
  _fix:_ Rename the general-management array to immediateAction (or primarySurvey) so it renders red, put the STOP items under dangersAndSafety (red), and leave the head-injury / overdose / seizure sub-sections under neutral navy keys to match the CPG's own colour split.
- [medium] wrong-value p34 — The app narrows the CPG's 'consider AEIOUTIPS causes' step to two named differentials.  
  _fix:_ Replace with 'Consider possible causes (AEIOUTIPS) + manage appropriately where possible'.
- [medium] wrong-value p34 — The SitRep step omits Hatzolah dispatch as a notification target.  
  _fix:_ Replace with 'SitRep: Hatzolah dispatch + call ambulance'.
- [medium] wrong-value p34 — The cardiac-arrest trigger is reworded, losing the CPG's 'becomes unresponsive' (deterioration during care) framing.  
  _fix:_ Use 'IF patient becomes unresponsive AND no pulse — manage as Cardiac Arrest'. The p36 note reinforces that this applies to patients who deteriorate while in Hatzolah care.
- [medium] missing-section p33 — The RECOGNITION lead-in sentence that frames why AEIOUTIPS is used is missing.  
  _fix:_ Add content.recognition = ['Cause(s) of acute altered conscious state may not always be obvious – in such cases use the following AEIOUTIPS pneumonic to consider some possible differentials'] above the mnemonic table.
- [low] render-risk p33 — Adding the AEIOUTIPS example column as uniform objects would silently degrade the mnemonic to stacked record cards on a 375px phone.  
  _fix:_ Use the explicit { headers, rows } table shape for aeioutips (sticky first column carries the letter) rather than adding a third object field.
- [low] typo p33 — The CPG itself misspells 'mnemonic' as 'pneumonic' — flagged so the app is not silently 'corrected' away from source.  
  _fix:_ Reproduce the CPG wording verbatim if the lead-in is added; do not silently change 'pneumonic' to 'mnemonic' without an editorial decision recorded against v6.2.
- [low] typo p36 — The CPG's first Further Note drops the word 'survey' — 'a thorough and systematic primary should be performed'.  
  _fix:_ When adding the note, reproduce the CPG text verbatim (including the missing 'survey') or record an explicit editorial correction; do not paraphrase.

## `dehydration-p75` — p75-76 · coverage: partial

**Graphics not represented in the app:**
- p75: Green DEFINITION banner block with a single green-asterisk bullet: 'Dehydration refers to a detrimental reduction of the amount of water in the body'.
- p75: Colour-coded perfusion criteria table: dark-green header band (blank / Skin / Pulse / sBP / Conscious State), mid-green row-label column with white text (Adequate / ↓ Adequate / No Perfusion), pale-green body cells, and inline reverse-video black 'OR' badges inside the Pulse and Conscious State cells of the ↓ Adequate row.
- p75: Red/pink highlighted caution strip immediately under the table with a red asterisk icon: 'Pulse and sBP in this table specifically refer to ↑ 15 year olds only'.
- p75: Inline reverse-video black 'IF' badge in the sentence 'The patient is considered to have less than adequate (↓ Adequate) perfusion IF 2 or more of the below criteria are outside of the Adequate criteria' - a logic operator glyph, not plain text.
- p75: Two-column bulleted symptom list where exactly one letter of each of the 8 bullets is bold + underlined (column 1: S, T, A, D; column 2: I, U, M, S) - a typographic mnemonic device that spells STADIUMS when read column-major. The emphasis is lost entirely in a text-layer extraction.
- p75: Two-column bulleted causes list (6 items) under 'Some causes may include'.
- p76: Navy MANAGEMENT block, caution rows: a bold '✗' row ('IV access and IV therapy may only be performed by accredited responders', with 'only' bold+underlined) and a '?' row ('Administer lower doses to patients that are elderly OR have renal OR heart failure'). The '?' caution row has no counterpart anywhere in the app protocol.
- p76: Two grey-header Normal Saline dosing cards, each with a pill icon, an 'IF ↓ Adequate PSA due to Dehydration' indication in the header, a people icon Demographic row carrying inline black '&' and 'OR' logic badges, and rows for Route / Initial Dose / Repeat Dose(s) / Maximum (Maximum row marked with a ⊘ no-entry icon).

**Confirmed (verified) findings:**

### [critical] missing-graphic · p76 — Both Normal Saline dosing cards are absent from the Dehydration protocol - the app gives the IV fluid instruction with no volume, no age limit, no maximum, and no elderly/renal/heart-failure dose split.
- **CPG:** Card 1: 'Normal Saline | IF ↓ Adequate PSA due to Dehydration' - 'Demographic: ↑ 15 years old & Elderly OR Renal OR Heart failure', 'Route: IV', 'Initial Dose: 500 mL | Titrate to response', 'Repeat Dose(s): Nil', 'Maximum: 500 mL'. Card 2: same header - 'Demographic: ↑ 15 years old & NOT elderly / NO renal OR heart failure', 'Route: IV', 'Initial Dose: 1000 mL | Titrate to response', 'Repeat Dose(s): Nil', 'Maximum: 1000 mL'.
- **App:** content.management[2] only: '💊 If ↓Adequate PSA AND unable to tolerate oral → IV access + Normal Saline (accredited only)'. There is no content.dosing key at all. (The two cards do exist under the separate 'normal-saline' medication entry, but a responder inside the Dehydration protocol never sees them.)
- **Fix:** Add a content.dosing array to the dehydration entry (a key named 'dosing' renders as dosing cards): [{ indication: '↓Adequate PSA due to dehydration', demographic: '↑15 years old AND (elderly OR renal OR heart failure)', route: 'IV', initial: '500 mL (titrate to response)', repeat: 'Nil', max: '500 mL' }, { indication: '↓Adequate PSA due to dehydration', demographic: '↑15 years old AND NOT elderly AND NO renal OR heart failure', route: 'IV', initial: '1000 mL (titrate to response)', repeat: 'Nil', max: '1000 mL' }].

### [high] missing-section · p75 — The '2 or more criteria' decision rule that defines ↓Adequate perfusion - the threshold that gates the whole IV-fluid pathway - is missing.
- **CPG:** 'The patient is considered to have less than adequate (↓ Adequate) perfusion IF 2 or more of the below criteria are outside of the Adequate criteria'.
- **App:** content.recognition is a single string: 'Less than adequate perfusion as per Perfusion Status Assessment.' The '2 or more' threshold appears neither here nor in the app's separate perfusion-assessment-fr entry (which holds only the bare table).
- **Fix:** Change content.recognition to an array so it renders as a bullet list, and include the rule verbatim: ['Less than adequate perfusion as per the Perfusion Status Assessment.', 'The patient is considered to have less than adequate (↓Adequate) perfusion IF 2 or more of the below criteria are outside of the Adequate criteria'].

### [high] missing-graphic · p75 — The entire 'Some symptoms may include' list (8 items, carrying a bold+underlined-letter mnemonic) is missing from the app.
- **CPG:** 'Some symptoms may include' - 'Poor Skin turgor', 'Postural Tachycardia & hypotension', 'Altered conscious state / fatigue', 'Postural Dizziness', 'Poor fluid Intake of fluid vs fluid loss', 'Decreased Urination', 'Dry Mouth & tongue', 'Decreased Sweating'. One letter in each bullet is bold + underlined (S, T, A, D down the left column; I, U, M, S down the right), spelling STADIUMS.
- **App:** No symptoms list. content.recognition is one sentence about perfusion only.
- **Fix:** Add the 8 bullets to content.recognition (so they show in the quick view, not only behind the 📖 detail view) with the mnemonic letter capitalised as printed, e.g. 'Poor Skin turgor', 'Postural Tachycardia & hypotension', ... 'Decreased Sweating'. Consider a content.mnemonic entry to preserve the STADIUMS device the bold/underline encodes.

**Medium/low (unverified):**
- [medium] missing-section p75 — The 'Some causes may include' list (6 items) is missing.  
  _fix:_ Add content.causes: ['Hot weather', 'Hyperglycaemia', 'Self-neglect', 'Significant vomiting / sweating', 'Psychostimulant overdose', 'Insufficient social support']. Note this key only surfaces in the detailed (📖) view; fold into recognition if it should appear in the quick view.
- [medium] missing-section p75 — The green DEFINITION block is missing.  
  _fix:_ Add content.definition: 'Dehydration refers to a detrimental reduction of the amount of water in the body'. A key named 'definition' renders green, matching the CPG's green DEFINITION banner.
- [low] colour-coding p75 — The RECOGNITION block is green in the CPG but the app's 'recognition' key renders navy/violet, so the page's green-vs-navy split (assessment vs management) is lost.  
  _fix:_ Either map 'recognition' to the CPG green in the section-colour table, or add the recognition content under a 'definition'-adjacent green key, so assessment content reads green and management reads navy as printed.
- [low] render-risk p76 — The third management bullet compresses three separate CPG rows into one long string, which wraps to 3-4 lines on a 375px phone and demotes the accredited-only restriction to a parenthetical.  
  _fix:_ Split into a management bullet for the condition + action ('If ↓Adequate PSA due to dehydration AND unable to tolerate oral fluids → gain IV access, Normal Saline IV') and move the restriction into content.notes so it renders in the red caution box with the emphasis the CPG's ✗ row gives it.

## `nausea-vomiting-p90` — p90-91 · coverage: partial

**Graphics not represented in the app:**
- p90: Four colour-coded section banner blocks with icons: RECOGNITION (green header + pale-green body, speech-bubble icon), STOP (red header + pale-pink body, flag icon), MANAGEMENT (navy header + pale-lilac body, clipboard icon), FURTHER NOTES (green header + pale-green body, list icon). The colour is the CPG's urgency encoding.
- p90: Short black vertical connector bar drawn between the STOP action row ('Position: Recovery position / laterally') and the MANAGEMENT block — a flowchart connector showing the STOP action feeds forward into MANAGEMENT.
- p90: Black inline 'OR' badge in the RECOGNITION list, marking the second criterion as an alternative to the first.
- p90: Per-row left-margin glyph column: red cross (STOP row), pill (Ondansetron row), telephone handset (Consult/Escalate row), green asterisks (FURTHER NOTES rows), green magnifier (RECOGNITION lead-in).
- p91: Three grey-headed Ondansetron dosing cards, each headed 'Ondansetron' + white-on-black 'ALL' badge + 'Non-tolerated Nausea / Vomiting', with rows Demographic / Route / Initial Dose / Repeat Dose(s) / Maximum. Demographic uses arrow glyphs: card 1 '↑ 11 years old', card 3 '↓ 5 years old'. Card 2 uses plain text '5 to 11 years old'. Card 1's Repeat row carries an inline black 'IF' badge ('IF Required   4 mg @ 20 mins').

**Confirmed (verified) findings:**

### [critical] missing-section · p91 — The entire p91 Ondansetron dosing table (all three age-banded cards) is absent from the protocol — the app tells the responder to give Ondansetron ODT but gives no dose, no age banding, no repeat interval and no maximum.
- **CPG:** Three cards, each headed 'Ondansetron … ALL Non-tolerated Nausea / Vomiting'. Card 1: 'Demographic ↑ 11 years old / Route ODT / Initial Dose 4 mg / Repeat Dose(s) IF Required 4 mg @ 20 mins / Maximum 8 mg (total dose)'. Card 2: 'Demographic 5 to 11 years old / Route ODT / Initial Dose 4 mg / Repeat Dose(s) Nil / Maximum 4 mg (total dose)'. Card 3: 'Demographic ↓ 5 years old / Route ODT / Initial Dose 2 mg / Repeat Dose(s) Nil / Maximum 2 mg (total dose)'.
- **App:** content.management contains only '💊 If alert → Ondansetron ODT'. There is no dosing key on 'nausea-vomiting' at all. (The three doses do exist under the separate 'ondansetron' medication entry in contentData.js, but nothing on the protocol page carries or links to them.)
- **Fix:** Add a `dosing` key to nausea-vomiting content (renders as dosing cards) with the three CPG cards verbatim: [{indication:'Non-tolerated nausea/vomiting', demographic:'↑11 years old', route:'ODT', initial:'4 mg', repeat:'4 mg @ 20 mins if required', max:'8 mg (total dose)'}, {…demographic:'5 to 11 years old', initial:'4 mg', repeat:'Nil', max:'4 mg (total dose)'}, {…demographic:'↓5 years old', initial:'2 mg', repeat:'Nil', max:'2 mg (total dose)'}]. Keep the ↑/↓ arrow glyphs — they are the CPG's own over/under operators. Reproduce the CPG's bands exactly, including that 5 and 11 appear in two bands; do not 'tidy' them.

### [high] missing-section · p90 — The mandatory VVED consult requirement for every patient given ondansetron is missing entirely.
- **CPG:** FURTHER NOTES: 'ALL patients receiving ondansetron must have a VVED consult attended as a minimum if not being transported by ambulance to hospital.' ('ALL' is bolded in the CPG.)
- **App:** No notes key at all. content has only recognition and management.
- **Fix:** Add content.notes = ['ALL patients receiving ondansetron must have a VVED consult attended as a minimum if not being transported by ambulance to hospital.', …]. Because this is a hard obligation attached to giving the drug, consider also surfacing it next to the Ondansetron step, not only at the bottom of the page.

### [high] colour-coding · p90 — The red STOP block is flattened into the ordinary management list, so the app renders the altered-conscious positioning step in navy alongside routine steps instead of as a red critical block.
- **CPG:** A red-headed 'STOP' banner with a pale-pink body and a red cross glyph containing exactly one row: 'IF Altered conscious | Position: Recovery position / laterally'. A black connector bar then leads into the navy MANAGEMENT block.
- **App:** 'If altered conscious → Recovery position/lateral' is item 1 of the plain `management` array, which the renderer colours navy — visually identical to 'If vomiting persists → consult/escalate'.
- **Fix:** Move the STOP row into its own red-rendering key (e.g. `immediateAction: ['IF Altered conscious → Position: Recovery position / laterally']`) and leave `management` with only the two MANAGEMENT rows (Alert → Ondansetron ODT; Vomiting persists → Consult/Escalate care), preserving the CPG's STOP-then-MANAGEMENT order.

**Medium/low (unverified):**
- [medium] missing-section p90 — The FURTHER NOTES instruction to maintain spinal immobilisation when lateralising a potential spinal patient is missing — it is the safety caveat that pairs with the recovery-position step and with the prophylaxis indication.  
  _fix:_ Add to content.notes: 'Maintain spinal immobilisation when placing potential spinal patients in lateral position'.
- [medium] missing-graphic p90 — The CPG's 'Ondansetron, ODT' action cell is specially highlighted as a drug-administration action, but the app renders it as plain prose with no route to the drug's dosing/contraindications.  
  _fix:_ Once `dosing` is added (finding 1) the drug action will be visually distinct as a dosing card. Additionally cross-link the step to the 'ondansetron' medication entry so contraindications and precautions are one tap away.
- [low] wrong-value p90 — The RECOGNITION lead-in sentence and the explicit 'OR' badge between the two criteria are dropped, so the app does not state that either criterion alone is sufficient.  
  _fix:_ Restore the CPG phrasing, e.g. recognition: ['Use this guideline for patients meeting either of the following criteria:', 'Nausea / vomiting that is NOT being tolerated', 'OR Prophylactically for spinally immobilised patients'].
- [low] wrong-value p90 — Management action wording is paraphrased away from the CPG's own text.  
  _fix:_ Use the CPG strings verbatim: 'Position: Recovery position / laterally' and 'Consult/Escalate care'.

## `safety-netting-p27` — p27-30 · coverage: missing
_Full replacement content was extracted by the audit agent._

**Graphics not represented in the app:**
- p27: Red 'SAFETY NETTING' title banner (white bold caps on solid red, framed by double red rules) - the CPG's section-title device.
- p27: Green 'DEFINITION' header bar with a '?' glyph, over a green-tinted body block bulleted with a green asterisk - the CPG's green = definition/framework colour code.
- p28: Navy 'MANAGEMENT' header bar with a clipboard glyph, spanning a three-zone column grid over a 17-row alternating navy-tint checklist table.
- p28: Per-row action icons in the MANAGEMENT table encoding action type: navy '+' (perform/document), navy speech bubble (discuss/explain), navy magnifier (confirm/verify), red telephone (phone escalation). Purely graphical - absent from the PDF text layer.
- p28: Inline inverse-black logic badges 'IF' and '&' used to build two condition->action rows laid out across the column break ('IF ANY abnormal findings found' | 'Discuss them with patient'; 'IF Advised transport & Patient refusing' | 'Consult clinician/COM'). The second of these rows is highlighted on a red tint.
- p28: Red 'ESCALATION OF CARE' header bar with a flag glyph, over a red-tinted body with a red telephone icon and a red asterisk bullet - the CPG's red = urgent/escalate colour code.
- p29: Green 'FURTHER NOTES' header bar with a list glyph, over three notes; note 1 is on a red tint with a red asterisk while notes 2 and 3 are on a green tint with green asterisks - a colour-coded severity split inside one block.
- p30: Full-page landscape three-column colour-coded patient referral infographic. Column headers on tinted strips: 'Medical Emergency' (red) / 'Medical Urgency' (amber) / 'Medical Uncertainty' (green); solid colour service bands: 'Ambulance Victoria' / 'Victorian Virtual Emergency Department' / 'Nurse-On-Call'; contact strips with phone and globe glyphs: '000' / 'www.VVED.org.au' / '1300 60 60 24'; heavy black vertical rules separating the columns; then per-column 'Some examples of when to...' asterisked lists and an italic footnote. This is the document the CPG tells crews to leave with the patient.

**Confirmed (verified) findings:**

### [critical] missing-section · p27 — The entire Safety Netting guideline (CPG p27-30) is absent from the app - there is no 'safety-netting' key in assessmentsContent, so none of the four sections and none of the patient-facing service handout are reachable in the field.
- **CPG:** "SAFETY NETTING" ... "In the prehospital context safety netting refers to providing patients and caregivers with advice and instructions for self-care as well providing an adequate contingency plan in case of unexpected deterioration. This is particularly important when leaving patients at home OR referring them to further care via non Hatzolah / Ambulance Victoria transport. Safety netting is also important for patients that refuse management and / or hospital transport despite Hatzolah advice." Followed by a 17-step MANAGEMENT checklist, an ESCALATION OF CARE block, three FURTHER NOTES, and a full-page three-service referral handout (000 / VVED / Nurse-On-Call).
- **App:** Nothing. grep "safety-netting" over src/data/contentData.js returns no key; the assessments key list runs vital-signs, clinical-flags, clinical-approach-cb/fr, conscious-status-cb/fr, respiratory-assessment-cb/fr, perfusion-assessment-cb/fr, pain-assessment, weight-calculations, ventilation-rates, paediatric-assessment-triangle. The only trace of the concept is two incidental sentences inside other protocols (contentData.js:946 alcohol-intoxication and :965 allergy-mild, both beginning 'Safety netting: ...').
- **Fix:** Add a new entry to assessmentsContent in src/data/contentData.js keyed 'safety-netting' with title: 'Safety Netting', level: 'FR', category: 'primary', summary: 'Advice, contingency planning and referral pathways when leaving a patient at home', and the content object supplied in proposedContent. Level FR because the section sits in the ASSESSMENTS block (pages 4-31); category 'primary' per the CLAUDE.md protocol->category mapping, which already lists Safety Netting under primary.

### [critical] missing-graphic · p30 — The full-page three-column patient handout on p30 (Ambulance Victoria / VVED / Nurse-On-Call, with contact details and 'when to use' example lists) is entirely absent - this is the artefact the CPG instructs the crew to physically leave with the patient.
- **CPG:** p29: "Consider leaving a copy of the following document with the patient alongside verbal advice on how and when to access the 3 services". p30 then presents three colour-coded columns: red "Medical Emergency / Ambulance Victoria / 000", amber "Medical Urgency / Victorian Virtual Emergency Department / www.VVED.org.au", green "Medical Uncertainty / Nurse-On-Call / 1300 60 60 24", each with a bulleted example list and an italic footnote.
- **App:** Nothing. The app has no entry for this section at all, so neither the columns, the phone numbers (000, 1300 60 60 24, 131 450 translator line), the URL www.VVED.org.au, nor any of the 8 + 12 + 5 example bullets exist anywhere in src/.
- **Fix:** Render the handout inside the new protocol as the whenToAccessServices nested section in proposedContent: a 3-row summary table (Level of concern / Service / Contact) followed by three named sub-lists. A future enhancement would give it a dedicated red/amber/green three-card SVG (a _serviceCards underscore key) mirroring the CPG's own colour-coding, since the colour IS the triage signal.

**Medium/low (unverified):**
- [medium] render-risk p30 — The p30 handout is a landscape three-column layout; naively porting it as a 3-column table would overflow a 375px phone because every column holds long text.
