  _fix:_ Use the shape in proposedContent: one short 3-column summary table (Level of concern / Service / Contact - all cells short, safe on 375px) plus three separately-headed nested string arrays (ambulanceVictoria / victorianVirtualEmergencyDepartment / nurseOnCall) that stack vertically. Do not attempt a single wide 3-column table of the example lists.
- [low] typo p29 — The CPG's first FURTHER NOTE contains a mid-sentence capital and a possessive/contraction error; transcribe verbatim rather than silently correcting.  
  _fix:_ Reproduce the CPG wording verbatim in furtherNotes[0] as done in proposedContent. Do not correct to 'its' or lowercase 'educate' without a documented content decision, since the app is meant to mirror the CPG.

## `ventilation-rates-p31` — p31 · coverage: partial

**Graphics not represented in the app:**
- p31: Red banner header 'ASTHMA - UNRESPONSIVE WITH PULSE' with white flag/triangle glyph, above a 3-row pink-tinted block giving the much slower asthma ventilation rates (↑ 11 years 8-12 s / 5-8x per min; 5-11 years 5-6 s / 10-14x per min; 2-4 years 4-5 s / 12-15x per min).

**Confirmed (verified) findings:**

### [critical] missing-section · p31 — The entire second block on p31, 'ASTHMA - UNRESPONSIVE WITH PULSE', is absent from the app — all three asthma-specific ventilation rates are missing.
- **CPG:** Red header block: 'ASTHMA - UNRESPONSIVE WITH PULSE'. Rows: 'IF ↑ 11 years  ventilate once every  8 - 12 seconds  OR 5 - 8x per minute'; 'IF 5 - 11 years  ventilate once every  5 - 6 seconds  OR 10 - 14x per minute'; 'IF 2 - 4 years  ventilate once every  4 - 5 seconds  OR 12 - 15x per minute'.
- **App:** content.table contains only the five INEFFECTIVE BREATHING rows. No asthma rows, no asthma heading, and no reference anywhere in contentData.js (grep for '8 - 12', '5–8x', '10–14x', '12–15x', 'Unresponsive with Pulse' returns nothing).
- **Fix:** Add a second section. Because the app renders a nested object as its own subsection, restructure content as two keys, e.g.: content: { ineffectiveBreathing: { table: { headers: ['Age','Ventilate once every','OR'], rows: [['↑ 15 years','5 seconds','12x per minute'],['12 - 15 years','3 - 4 seconds','14 - 26x per minute'],['5 - 11 years','2 - 3 seconds','16 - 34x per minute'],['1 - 4 years','2 - 3 seconds','20 - 40x per minute'],['↓ 1 year','2 seconds','25 - 55x per minute']] } }, asthmaUnresponsiveWithPulse: { table: { headers: ['Age','Ventilate once every','OR'], rows: [['↑ 11 years','8 - 12 seconds','5 - 8x per minute'],['5 - 11 years','5 - 6 seconds','10 - 14x per minute'],['2 - 4 years','4 - 5 seconds','12 - 15x per minute']] } } }. Transcribe the asthma rows exactly as printed; the CPG gives no asthma row below 2 years.

### [high] missing-section · p31 — The app's single table is unlabelled, so the INEFFECTIVE BREATHING qualifier is lost and its rates read as the universal ventilation rates for any patient.
- **CPG:** The five rates the app carries sit under the red header 'INEFFECTIVE BREATHING'. They are one of two mutually exclusive rate sets on the page; the other is headed 'ASTHMA - UNRESPONSIVE WITH PULSE'.
- **App:** content.table with headers ['Age','Rate','Interval'] and no heading, title or note naming the indication. ProtocolView renders it under the generic humanised key 'Table' inside a protocol titled only 'Ventilation Rates'.
- **Fix:** Label the block 'Ineffective Breathing' (nested key ineffectiveBreathing, which the renderer humanises to 'Ineffective Breathing'), so the two rate sets are visibly distinct once the asthma block is added. Combined with the finding above, a responder currently reads 20-40x/min for a 3-year-old asthmatic where the CPG says 12 - 15x per minute.

**Medium/low (unverified):**
- [medium] colour-coding p31 — Both p31 blocks are red-banner critical blocks in the CPG; the app renders the content as a navy default section with a green table header.  
  _fix:_ Add the two new section keys to SECTION_COLORS with the red treatment ({ title: CPG.red, bg: CPG.redTint, border: '#eb1f2733' }) — the same entry shape already used for dangersAndSafety / immediateAction — so both ventilation blocks read red as on the page.
- [low] wrong-value p31 — The CPG's glyph operator '↑ 15 years' has been transcribed as '≥16 years', a number the CPG never prints, and it contradicts the app's own vital-signs table which renders the adult band as '≥15 years'.  
  _fix:_ Transcribe as printed — '↑ 15 years' (or '>15 years' if the arrow glyph is being normalised) — and apply the same arrow convention consistently across contentData.js. The clinical band boundary is unchanged, but the app should not invent the figure 16.
- [low] table-misrendered p31 — Column order and header wording diverge from the CPG: the page leads with the timing interval, the app leads with the per-minute rate, and the CPG's literal column phrases are replaced by 'Rate' / 'Interval'.  
  _fix:_ Reorder to ['Age','Ventilate once every','OR'] so the seconds interval is the second column, matching the page. The numeric values themselves are all correct: 5 s/12x; 3-4 s/14-26x; 2-3 s/16-34x; 2-3 s/20-40x; 2 s/25-55x.
- [low] not-in-cpg p31 — The summary line says 'BVM ventilation rates by age'; p31 never mentions BVM, and 'by age' asserts that age alone determines the rate, which p31 contradicts.  
  _fix:_ Reword to something indication-aware, e.g. 'Ventilation rates — ineffective breathing and asthma'. (BVM is linked to ventilation rates elsewhere in the app's own text at contentData.js:757 and :825, but it is not stated on p31.)
- [low] render-risk p31 — The protocol is filed under category 'respiratory' although p31 belongs to the CPG's ASSESSMENTS section, which the project maps to 'primary'.  
  _fix:_ Set category to 'primary' to match the CPG section and the Protocol → Category mapping in CLAUDE.md, which lists Ventilation Rates under primary.

## `chest-trauma-p108` — p108 · coverage: missing
_Full replacement content was extracted by the audit agent._

**Graphics not represented in the app:**
- p108: Red "CHEST TRAUMA" title banner with double red rules above and below — the standard TRAUMA-section page title device (same as p104 HEAD TRAUMA), not a criticality marker.
- p108: Navy "MANAGEMENT – CHEST INJURY" table header bar with a white ▶ flag icon at the left.
- p108: Icon-coded management table, 7 rows in alternating lavender bands, with a dedicated left icon column: 📞 phone (SitRep row), ✚ blue cross (positioning rows), pill/capsule icon (2 medication rows), ✗ blue cross-out (2 prohibition rows).
- p108: Inline black condition chips (white-on-black badges): IF ×3, OR ×1, UNLESS ×1.
- p108: Indigo/purple highlighted medication rows with bold white text: "Oxygen, NRB (10-15L O2)" and "Pain Relief, PRN".

**Confirmed (verified) findings:**

### [critical] missing-section · p108 — The entire Chest Trauma protocol (CPG p108) is absent from the app — no key, no tile, no content.
- **CPG:** Page 108 title banner "CHEST TRAUMA" with a single table "MANAGEMENT – CHEST INJURY" containing 7 rows: "SitRep: Hatzolah dispatch + call ambulance"; "IF Spinal precaution OR Poor perfusion → Position: Supine with slight head elevation"; "IF Awake / spontaneously ventilating → Position: Sitting upright"; "Oxygen, NRB (10-15L O2)"; "IF Required → Pain Relief, PRN"; "UNLESS Life threatening bleed → Do NOT cover open chest wounds"; "Do NOT splint chest injury".
- **App:** Nothing. grep for "chest trauma" / "chest-trauma" across src/ returns zero hits; scripts/output/content/ has head-trauma.json, spinal-trauma.json, burns.json, wound-care.json but no chest-trauma.json; contentData.js trauma keys run general-trauma-cb, general-trauma-fr, head-trauma, spinal-trauma, burns, wound-care, falls — Chest Trauma is the only TRAUMA-section protocol with no entry.
- **Fix:** Add a conditionsContent key 'chest-trauma' (level FR, category trauma) between spinal-trauma and burns, using the proposedContent object from this audit.

### [critical] missing-section · p108 — Both safety-critical prohibitions are missing: do NOT cover open chest wounds, and do NOT splint chest injury.
- **CPG:** ✗ "UNLESS Life threatening bleed  Do NOT cover open chest wounds" and ✗ "Do NOT splint chest injury" (both bottom rows, marked with the blue ✗ prohibition icon; NOT and UNLESS are emphasised in the CPG).
- **App:** Absent. A responder searching the app for chest injury finds nothing, and the app never states these negatives anywhere — general-trauma-fr, head-trauma and wound-care contain no equivalent instruction.
- **Fix:** Carry both strings verbatim into a doNot section of the new chest-trauma protocol, preserving the UNLESS condition attached to the open-chest-wound rule.

**Medium/low (unverified):**
- [medium] missing-graphic p108 — Inline black IF / OR / UNLESS condition chips are graphic badges that extract as hyphen-wrapped artifacts.  
  _fix:_ Strip the hyphen artifacts and keep the conditional wording as the first column of the positioning table / the indication field of the dosing entries, so each condition stays bound to its action.
- [medium] colour-coding p108 — The two medication rows are colour-highlighted in the CPG; the app has no chest-trauma entry, so that emphasis is lost entirely.  
  _fix:_ Put Oxygen and Pain Relief under a "dosing" key so the renderer gives them dosing-card styling, matching the CPG's purple highlight. Leave the doNot rows navy unless a dedicated prohibition style is added to the renderer's colour map.
- [low] render-risk p108 — Flattening the conditional positioning rows into a plain bullet list would break the condition → action pairing on a 375px phone.  
  _fix:_ Use the explicit { headers, rows } table form for positioning so the renderer produces a two-column table with a sticky first column and horizontal scroll rather than bullets.

## `respiratory-assessment-cb-p122` — p122-123 · coverage: partial

**Graphics not represented in the app:**
- p122: Green 'RECOGNITION' section banner (speech-bubble icon) heading the severity table, plus the lighter-green column-header strip with magnifier icon. The whole block is colour-coded green (recognition/definition).

### [critical] wrong-value · p122 — The entire Speech row of the severity table is shifted one column to the left in the app, so every speech finding is graded one severity level too low.
- **CPG:** Speech | Normal: 'Clear, Steady' | Mild: 'Full Sentences' | Moderate: 'Short Sentences' | Severe: 'Words -OR- None'
- **App:** "Speech", "Clear, full sentences", "Short sentences", "Words only", "None" - i.e. Normal='Clear, full sentences', Mild='Short sentences', Moderate='Words only', Severe='None'
- **Fix:** Replace the Speech row with ["Speech", "Clear, Steady", "Full Sentences", "Short Sentences", "Words -OR- None"].

### [high] wrong-value · p122 — The Severe cell of Work of Breathing drops 'fast breathing', losing one of the three CPG severe signs.
- **CPG:** Work of Breathing | Severe: 'Marked increase, fast breathing, Tripod position'
- **App:** "Marked increase, tripod position"
- **Fix:** Set the Severe cell to "Marked increase, fast breathing, Tripod position".

**Medium/low (unverified):**
- [medium] table-misrendered p123 — The 'Signs of low oxygen in children' two-column table is stored as a nested object, so the app renders subsections titled 'Infants' and 'Children' and the CPG's age ranges are lost entirely.  
  _fix:_ Replace with a table: { headers: ["Infants (0 - 12 months)", "Children (1 - 15 years)"], rows: [["Lethargy","Restlessness"],["Floppy","Fast breathing"],["Confusion","Cyanosis (Blue skin)"]] } so the age bands render as column headers.
- [medium] render-risk p122 — The bold 'NO' that governs the entire Severe sounds cell is flattened to lower-case 'No', so the silent-chest sign reads as a mere absence of findings rather than the CPG's emphasised critical sign.  
  _fix:_ Restore the CPG capitalisation "NO Cough, Wheeze, Stridor" (or otherwise emphasise NO) so the absence of sounds is not mistaken for a reassuring finding.
- [low] missing-section p122 — The CPG's 'RECOGNITION' block label above the severity table is not present in the app; the table renders under the generic key 'table'.  
  _fix:_ Wrap the severity table under a 'recognition' key (e.g. content.recognition = { table: {...} }) so the section carries its CPG label.
- [low] colour-coding p123 — Both p123 blocks are green (recognition/definition) in the CPG, but the app's keys paediatricDistressSigns and paediatricLowO2Signs fall through to navy section colouring.  
  _fix:_ Nest these under a green-coloured recognition/definition section, or add the two keys to the green section-colour list, so p123 matches the CPG's green coding.
- [low] wrong-value p122 — Work of Breathing Mild and Moderate cells add the word 'increase' that the CPG does not use.  
  _fix:_ Use the CPG wording "Slight" and "Marked".
- [low] wrong-value p122 — The Skin Severe cell drops the CPG's parenthetical '(blue)' explanation of cyanosis.  
  _fix:_ Set the cell to "Pale, Sweaty +/- Cyanosis (blue)".
- [low] typo p123 — The CPG itself misspells 'RESPIRATORY' as 'REPSIRATORY' in the first p123 banner; the app does not reproduce the heading at all, so the wording (including the source typo) is not carried through.  
  _fix:_ No clinical change needed; if heading fidelity is wanted, label the section 'Signs of Respiratory Distress in Children' and record that the source PDF has the typo 'REPSIRATORY' so it is not re-flagged later.
- [low] render-risk p122 — The 5-column severity table with multi-word cells (up to 'Marked increase, fast breathing, Tripod position') will need horizontal scrolling on a 375px phone.  
  _fix:_ Keep the table (it matches the CPG) but verify the sticky first column plus horizontal scroll is usable at 375px; consider abbreviating nothing in the data and instead tightening cell padding in the renderer.

## `asthma-cb-p130` — p130-133 · coverage: partial

**Graphics not represented in the app:**
- p130: Green DEFINITION block: asterisk-bulleted definition paragraph plus a two-column grid of six trigger factors (Allergens / Exercise / Respiratory infections / Weather / Medications / Irritants)
- p131: Navy age-band banner '2 YEARS OLD to ADULT' with double rules, scoping the whole algorithm on p131
- p131: Red STOP block: magnifier + [IF] Patient becomes unresponsive [&] Ineffective breathing, then a plus glyph and red bold 'Manage as Cardiac Arrest!'
- p131: Vertical black connector bars linking the four blocks into a top-to-bottom algorithm: STOP -> MANAGEMENT -> THUNDERSTORM ASTHMA -> ESCALATION OF CARE
- p131: Red THUNDERSTORM ASTHMA block header and red ESCALATION OF CARE block header (danger colour-coding distinct from the navy MANAGEMENT block)
- p132: Photograph of a blue pMDI reliever puffer in the Salbutamol Route row
- p132: Grey-headed Adrenaline dosing table for >=6 years old & up-arrow 20 kgs (0.3 mg, IM, Epi-Pen, Repeat/Max = Consult)
- p132: Photograph of a yellow adult Epi-Pen auto-injector in the 0.3 mg Initial Dose row
- p132: Grey-headed Adrenaline dosing table for <6 years old OR down-arrow 20 kgs (0.15 mg, IM, Epi-Pen Jr, Repeat/Max = Consult)
- p132: Photograph of a green Epi-Pen Jr auto-injector in the 0.15 mg Initial Dose row (colour is the field cue for picking the right device)

**Confirmed (verified) findings:**

### [critical] missing-section · p131 — The red STOP block ('Manage as Cardiac Arrest!') is completely absent from the app
- **CPG:** STOP — 'IF Patient becomes unresponsive & Ineffective breathing' → '+ Manage as Cardiac Arrest!' (red block, red bold text, sits FIRST, above MANAGEMENT)
- **App:** Nothing. content.recognition and content.management contain no mention of unresponsiveness, ineffective breathing, or cardiac arrest. The app's asthma-cb protocol gives a responder no instruction for the deteriorating-to-arrest patient.
- **Fix:** Add a red-coloured key that the renderer tints red, e.g. content.immediateAction = ['STOP — IF patient becomes unresponsive AND ineffective breathing → Manage as Cardiac Arrest'], placed before management. Per the renderer, 'immediateAction' is one of the keys coloured red.

### [high] wrong-value · p133 — Further Note 1 flips the CPG's OR to AND and drops the sentence 'All instances of Asthma must be escalated.'
- **CPG:** 'If 000 had NOT been called OR ambulance is NOT attending for mild asthma the VVED must be used as a minimum. All instances of Asthma must be escalated.'
- **App:** 'If 000 NOT called and ambulance NOT attending for mild asthma — VVED minimum'. The 'and' narrows the trigger so a responder who called 000 but has no ambulance attending reads the VVED requirement as not applying, and the blanket escalation rule is absent.
- **Fix:** Restore the CPG wording verbatim: 'If 000 had NOT been called OR ambulance is NOT attending for mild asthma the VVED must be used as a minimum. All instances of Asthma must be escalated.'

**Medium/low (unverified):**
- [medium] colour-coding p131 — THUNDERSTORM ASTHMA and ESCALATION OF CARE are red (danger) blocks in the CPG but render as ordinary navy management bullets in the app  
  _fix:_ Split into separate keys so the renderer's colour rules apply: keep routine steps in `management`, move the thunderstorm-asthma pathway and the escalation criterion into red-coloured keys (e.g. `immediateAction`, and add `escalation`), mirroring the CPG's block structure.
- [medium] wrong-value p132 — Salbutamol demographic is stored as the mis-extracted glyph string '↑2 years' instead of the CPG's '≥ 2 years old'  
  _fix:_ Set the value to '≥ 2 years old' (and see the render-risk finding — the key must also be renamed to `demographic` before it displays at all).
- [medium] missing-graphic p131 — The p131 four-block algorithm (STOP → MANAGEMENT → THUNDERSTORM ASTHMA → ESCALATION OF CARE joined by connector bars) is not represented as a flow anywhere  
  _fix:_ Either add a `_flowchart` SVG for asthma-cb mirroring the four blocks, or at minimum restructure the content into the four CPG-named sections so the block order and colours survive.
- [medium] missing-section p132 — The Salbutamol dosing table's indication qualifier 'Mild / moderate asthma' is missing  
  _fix:_ Add indication: 'Mild / moderate asthma' to the salbutamol dosing record.
- [low] colour-coding p133 — FURTHER NOTES is a green (informational) block in the CPG but the app renders 'notes' as a red-tinted danger section in the quick view  
  _fix:_ Render `notes` in the CPG's informational green (or neutral) rather than red danger styling, reserving red for the STOP / thunderstorm / escalation blocks.
- [low] typo p130 — Protocol title drops the word 'HISTORY'  
  _fix:_ Retitle to 'Asthma History / Wheeze (L1)' to match the CPG heading.
- [low] wrong-value p131 — Thunderstorm declaration authority abbreviated, losing 'Hatzolah'  
  _fix:_ Use the CPG's full wording: 'declared by Hatzolah Clinical Operations Manager'.
- [low] missing-graphic p132 — The three device photographs (blue pMDI puffer, yellow Epi-Pen, green Epi-Pen Jr) are not represented  
  _fix:_ At minimum carry the device names and their colours in text ('Epi-Pen (yellow)', 'Epi-Pen Jr (green)') on the adrenaline dosing records.
- [low] missing-section p131 — The '2 YEARS OLD to ADULT' age-band banner scoping the whole algorithm is not carried  
  _fix:_ Label the management section with the CPG's band, e.g. a leading item or an `ageBand: '2 years old to adult'` field.

## `hypoglycaemia-p83` — p83-85 · coverage: partial

**Graphics not represented in the app:**
- p83: Green DEFINITION block: green asterisk lead-ins with two two-column nested bullet grids (5 causes; 8 symptoms). The two-column layout means text-layer extraction interleaves left/right columns.
- p83: Navy MANAGEMENT block rendered as a colour-coded conditional algorithm: each row is condition-cell(s) + action-cell. Inverted black logic badges IF / & / OR / ONCE join the conditions; the first row ('Not responding' -> 'Call for Ambulance / 000') is red-tinted with a red phone icon; the two drug rows carry purple/lavender action cells ('Glucose Paste, Oral', 'Glucagon, IM') with a pill icon; two black cross (X) prohibition rows sit under the drug rows; indented sub-rows carry the AEIOUTIPS branch and the carbohydrate examples.
- p83: Short vertical black connector bar centred at the foot of p83, carrying the algorithm flow onto p84.
- p84: Red ESCALATION OF CARE block: red header bar, peach row with red phone icon - 'IF No improvement | Call for Ambulance / 000' - plus a black connector bar beneath it.
- p84: Navy REFER TO GP block: 10-item criteria checklist with an inverted black 'ALL' badge in the intro line, and a nested sub-list of 4 black-cross (X) exclusion risks under the 'There are NONE of the following risks' item.
- p85: Three grey-header medication dosing tables (Glucose Paste; Glucagon >= 8 years; Glucagon < 8 years). Each header carries the condition 'IF BGL [down-arrow] 4'; each Demographic row is three cells joined by inverted '&' and 'OR' badges; rows are Demographic / Route / Initial Dose / Repeat Dose(s) / Maximum with people, arrow and no-entry icons.
- p85: Green FURTHER NOTES block: a green warning-triangle row (scene safety) plus two green-asterisk items, one with a nested 3-item bullet list of BGL-measurement indications.

**Confirmed (verified) findings:**

### [high] missing-section · p84 — The entire red ESCALATION OF CARE block is missing from the app.
- **CPG:** ESCALATION OF CARE (red header): 'IF No improvement | Call for Ambulance / 000' (red phone icon, peach row).
- **App:** No escalation key at all. content has only definition, symptoms, management, referToGP.
- **Fix:** Add `immediateAction` (renders red) or an `escalation` entry containing 'IF no improvement → Call for Ambulance / 000', placed after management and before referToGP.

### [high] missing-section · p85 — The entire green FURTHER NOTES block is missing - including the scene-safety aggression warning and the three indications for measuring BGL.
- **CPG:** FURTHER NOTES: 'Severe hypoglycaemia may cause some patients to act aggressively, consider scene safety'; 'BGL should be measured in patients with any of the following' – 'Altered consciousness', 'History of diabetes', 'Undifferentiated acute medical illness'; 'The patient may become hypoglycaemic once more if the underlying cause is not addressed'.
- **App:** Nothing. No notes/furtherNotes key exists.
- **Fix:** Add a `notes` array with the scene-safety warning, the recurrence warning, and a nested `measureBglIn` list (Altered consciousness / History of diabetes / Undifferentiated acute medical illness).

### [high] missing-section · p83 — The AEIOUTIPS branch after the BGL recheck is missing - the app never tells the responder what to do if symptoms persist despite a corrected BGL.
- **CPG:** Under 'Recheck BGL: After 5 minutes': '▸ IF Symptoms continue & BGL [up-arrow] 4 | Consider other causes (AEIOUTIPS)'. The up-arrow is this CPG's greater-than operator, i.e. BGL > 4.
- **App:** 'Recheck BGL after 5 minutes' - and nothing else. No AEIOUTIPS, no persistent-symptoms branch.
- **Fix:** Append to management (or make the recheck a nested branch): 'IF symptoms continue AND BGL > 4 → consider other causes (AEIOUTIPS)'. AEIOUTIPS is already defined in the app's altered-consciousness mnemonic, so cross-reference it.

### [high] missing-section · p83 — Both black-cross prohibition warnings in MANAGEMENT are lost; most importantly the ban on oral medication in altered conscious state.
- **CPG:** '✖ Never administer oral medications to patients that are in an altered conscious state' (directly under the Glucose Paste row) and '✖ Only accredited responders may administer Glucagon' (under the Glucagon row).
- **App:** The Glucose Paste line is just '💊 If responding AND can swallow → Glucose Paste 15 g oral' with no prohibition. The Glucagon warning is compressed to a parenthetical '(accredited only)'.
- **Fix:** Add both prohibitions as their own lines under the respective drug steps, keeping the CPG's absolute wording 'Never administer oral medications to patients that are in an altered conscious state' and 'Only accredited responders may administer Glucagon'.

**Medium/low (unverified):**
- [medium] missing-section p83 — The green DEFINITION block is not represented: the definition statement and all five causes are missing, and the `definition` key instead holds the RECOGNITION threshold.  
  _fix:_ Set definition to the CPG statement plus a `causes` array of the five verbatim causes, and move 'Use this guideline if BGL < 4 mmol/L' into a `recognition` key so definition and recognition are not conflated.
- [medium] colour-coding p83 — The red-highlighted 'Not responding → Call for Ambulance / 000' management row renders as an ordinary navy bullet with no urgency marking.  
  _fix:_ Promote this step to an `immediateAction` key (which the renderer colours red) or split it out so the ambulance call is visually urgent, matching the CPG's red treatment.
- [low] missing-section p83 — The worked examples of long-lasting carbohydrates are dropped from the final management step.  
  _fix:_ Append the CPG's examples: '... within 20 minutes – e.g. sandwich, fruit, glass of milk'.
- [low] wrong-value p83 — Two symptom strings are truncated relative to the CPG wording.  
  _fix:_ Restore 'Slurred speech / difficulty speaking' and label the section 'Some symptoms MAY include' to preserve the CPG's hedge.
- [low] colour-coding p83 — Symptoms are rendered navy although the CPG prints them inside the green DEFINITION block.  
  _fix:_ Nest symptoms (and causes) under the `definition` key so they inherit the CPG's green DEFINITION colouring, or extend the green key list to cover `symptoms`.

## `anaphylaxis-fr-p43` — p43-47 · coverage: partial

**Graphics not represented in the app:**
- p43: Inline black logic chips forming the diagnostic algorithm: IF / & / REGARDLESS / OR badges linking the sudden-onset criterion, the 2-of-4 R.A.S.H criterion and the two isolated-symptom + known-antigen criteria
- p44: Red-headed STOP block (red header bar, pink body) — the deteriorating-patient algorithm: unresponsive & ventilating inadequately → supine → ventilate with BVM → SitRep
- p44: Ventilation-rate table inside the STOP block: 5 age-band rows (>15 y, 12–15 y, 5–11 y, 1–4 y, <1 y) x columns 'ventilate every N seconds' and 'OR Nx per minute', headed by a purple 'Oxygen, BVM (8-15L O2)' medication chip
- p47: Grey-headed Salbutamol dosing card 1 — 'IF Anaphylaxis & Wheezing', ≥16 years, nebulised 10 mg (2 ampules) with 8L O2, repeat 5 mg @ 5 min, Maximum Nil
- p47: Grey-headed Salbutamol dosing card 2 — 'IF Anaphylaxis & Wheezing', 6–15 years, nebulised 5 mg (1 ampule) with 8L O2, repeat 5 mg @ 20 min, Maximum Nil
- p47: Grey-headed Salbutamol dosing card 3 — 'IF Anaphylaxis & Wheezing', 2–5 years, nebulised 2.5 mg (1/2 ampule) with 8L O2, repeat 2.5 mg @ 20 min, Maximum Nil

**Confirmed (verified) findings:**

### [high] colour-coding · p44 — The CPG's red STOP block is folded into the navy MANAGEMENT list, so the app shows no urgent/critical styling for the deteriorating-patient pathway.
- **CPG:** p44 top block is a red-headed section titled 'STOP' with a red/pink body, visually separated from and printed above the navy 'MANAGEMENT' block.
- **App:** The content has no red-rendering key (no immediateAction / dangersAndSafety / redFlags / primarySurvey / rapidAssessment). The STOP content appears as bullet 1 of content.management, which the renderer colours navy.
- **Fix:** Move the STOP content into a key the renderer colours red — 'immediateAction' — so it renders as a red block above Management, matching the CPG.

### [high] missing-section · p43 — The gating recognition criterion 'Sudden onset of symptoms' and its time window are missing — the app presents '≥2 R.A.S.H. categories' as sufficient on its own.
- **CPG:** RECOGNITION: 'IF Sudden onset of symptoms (usually within 30 minutes but may be up to 4 hours)' '& Symptoms from 2 or more of the following R.A.S.H categories'. The two are joined by an AND chip.
- **App:** content.recognition.trigger = "≥2 R.A.S.H. categories (regardless of known antigen)\nOR: Isolated hypotension (sBP <90) + known antigen\nOR: Isolated respiratory distress + known antigen" — no mention of sudden onset or the 30 minutes / up to 4 hours window.
- **Fix:** Rewrite trigger as an array: ['Sudden onset of symptoms (usually within 30 minutes but may be up to 4 hours) AND symptoms from 2 or more R.A.S.H categories — regardless of whether there has been exposure to a known antigen','OR isolated hypotension (sBP <90) AND exposure to a KNOWN antigen','OR isolated respiratory distress AND exposure to a KNOWN antigen'].

### [high] missing-section · p45 — The Further Notes definition of 'known antigen' is missing, yet two of the three recognition criteria hinge on that exact term.
- **CPG:** FURTHER NOTES bullet 2: "For the purposes of this guideline a 'known antigen' refers to any substance that has previously caused an anaphylactic reaction in this patient".
- **App:** content.notes has 4 bullets; this one is absent. content.recognition.trigger uses the phrase 'known antigen' twice with no definition anywhere in the protocol.
- **Fix:** Add that sentence verbatim to content.notes.

**Medium/low (unverified):**
- [medium] missing-section p44 — The condition that triggers the repeat Adrenaline dose ('worsens OR no change @ 5 mins') is dropped — the app states the repeat rule with no trigger.  
  _fix:_ Change the management bullet to 'If worsens OR no change @ 5 mins → repeat initial Adrenaline dose ONCE only; consult clinician regarding further doses', and set dosing[*].repeat to 'Can repeat initial dose ONCE only at 5/60 if worsens or no change'.
- [medium] missing-section p43 — Two of the five example-antigen categories are missing: exercise-induced and idiopathic.  
  _fix:_ Convert examples to an array of the five CPG bullets verbatim, including 'Exercise-induced (typically affecting young adults - rare)' and 'Idiopathic (I.E. no known trigger - rare)'.
- [medium] render-risk p43 — recognition.trigger is a single string with embedded \n characters; HTML collapses newlines, so the three alternative criteria will run together as one paragraph on a 375px phone.  
  _fix:_ Make trigger an array of three strings so the renderer emits a bullet list; do not rely on \n inside a string.
- [medium] missing-section p44 — The BVM oxygen flow rate (8-15L O2) and the 'SitRep: Hatzolah dispatch' escalation step are lost.  
  _fix:_ Restore 'Ventilate — Oxygen, BVM (8-15L O2)' and 'SitRep: Hatzolah dispatch + call ambulance' as separate steps in the STOP/immediateAction section.
- [low] wrong-value p43 — Antigen examples are abbreviated in ways that drop listed items: 'jumping jacks ants' → 'ants', 'tree nuts' → 'nuts', 'fish / shellfish' → 'fish' (shellfish dropped), 'sesame seeds' → 'sesame'.  
  _fix:_ Reproduce the CPG wording verbatim, including 'jumping jacks ants', 'tree nuts', 'shellfish', 'dairy products' and 'sesame seeds'.
- [low] wrong-value p43 — The definition is truncated — the qualifier 'that is normally harmless' is dropped from the end.  
  _fix:_ Append 'that is normally harmless' to content.definition.
- [low] wrong-value p44 — The CPG's ELSE IF precedence between the two positioning rules is flattened into two independent bullets, and 'unresponsive' is restated as 'unconscious'.  
  _fix:_ Write the second as 'ELSE if breathing difficulties → Position: Sitting / tripoding' to preserve precedence, and use the CPG's word 'unresponsive'.
- [low] render-risk p46 — The dosing entries use the opaque key 'demo' and omit the CPG's Route and Maximum rows, so the dosing card renders with fewer labelled fields than the printed card.  
  _fix:_ Restructure each dosing entry as {demographic, route, initialDose, repeat, maximum} so the dosing-card renderer produces the same labelled rows as the CPG.

## `perfusion-assessment-cb-p124` — p124 · coverage: partial

**Graphics not represented in the app:**
- p124: MANAGEMENT row action icons in the left gutter: blue ✕ (avoid/do-not), blue ✚ plus (do/perform), blue telephone handset (call/notify). Each icon classifies the action type of its row.
- p124: Section header icons: '?' (Definition), speech bubble (Recognition), clipboard (Management) — the CPG's visual section-type key.

**Confirmed (verified) findings:**

### [high] wrong-value · p124 — MANAGEMENT positioning instruction loses 'legs raised' — the app says only 'Position flat'.
- **CPG:** MANAGEMENT row 2: '✚ [IF] ↓ Adequate perfusion — Position: Supine / legs raised'
- **App:** content.management: "If ↓ Adequate perfusion: Avoid standing the patient. Position flat. Call ambulance."
- **Fix:** Replace with the CPG wording 'Position: Supine / legs raised'. Supine alone is not the CPG instruction — raising the legs is the perfusion-supporting half of it.

### [high] wrong-value · p124 — MANAGEMENT escalation drops the Hatzolah dispatch SitRep; the app says only 'Call ambulance'.
- **CPG:** MANAGEMENT row 3: '📞 [IF] ↓ Adequate perfusion — SitRep: Hatzolah dispatch + call ambulance'
- **App:** content.management: "... Call ambulance."
- **Fix:** Restore the full CPG action: 'SitRep: Hatzolah dispatch + call ambulance'. For a Level 1 (CB) responder, notifying Hatzolah dispatch is a mandated escalation step and is currently absent from the app.

### [high] missing-section · p124 — The RECOGNITION decision rule that tells the responder how to read the criteria table is entirely missing.
- **CPG:** RECOGNITION: 'The patient is considered to have less than adequate (↓ Adequate) perfusion [IF] 1 or more of the below criteria are outside of the Adequate criteria'
- **App:** No recognition text at all — content jumps straight from `definition` to the bare `table`.
- **Fix:** Add a `recognition` key (string or single-item array) with the CPG sentence verbatim, rendered above the table, so the '1 or more criteria' rule is visible.

**Medium/low (unverified):**
- [medium] wrong-value p124 — DEFINITION is truncated — the second half of the CPG's first bullet is cut off.  
  _fix:_ Restore the full sentence including 'to meet current requirements to sustain life - while also removing carbon dioxide and other waste products'.
- [medium] missing-section p124 — The second DEFINITION bullet — what the Perfusion Status Assessment tells you — is absent from the app.  
  _fix:_ Make `definition` an array of two strings so both CPG bullets render as separate bullet points, matching the CPG's two-row green definition block.
- [medium] wrong-value p124 — MANAGEMENT 'avoid' row drops 'walking' — the app forbids standing only.  
  _fix:_ Use the CPG wording 'Avoid: Standing / walking patient' — walking the patient is separately prohibited.
- [medium] render-risk p124 — `management` is a single prose string, so three discrete CPG action rows collapse into one paragraph with no per-action structure or icons.  
  _fix:_ Convert to an array of three strings (or `{headers:['If','Action'], rows:[...]}`), one per CPG row, preserving the 'Avoid:', 'Position:', 'SitRep:' labels. On a 375px phone the current single string wraps into an undifferentiated block where the three separate actions are easy to miss.
- [low] colour-coding p124 — The RECOGNITION block is green in the CPG but renders navy in the app because the criteria table sits under a generic `table` key.  
  _fix:_ Nest the criteria table under a `recognition` key and extend the green-section list to include `recognition`, so the app's colour matches the CPG's own colour-coding.
- [low] typo p124 — The app silently corrects the CPG's wording 'the bodies tissues' to 'the body's tissues'.  
  _fix:_ Low impact, but per the verbatim-source rule the app should reproduce the CPG's text as printed; flag the typo upstream rather than silently editing it.
- [low] wrong-value p124 — Category is 'cardiac', but this is an assessment page and the project's own mapping puts Perfusion Assessment under 'primary'.  
  _fix:_ Change to "primary" to match CATEGORY_COLORS mapping in CLAUDE.md ('primary: ... Perfusion Assessment ...') and to sit with the sibling L1 assessment protocols.

## `airway-obstruction-cb-p126` — p126 · coverage: partial

**Graphics not represented in the app:**
- p126: Navy 'BOX A' algorithm box, header-right condition chips 'IF Conscious & Ineffective Cough', containing five glyph-prefixed rows: '+ Back blows, up to 5'; '+ IF Still obstructed → Chest thrusts, up to 5'; '🔍 Monitor for clearance / deterioration'; '+ IF Still obstructed → Repeat back blows + chest thrusts, as above'; '+ IF Paediatric & Safe to do so → Use gravity to assist with manoeuvres'.
- p126: Green 'FURTHER NOTES' block with two asterisk-glyph rows (bite-reflex warning; VVED requirement after recovery when ambulance is not attending).
- p126: Conditional-logic glyph system used throughout the page: black inverse 'IF' and '&' badge chips joining a condition pair to a destination/action, plus row-action glyphs (✗ = prohibition, + = do this, 🔍 = monitor, 📞 = call, ✱ = note, ▶ flag = red section header, 📋 clipboard = box header).

**Confirmed (verified) findings:**

### [high] missing-section · p126 — The entire green FURTHER NOTES block is absent from the app, including the bite-reflex safety prohibition and the VVED follow-up requirement.
- **CPG:** FURTHER NOTES — '✱ Do NOT place fingers in patient's mouth as this may trigger a bite reflex' and '✱ If the patient recovers from basic first aid and ambulance is not attending, a minimum of a VVED must occur'.
- **App:** content has only three keys: critical, flowchart, escalation. There is no notes / furtherNotes key, so neither statement appears anywhere in the protocol.
- **Fix:** Add "furtherNotes": ["Do NOT place fingers in patient's mouth as this may trigger a bite reflex", "If the patient recovers from basic first aid and ambulance is not attending, a minimum of a VVED must occur"] to content (CPG prints this block green, i.e. notes-toned).

### [high] wrong-value · p126 — Box A is stated as fixed counts of 5 and 5; the CPG says 'up to 5' for each manoeuvre and gates the chest thrusts behind 'IF Still obstructed'.
- **CPG:** BOX A: '+ Back blows, up to 5' then '+ IF Still obstructed | Chest thrusts, up to 5'.
- **App:** flowchart[1].action = "5 back blows, then 5 chest thrusts. Repeat until clear. Use gravity for paediatric patients if safe." — the 'up to' qualifier is dropped from both manoeuvres and the 'IF Still obstructed' gate before chest thrusts is dropped.
- **Fix:** Restore the CPG's stepwise wording, e.g. steps: ["Back blows, up to 5", "If still obstructed: chest thrusts, up to 5", "Monitor for clearance / deterioration", "If still obstructed: repeat back blows + chest thrusts, as above", "If paediatric and safe to do so: use gravity to assist with manoeuvres"].

### [high] colour-coding · p126 — The red STOP block — the newborn contraindication and the cardiac-arrest triage row — renders navy in the app, with no red/urgent styling.
- **CPG:** A full-width red 'STOP' header bar over a red-tinted block; '✗ This guideline is NOT to be used on newborns'; and the destination 'Manage as Cardiac Arrest!' set in red bold with an exclamation mark.
- **App:** content keys are 'critical' and 'flowchart'. SECTION_COLORS in src/components/ProtocolView.jsx only red-codes dangersAndSafety, rapidAssessment, primarySurvey, redFlags and immediateAction; every other key falls through to the navy default, so both the contraindication and the triage rows render navy on white. The app also drops the exclamation mark from 'Manage as Cardiac Arrest'.
- **Fix:** Rename 'critical' to a red-coded key (e.g. 'immediateAction'), or add 'critical' to SECTION_COLORS with { title: CPG.red, bg: CPG.redTint }; keep the three STOP triage rows inside that same red section and restore 'Manage as Cardiac Arrest!' with its exclamation mark.

**Medium/low (unverified):**
- [medium] missing-section p126 — Box A's 'Monitor for clearance / deterioration' step is missing — the app shows monitoring only under Box B.  
  _fix:_ Insert "Monitor for clearance / deterioration" into the Box A step sequence, between the chest thrusts step and the repeat step, exactly as the CPG orders it.
- [medium] colour-coding p126 — ESCALATION OF CARE is a red CPG block but the app's 'escalation' key renders navy, so immediate escalation is not visually flagged as urgent.  
  _fix:_ Add escalation: { title: CPG.red, bg: CPG.redTint, border: '#eb1f2733' } to SECTION_COLORS in src/components/ProtocolView.jsx — the CPG prints escalation-of-care banners red; check the other protocols' escalation sections at the same time since the change is global.
- [low] render-risk p126 — The three triage rows degrade to stacked 'Condition / Action' record cards on a 375px phone instead of keeping the CPG's aligned IF | & | THEN columns.  
  _fix:_ Express the triage as a table — { headers: ["If", "And", "Then"], rows: [["Unconscious", "Ineffective breathing", "Manage as Cardiac Arrest!"], ["Conscious", "Ineffective cough", "Manage as per Box A"], ["Conscious", "Effective cough", "Manage as per Box B"]] } — which keeps the columns short enough to render as a real table on a narrow screen.

**Confirmed (verified) findings:**

### [high] missing-section · p41 — The Safety Netting bullet is truncated: 'responsible third party' became 'responsible adult', and 'call ambulance if patient deteriorates (e.g. develops SOB, ALOC, GIT upset)' is dropped entirely.
- **CPG:** MANAGEMENT → Safety Netting → 'Leave in care of a responsible third party to observe with advice to seek help/call ambulance if patient deteriorates (e.g. develops SOB, ALOC, GIT upset)'.
- **App:** 'Safety netting: leave in care of responsible adult with advice to seek help if worsening'
- **Fix:** Replace with the CPG wording: 'Safety netting: leave in care of a responsible third party to observe, with advice to seek help/call ambulance if patient deteriorates (e.g. develops SOB, ALOC, GIT upset)'.

### [high] missing-section · p41 — The second Safety Netting sub-bullet — GP follow-up advice — is missing from the app entirely.
- **CPG:** MANAGEMENT → Safety Netting → second ▸ sub-bullet: 'Advise patient to follow up with their GP regarding incident'.
- **App:** No GP follow-up item anywhere in content.management (only one safety-netting line is present).
- **Fix:** Add a management item 'Advise patient to follow up with their GP regarding incident' after the safety-netting line (ideally as a nested safetyNetting array holding both CPG sub-bullets).

**Medium/low (unverified):**
- [medium] missing-graphic p41 — The STOP decision gate and its connector line into MANAGEMENT are not represented, so the app reads the RASH check as management step 1 rather than a stop-gate that must be cleared before any management.  
  _fix:_ Represent the gate explicitly — either as the red immediateAction section above management (see the colour-coding finding), or as a decision object with branches (e.g. { branches: [{ condition: 'Meets RASH criteria', action: 'Mx as per Anaphylaxis / Call ambulance' }, { condition: 'Does not meet RASH criteria', action: 'Continue to Management' }] }) so the renderer emits tappable decision chips.
- [low] colour-coding p41 — The CPG's RECOGNITION band is green, but the app's 'recognition' key falls through to navy.  
  _fix:_ Add 'recognition' to the green section-colour list in the protocol renderer so it matches the CPG's green RECOGNITION band.
- [low] typo p41 — The CPG bolds 'OR' in two recognition rows to mark either sign as sufficient; the app lowercases it, losing the emphasis.  
  _fix:_ Restore the CPG's capitalised operator: 'Hives OR welts', 'Itchy OR swollen eyes'.

## `clinical-approach-fr-p10` — p10-11 · coverage: partial

**Graphics not represented in the app:**
- p10: Highlighted medication row inside RESPONDER ACTION: a darker purple band with pill icon, 'IF Required' on the left and bold white 'Oxygen' on the right — visually distinct from the plain action rows around it.
- p10: Left-hand icon glyph column encoding row semantics: magnifier = assess/look, telephone = call, plus = treatment, speech bubble = communicate/establish, warning triangle = caution, pill = medication, clipboard = section header.
- p11: Two red/pink-tinted rows sitting inside the otherwise navy PAUSE & PLAN block ('Call 000 for ALL patients…' and 'IF Further clinical advice required'), marking them as escalation-critical.
- p11: Ambulance icon on the 'Transport' row of TREATMENT & AV ATTENDANCE, distinguishing transport from clinical actions.

**Confirmed (verified) findings:**

### [high] table-misrendered · p11 — 'Other assessments' is placed in the 'Ask About' column instead of 'Assessment Tools', corrupting the SAMPLE mnemonic and dropping the 8th assessment tool.
- **CPG:** ASSESS table — 'Ask About' column has exactly six items with underlined initials: Situation, signs + symptoms / Allergies / Medications / Past medical history / Last well (onset time) / Extra information. The 'Assessment Tools' column has eight items: PSA / RSA / GCS / Medical time critical / Trauma time critical / Trauma 2° survey / DOLOR / AEIOUTIPS / Other assessments.
- **App:** content.assess row 7 is ["Other assessments", "DOLOR / AEIOUTIPS", ""] — 'Other assessments' appears as a seventh 'Ask About' item (which is not in the CPG and breaks S-A-M-P-L-E), and it is absent from the Assessment Tools column, which ends at 'DOLOR / AEIOUTIPS'.
- **Fix:** Change row 7 to ["", "DOLOR / AEIOUTIPS", ""] and add row 8 ["", "Other assessments", ""] so Ask About holds only the six SAMPLE items and Assessment Tools holds all eight tools.

**Medium/low (unverified):**
- [medium] wrong-value p11 — 'Alternate Tx' under Transport is expanded to 'alternate treatment'; in context Tx means transport.  
  _fix:_ Change to 'If appropriate, consider alternate transport (Tx)' so the sub-bullet stays under Transport and matches the CPG's use of Tx.
- [medium] colour-coding p11 — The two red-highlighted escalation rows inside PAUSE & PLAN render as ordinary navy bullets with no urgency marking.  
  _fix:_ Split the two escalation rows into a red-coloured key (e.g. content.immediateAction or an escalation sub-array that maps to the red section palette) so the Call 000 / clinical operations manager rows carry the CPG's red emphasis.
- [medium] colour-coding p10 — The highlighted Oxygen medication row in RESPONDER ACTION is flattened into a plain bullet.  
  _fix:_ Mark the Oxygen entry as a medication row (e.g. a dosing-style or highlighted sub-object) so it stands out as the CPG's only drug action in this block.
- [low] missing-section p10 — The CPG stage name 'STOP' is absent; the app names the block 'Dangers And Safety'.  
  _fix:_ Rename the section title to 'STOP — Dangers & Safety' (or nest dangersAndSafety under a 'stop' key) so the algorithm stage names in the app match the CPG's eight named stages.
- [low] missing-section p10 — Two separate RESPONDER ACTION rows are merged into one bullet, losing the CPG's second column example.  
  _fix:_ Split into two entries: 'Establish: if refusal' and 'Establish: if limitation of treatment — e.g. Not For Resuscitation (NFR)'.
- [low] typo p11 — The CPG's own wording is 'clinical rational'; the app silently corrects it to 'clinical rationale'.  
  _fix:_ No clinical change needed; note that the source reads 'clinical rational' so the divergence is a deliberate spelling correction, not a data error. Keep 'rationale' only if the team accepts diverging from the printed CPG.
- [low] not-in-cpg p11 — Four glosses/labels appear in the app that are not printed anywhere on pages 10–11.  
  _fix:_ Benign expansions (SAMPLE is implied by the underlined initials; PSA/RSA match CPG protocol names). Keep if intended, but mark them as editorial additions rather than CPG text.
- [low] render-risk p11 — The two terminal boxes are stored as single prose strings, so their bulleted structure collapses into paragraphs on a 375px phone.  
  _fix:_ Convert both to arrays of strings so each CPG row becomes its own bullet, e.g. treatAndRefer: ["Ensure low acuity", "AND isolated simple problem", "AND covered by a Treat & Refer care plan", "Ensure patient consents to plan including VED, NEPT, private Tx (with Clinician consult)"].
- [low] render-risk p10 — The page is a flowchart, but the app's SVG workflow is reachable only inside the detailed view behind a collapsed 'Show ▼' toggle.  
  _fix:_ Surface the workflow diagram in the quick view, or default the 'Workflow diagram' toggle to expanded, so the algorithm structure is visible without two taps.
- [low] render-risk p11 — The ASSESS block's leading instruction renders as its own separate card titled 'Assess Guidance'.  
  _fix:_ Nest the guidance inside the assess section (e.g. assess: { note: '…', headers: […], rows: […] }) so it reads as one ASSESS block like the CPG.
- [low] wrong-value p10 — The Circulation row drops the CPG's leading '+' connector.  
  _fix:_ Set the Circulation action to "+ Major bleeding?" to match the CPG wording.

## `pain-relief-p92` — p92-95 · coverage: partial

**Graphics not represented in the app:**
- p92: Green RECOGNITION header band (speech-bubble icon) over a pale-green tinted body containing the pain-score row and the six DOLORS rows, each prefixed by a green capital letter (D, O, L, O, R, S) in a left gutter column.
- p92: Icon gutter in the MANAGEMENT block encoding the action type of each row: blue '+' = intervention, magnifier = assess, blue pill/capsule = administer medication, blue telephone handset = consult. Inline black 'IF' badges mark the conditional trigger on four of the five rows.
- p92: Two solid purple/indigo highlight bands in the right column of the MANAGEMENT table marking the drug rows ('Paracetamol, Oral' and 'Methoxyflurane, Inhaled') in bold white — the CPG's visual cue that these are cross-links to a dose block.
- p93: Three grey-banded Paracetamol dose-block tables (grey header row with pill icon + 'IF Non-tolerated Pain score ↑ 0'; rows Demographic / Route / Initial Dose / Repeat Dose(s) / Maximum, with a people icon on Demographic and a circle-slash icon on Maximum). Demographic cells use inline black 'OR' and '&' operator badges and ↑ / ↓ arrow glyphs.
- p94: Fourth Paracetamol dose block (↓12 years old, Oral liquid, 15 mg x kg) containing an indented 13-row weight ladder — each row an 'IF <weight>' badge, approximate age, mg dose and mL volume — preceded by the bold instruction to confirm doses against the bottle label.
- p95: Methoxyflurane dose-block table (grey header with pill icon + 'IF Pain score ↑ 3'; rows Demographic / Route / Initial Dose / Repeat Dose(s) / Maximum).

**Confirmed (verified) findings:**

### [high] missing-section · p92 — The escalation row 'IF Pain uncontrolled → Consult: Clinician' is missing from management — the protocol has no escalation path in the app at all.
- **CPG:** MANAGEMENT, final row (telephone icon): 'IF Pain uncontrolled' → 'Consult: Clinician'.
- **App:** content.management has only four items and stops after '💊 If pain ≥3 → Methoxyflurane inhaled'. No escalation string, no `escalation` key.
- **Fix:** Append to content.management: '📞 If pain uncontrolled → Consult: Clinician'.

**Medium/low (unverified):**
- [medium] missing-section p92 — The six-row DOLORS mnemonic that makes up the bulk of the green RECOGNITION block is entirely absent from this protocol.  
  _fix:_ Add a `dolors` array of {letter, meaning} to pain-relief content (the renderer already draws that shape as a table), or at minimum cross-reference the Pain Assessments protocol from the Recognition section.
- [medium] missing-section p93 — The 'Non-tolerated' qualifier that gates every Paracetamol dose block is not carried anywhere in the app — the trigger is recorded only as a bare pain score.  
  _fix:_ Set indication: 'Non-tolerated pain score ≥0' on all four Paracetamol dose blocks (and 'Pain score ≥3' on Methoxyflurane), so the responder sees that Paracetamol is indicated for pain the patient is not tolerating, not for any pain score above zero.
- [medium] missing-section p94 — The bolded paediatric safety instruction to check the doses against the bottle label before administering is missing.  
  _fix:_ Carry the sentence verbatim as a note on the ↓12 years dose block, e.g. note: 'Confirm the following doses with the label on the bottle prior to administration!', rendered above the weight table.
- [low] wrong-value p92 — The 'IF Appropriate' condition on the non-pharmacological management row is dropped, turning a conditional option into an unconditional instruction.  
  _fix:_ Reword to 'If appropriate → Non-pharmacological management: positioning, splinting, ice pack'.
- [low] colour-coding p92 — The CPG's green RECOGNITION block is not rendered green — the app shows Recognition in purple (quick view) and navy (detail view), while colouring Management green, inverting the CPG's own coding.  
  _fix:_ Add `recognition: { title: CPG.green, bg: CPG.greenTint, border: '#00a64f33' }` to SECTION_COLORS and swap the quick-view Recognition/Management colours so Recognition is green and Management navy, matching the CPG.
- [low] render-risk p92 — content.recognition is a bare string rather than an array, so it renders as a single paragraph and cannot hold the multi-row RECOGNITION block the CPG page actually contains.  
  _fix:_ Convert to an array (['Pain score ≥0 out of 10']) so it renders as a bullet list and can be extended with the DOLORS rows.

## `seizure-p96` — p96-98 · coverage: partial

**Graphics not represented in the app:**
- p96: Green 'RECOGNITION' header block (speech-bubble icon), body 1: four-line prose paragraph on the variety of seizure causes/symptoms and erring on the side of caution.
- p97: Red 'STOP' coloured algorithm box (flag icon) — pink-tinted conditional rows with black 'IF' chips in column 1 and purple/indigo action chips in column 2 ('Oxygen, BVM (8-15L O2)', 'Oxygen, NRB (10 - 15L O2)'), plus a black 'ONLY IF' chip row.
- p97: BVM ventilation-rate table nested inside the red STOP box — 5 age-band rows x (age, breath interval in seconds, rate in x p/min), age bands encoded with up/down arrow glyphs (↑15 years old, ↓1 year old).
- p98: Midazolam dosing table 1 — grey header row with pill icon and black 'IF'/'OR' chips ('IF GCSE ↑ 5 mins OR Multiple GCSE *'); rows: Demographic (people icon), Route, Initial Dose, Repeat Dose(s), Maximum (no-entry icon). 5 mg regimen.
- p98: Midazolam dosing table 2 — identical grey-header structure; Demographic uses two '&' chips ('↑15 years old & NOT Elderly/Frail & ↑60 kgs'). 10 mg regimen.

**Confirmed (verified) findings:**

### [high] missing-section · p96 — The entire RECOGNITION prose paragraph is missing — the app has no `recognition` key.
- **CPG:** 'Seizures can present due to a wide variety of causes and with a wide variety of symptoms. Differentiating between types and causes of seizures in the pre-hospital environment can be difficult. When in doubt it is prudent to err on the side of caution and treat likely seizure activity with this guideline.'
- **App:** No recognition section. content = { definition, midazolamIndication, management } only.
- **Fix:** Add `recognition: ['Seizures can present due to a wide variety of causes and with a wide variety of symptoms. Differentiating between types and causes of seizures in the pre-hospital environment can be difficult.', 'When in doubt it is prudent to err on the side of caution and treat likely seizure activity with this guideline.']`.

**Medium/low (unverified):**
- [medium] missing-section p96 — The definition is truncated — the clause describing what a seizure causes is cut off.  
  _fix:_ Restore the full sentence: 'Sudden, uncontrolled electrical disturbance in the brain which can cause changes in behaviour, movements, feelings and consciousness.'
- [medium] render-risk p97 — Two visually distinct CPG algorithm boxes and their purple action chips are flattened into one undifferentiated 9-bullet list, losing the STOP/SEIZURE separation and the drug-action emphasis.  
  _fix:_ Split into two keys (red STOP / navy SEIZURE) and express each conditional as a two-column structure ({ if, action }) so the renderer can produce a table with the action visually separated, matching the CPG's chip column.
- [medium] wrong-value p97 — The condition for assisting with the patient's own seizure plan is narrowed from 'Available (E.G. prescribed medications)' to 'If prescribed'.  
  _fix:_ Change to 'If available (e.g. prescribed medications) → assist carer(s) to administer seizure plan' — the CPG gate is availability of the plan, with prescribed medications given only as an example.
- [medium] wrong-value p98 — The definition of 'multiple seizures' loses the CPG's recovery qualifier (conscious state / normal baseline).  
  _fix:_ Restore the qualifier and carry the p98 footnote as its own note: 'Multiple GCSE refers to multiple tonic clonic seizures occurring WITHOUT full recovery to the patient's normal baseline in between seizures.'
- [low] wrong-value p97 — Oxygen flow units and the titration wording are transcribed differently from the CPG.  
  _fix:_ Match the CPG wording: 'Oxygen, BVM (8-15L O2)', 'Oxygen, NRB (10-15L O2)', and 'ONLY IF adequate perfusion → titrate down to SpO2 92-96%'.
- [low] wrong-value p98 — The GCSE time threshold uses '≥5 min' in the app; p98's table headers encode it with the '↑' (over) glyph, which is stricter than p96's prose.  
  _fix:_ Reproduce both forms as the CPG does — keep 'Lasted 5 or more minutes' in the indication text and label the dosing cards 'IF GCSE ↑5 mins OR Multiple GCSE' — rather than silently normalising the two.
- [low] typo p97 — The SitRep line drops the word 'dispatch'.  
  _fix:_ Restore to 'SitRep: Hatzolah dispatch + call ambulance'.

## `conscious-status-cb-p121` — p121 · coverage: partial

**Medium/low (unverified):**

## `adrenaline-p138` — p138-139 · coverage: partial

**Graphics not represented in the app:**
- p138: Green header bar with white tick icon '✔ INDICATIONS' over pale-green tinted rows with green asterisk bullets — green = permitted-use/definition colour band.
- p138: Navy header bar with white play/triangle icon 'ADVERSE EFFECTS' over lavender rows; hierarchical list — parent bullet 'Arrhythmias' with three indented sub-items (Sinus tachycardia, SVT, VT) then five sibling bullets. Navy = lowest-urgency band, and it is the LAST block on the page.
- p139: Inline operator glyphs inside the Demographic rows: table 1 '≥ 6 years old' + inverted black '&' badge + '↑ 20 kgs'; table 2 '↓ 6 years old' + inverted black 'OR' badge + '↓ 20 kgs'. The badge encodes AND vs OR logic; ↑/↓ encode over/under.

**Confirmed (verified) findings:**

### [high] wrong-value · p138 — Both dose-adjustment precautions drop the word 'consulting', turning a 'consult before adjusting' instruction into an autonomous dose-adjustment instruction.
- **CPG:** 'Consider consulting for reduced doses for — Elderly / frail patients; Cardiovascular disease PHx; Monoamine Oxidase Inhibitors (MAOIs) – Current / recent use' and 'Consider consulting for increased doses for — Beta blockers – Current / recent use'.
- **App:** 'Consider reduced doses for: elderly/frail, cardiovascular disease, MAOIs' and 'Consider increased doses for: beta blockers (current/recent use)'.
- **Fix:** Restore 'consulting': 'Consider CONSULTING for reduced doses for: elderly / frail patients, cardiovascular disease PHx, MAOIs (current / recent use)' and 'Consider CONSULTING for increased doses for: beta blockers (current / recent use)'.

**Medium/low (unverified):**
- [medium] missing-section p138 — The 'Arrhythmias' parent heading in ADVERSE EFFECTS is dropped and its three sub-items are promoted to top-level siblings, flattening the CPG's two-level list.  
  _fix:_ Restore the grouping, e.g. adverseEffects: ['Arrhythmias — sinus tachycardia, supraventricular tachycardia (SVT), ventricular tachycardia (VT)', 'Myocardial infarction exacerbation', 'Hypertension', 'Anxiety', 'Palpitations', 'Pupillary dilatation'] (or a nested object so the renderer shows the parent/child levels).
- [medium] colour-coding p138 — ADVERSE EFFECTS is the CPG's navy (lowest-urgency) block and appears LAST on the page, but the app renders it FIRST in red as '⚠️ Adverse Effects / Warnings', above Indications and Dosing.  
  _fix:_ Render adverse effects last (or at least after Indications/Dosing) and in the CPG's navy, reserving red for Contraindications; keep the ⚠️ marker for the red 'do not give' block only.
- [low] colour-coding p138 — INDICATIONS is a green block in the CPG but is rendered with a blue accent in the app, so the drug pages no longer match the CPG's own colour language.  
  _fix:_ Use the CPG green (#00a64f header, #e9f4ea tint) for the Indications section on medication pages.
- [low] wrong-value p138 — Two qualifiers are lost from the reduced-dose precaution: 'PHx' (past history) on cardiovascular disease, and 'Current / recent use' on MAOIs.  
  _fix:_ Write 'cardiovascular disease PHx' and 'MAOIs (current / recent use)' so the two drug-history qualifiers survive.
- [low] render-risk p139 — The ↑ / ↓ demographic glyphs are carried into the app with no legend, so on a 375px phone the card reads '👥 ↑6 years old AND ↑20 kg' with nothing explaining that ↑ means 'over'.  
  _fix:_ Either spell the thresholds out ('6 years or older AND over 20 kg' / 'under 6 years OR under 20 kg') or add a one-line arrow legend to the Dosing section header.
- [low] typo p138 — The CPG's own section header is misspelled 'CONTRINDICATIONS' (missing the 'A'); the app spells it correctly, so no app change is needed — flag for the CPG editors.  
  _fix:_ No app change — keep the correct spelling. Raise the typo with the CPG authors for the next revision.

## `glucagon-p143` — p143 · coverage: partial

**Graphics not represented in the app:**
- p143: Four colour-coded section banner blocks with icon glyphs: green ✔ INDICATIONS, red ✕ CONTRINDICATIONS, yellow ? PRECAUTIONS, navy ▶ ADVERSE EFFECTS — each with a matching pale body tint. This is the CPG's semantic colour language for medication pages.

**Confirmed (verified) findings:**

### [high] wrong-value · p143 — Adult/older-child dosing band uses an up-arrow instead of the CPG's ≥ operator, so exactly-8-year-olds fall outside both dosing bands.
- **CPG:** Dosing table 1, Demographic row: "≥ 8 years old" (greater-than-OR-EQUAL-TO). Dosing table 2, Demographic row: "↓ 8 years old".
- **App:** content.dosing[0].demographic = "↑8 years old"; content.dosing[1].demographic = "↓8 years old".
- **Fix:** Change content.dosing[0].demographic to "≥8 years old" (or spell it "8 years and older"). The CPG's inclusive ≥ must be preserved — an up-arrow reads as strictly greater-than, leaving an 8-year-old matching neither card or being pushed to the 0.5 mg card.

**Medium/low (unverified):**
- [medium] render-risk p143 — The two dosing cards are near-identical and are discriminated only by a single arrow glyph in the demographic field, which is easy to mis-read on a 375px phone.  
  _fix:_ Spell the bands out in the demographic field ("8 years and older" / "Under 8 years") or at minimum use "≥8 years old" / "<8 years old", so the two dosing cards are distinguishable at a glance under stress.
- [medium] colour-coding p143 — The CPG's red-banded CONTRINDICATIONS block (accreditation restriction) renders navy like every other section, losing its danger emphasis.  
  _fix:_ Add "contraindications" to the red section-colour set in the renderer (or store this as an immediateAction/redFlags-equivalent key), and keep MUST emphasised — for an SR-only drug the accreditation bar is the critical safety statement on the page.
- [low] colour-coding p143 — The page's green INDICATIONS and amber PRECAUTIONS banners are flattened to navy, so the CPG's four-colour medication banner scheme is not conveyed.  
  _fix:_ Map indications → green and precautions → amber in the section-colour table so medication pages mirror the CPG's own colour language.
- [low] typo p143 — The CPG itself misspells the section heading as "CONTRINDICATIONS"; the app silently corrects it to "contraindications".  
  _fix:_ No app change needed — the app's spelling is the correct one. Recorded so the source typo is not later mistaken for an extraction error, and so it can be raised with the CPG authors.

## `gtn-p145` — p145-146 · coverage: partial

**Graphics not represented in the app:**
- p145: Green INDICATIONS banner with white tick (✔) icon over a pale-green body row — the CPG's 'this is what it is for' colour code.
- p145: Red CONTRINDICATIONS banner with white cross (✖) icon over pale-red body rows; red asterisk bullets with two levels of indentation (Ventricular Tachycardia → ECG sub-bullet; PDE5 inhibitors → four drug/time sub-bullets). The red banding is the CPG's absolute-danger marker.
- p145: Yellow/amber PRECAUTIONS banner with '?' icon over pale-yellow body rows; amber asterisk bullets with indented sub-bullets ('Risk of causing severe hypotension'; Age ↑ 60 / no previous GTN history / recent MI).
- p146: Purple PREGNANCY / POSTPARTUM banner with flag (▶) icon over pale-purple body rows, including an un-bulleted continuation paragraph under 'Monitoring required if breastfeeding'.

**Confirmed (verified) findings:**

### [high] missing-section · p145 — All PDE5 inhibitor brand names are dropped, and the four separate contraindication sub-bullets are collapsed into one run-on line.
- **CPG:** "PDE5 inhibitors - Current / recent use" with sub-bullets: "Avanafil (Stendra) <12 hours", "Sildenafil (Viagra) <24 hours", "Tadalafil (Cialis) <48 hours", "Vardenafil (Levitra, Staxyn) <24 hours".
- **App:** "PDE5 inhibitors — Avanafil <12h, Sildenafil <24h, Tadalafil <48h, Vardenafil <24h"
- **Fix:** Restore the brand names (Stendra, Viagra, Cialis, Levitra/Staxyn) and the 'Current / recent use' qualifier, and split into a nested list so each drug is its own line — patients report brand names, not generics, and on a 375px screen this single bullet wraps to 3-4 lines that cannot be scanned under time pressure.

**Medium/low (unverified):**
- [medium] missing-section p146 — The breastfeeding guidance paragraph under PREGNANCY / POSTPARTUM is dropped entirely.  
  _fix:_ Append the CPG's own sentence: 'Use lowest effective dose and observe infant for adverse effects such as flushing or discomfort after breast feeding.'
- [medium] colour-coding p145 — The CPG's amber PRECAUTIONS block renders navy in the app, losing the caution coding.  
  _fix:_ Add "precautions" to the amber-rendered section keys.
- [medium] wrong-value p145 — The first precaution is abbreviated to 'Right/inferior MI', collapsing the CPG's two explicitly OR-ed infarct types into one and re-scoping the sBP qualifier.  
  _fix:_ Use the CPG's verbatim wording, keeping 'Right ventricular myocardial infarction OR inferior myocardial infarction' spelled out with the bolded OR, and keep 'Risk of causing severe hypotension' as a sub-bullet.
- [low] render-risk p146 — Demographic keeps the raw CPG arrow glyph ('↑15 years old') while every other GTN value was converted to </>, so the dosing card mixes two operator conventions.  
  _fix:_ Render as ">15 years old" for consistency with the rest of the app (or convert every field back to glyphs) — a lone ↑ on a dosing card can be misread as 'increasing' rather than 'over'.
- [low] wrong-value p146 — Adverse effect shortened from 'Syncope / faint' to 'Syncope'.  
  _fix:_ Restore "Syncope / faint".
- [low] typo p145 — App silently corrects the CPG's spelling and drops the 'Concurrent use with other' phrasing in the preterm-labour precaution.  
  _fix:_ Match the CPG structure — 'Preterm labour — concurrent use with other tocolytics'. Flag the CPG's 'toctolytics' spelling to the guideline authors rather than silently diverging.
- [low] colour-coding p145 — INDICATIONS (green in the CPG) and PREGNANCY / POSTPARTUM (purple in the CPG) both render navy in the app.  
  _fix:_ Lower priority than the red/amber blocks, but map "indications" to green so the CPG's four-colour hierarchy (green/red/amber/navy) survives in the app.

## `oxygen-p157` — p157-158 · coverage: partial

**Graphics not represented in the app:**
- p157: Green 'DEFINITION' header band with '?' icon over a pale-green tinted body block containing the hypoxia definition paragraph.
- p157: COPD mnemonic device: the four 'Suspect COPD' sub-bullets have their leading letter set bold + underlined so they read down as C-O-P-D (Chronic cough, Older than 40, Past history of smoking, Dyspnoea on exertion).
- p158: Two-column IF -> THEN decision table: four rows each opened by a black inverse 'IF' chip badge in the left column, with the oxygen order in the right column, and indented triangle sub-bullets listing the qualifying conditions under each IF.

**Confirmed (verified) findings:**

### [high] missing-section · p157 — The entire green DEFINITION block on p157 is absent from the app - the app has no 'definition' key at all.
- **CPG:** DEFINITION: "Hypoxia occurs when there is inadequate oxygen in the tissues to maintain homeostasis. It may lead to symptoms such as an altered conscious state, dyspnea, tachycardia, tachypnea, diaphoresis, anxiety and cyanosis."
- **App:** content = { note, copdSuspect, indications }. No definition key, and none of the listed hypoxia symptoms (altered conscious state, dyspnea, tachycardia, tachypnea, diaphoresis, anxiety, cyanosis) appear anywhere in the oxygen entry.
- **Fix:** Add content.definition = 'Hypoxia occurs when there is inadequate oxygen in the tissues to maintain homeostasis. It may lead to symptoms such as an altered conscious state, dyspnea, tachycardia, tachypnea, diaphoresis, anxiety and cyanosis.' The key name 'definition' also makes the renderer colour it green, matching the CPG.

**Medium/low (unverified):**
- [medium] not-in-cpg p158 — The app invents an SpO2 target for the 'severe shortness of breath' row; the CPG leaves that row's order column empty.  
  _fix:_ Change to { condition: 'Severe shortness of breath', target: 'Apply oxygen' } - or render it as a standalone lead-in bullet above the IF rows, as the CPG does. Do not attach an SpO2 target to it.
- [medium] missing-graphic p157 — The C-O-P-D mnemonic (bold/underlined leading letters) is lost, and rewording 'Older than 40' to 'Age >40' breaks the 'O' of the mnemonic.  
  _fix:_ Restore the CPG wording verbatim and encode the mnemonic, e.g. copdSuspect: [{letter:'C',meaning:'Chronic cough / sputum production'},{letter:'O',meaning:'Older than 40'},{letter:'P',meaning:'Past history of smoking (including current + ex-smokers)'},{letter:'D',meaning:'Dyspnoea on exertion'}] so the renderer can show the letters, matching the CPG's underlined-initial device.
- [medium] wrong-value p158 — 'Severe kyphoscoliosis' is shortened to 'kyphoscoliosis' in the 88-92% risk group, widening who gets the lower oxygen target.  
  _fix:_ Use the CPG's exact six items: 'COPD', 'Neuromuscular disorder', 'Cystic fibrosis', 'Bronchiectasis', 'Severe kyphoscoliosis', 'Obesity'.
- [medium] render-risk p158 — The INDICATIONS decision table degrades to stacked record cards with slash-run-on condition strings on a 375px phone.  
  _fix:_ Restructure each entry as { trigger: 'IF ANY of the below', conditions: ['SpO2 ↓ 85%','Major trauma','Head injury','Shock','Severe sepsis','Anaphylaxis','Seizure'], target: 'Initial Mx NRB 10-15 L/min', then: 'ONCE hemodynamically stable: titrate oxygen to SpO2 92 - 96%' } so conditions render as a bullet list and the sequenced ONCE step is its own line.
- [low] missing-section p157 — The two FURTHER NOTES bullets are merged into a single 'note' string, losing the CPG's separate rows and exact wording.  
  _fix:_ Split into notes: ['This CPG is intended for patients aged 16 and older', 'High concentration oxygen may be harmful for patients at risk of hypercapnic respiratory failure'] so it renders as the CPG's two-bullet list.
- [low] wrong-value p158 — The CPG's down-arrow comparison glyph is silently converted to '<', contrary to the convention used everywhere else in contentData.js.  
  _fix:_ Use 'SpO2 ↓ 92%' / 'SpO2 ↓ 85%' to match both the CPG page and the arrow convention already applied across the rest of the app.
- [low] colour-coding p157 — Every block on p157-158 is green in the CPG, but no app key maps to the green treatment, so the whole protocol renders navy.  
  _fix:_ Adding content.definition (see the DEFINITION finding) restores the green block; optionally treat 'indications' and 'notes' as green for pharmacology entries to match the CPG's colour scheme.
- [low] typo p158 — The CPG source itself misspells 'below' as 'bellow' in all three IF headers - recorded so it is not silently 'corrected' or treated as an extraction error.  
  _fix:_ If the IF framing is restored (see the render-risk finding), quote the CPG verbatim including its 'bellow' spelling, or use 'below' deliberately and note the source typo - do not treat it as a text-layer extraction fault.

## `paracetamol-p159` — p159-161 · coverage: partial

**Graphics not represented in the app:**
- p159: Two-level indented bullet hierarchy inside PRECAUTIONS: parent asterisk bullets ('Liver toxicity may occur in paracetamol overdose', 'Risk of liver toxicity increases in the following circumstances') each with three dash sub-bullets. The parent/child relationship is purely visual.

**Confirmed (verified) findings:**

### [high] missing-section · p161 — The safety instruction to cross-check the paediatric liquid doses against the bottle label is missing entirely from the app.
- **CPG:** p161, directly beneath 'Initial Dose  15 mg x kg' and immediately above the 13-row dose ladder: 'Confirm the following doses with the label on the bottle prior to administration!'
- **App:** The ↓12 years dosing object jumps straight from "initial": "15 mg/kg" to the weightTable array. No confirmation instruction is stored or displayed; DosingCards prints only the hard-coded caption 'Paediatric Doses (15 mg/kg):' above the table.
- **Fix:** Add a field to the ↓12 years dosing object carrying the CPG's exact wording, e.g. "confirm": "Confirm the following doses with the label on the bottle prior to administration!", and render it above d.weightTable in DosingCards (ProtocolView.jsx:98-101) in a warning style. Paediatric oral paracetamol liquid exists in multiple concentrations, so the mL column is only valid against the specific bottle in hand.

**Medium/low (unverified):**
- [medium] missing-section p159 — The PRECAUTIONS block has been flattened: both parent statements are dropped, the two 24-hour maximum limits are merged into one bullet, and 'Impaired liver function' is lost.  
  _fix:_ Restructure content.precautions to mirror the CPG's two-level shape — e.g. nest the three 'Do NOT' rules under a 'Liver toxicity may occur in paracetamol overdose' key and the three risk factors under 'Risk of liver toxicity increases in the following circumstances', restoring 'Impaired liver function / liver disease' in full and splitting the 4 G adult and 60 mg/kg paediatric limits back into two separate bullets as the CPG has them.
- [medium] render-risk p160 — The demographic gate that selects between the 500 mg, 1000 mg and weight-based doses is rendered in the smallest, lowest-contrast style on each dosing card, below a bold green dose figure.  
  _fix:_ Raise the demographic line to at least `text-sm font-semibold text-gray-800` (or render it as a labelled row like the CPG) so the selection criterion is not subordinate to the dose figure it governs.
- [low] colour-coding p159 — Section colours and ordering diverge from the CPG: INDICATIONS is green in the CPG but blue in the app, and ADVERSE EFFECTS is a navy informational block in the CPG but is promoted to a red warning shown first in the app.  
  _fix:_ Recolour the Indications section green (#16a34a) to match the CPG's green header, and drop Adverse Effects from red-and-first to navy in its CPG position after Precautions — the CPG reserves red for the contraindications block, and paracetamol's adverse effects (rashes, haematological reactions, IV-infusion hypotension) are not flagged as critical there.
- [low] wrong-value p159 — The hypotension adverse-effect qualifier is truncated, dropping the population it applies to.  
  _fix:_ Restore the full CPG wording: 'Hypotension (associated with IV infusion, particularly in critically ill patients)'.
- [low] wrong-value p159 — The tile summary narrows the indication to 'mild to moderate' pain, whereas the CPG also covers severe pain in combination with other analgesics.  
  _fix:_ Change the summary to something like 'Mild pain; moderate/severe pain with other analgesics — oral' so the tile does not exclude the severe-pain-in-combination indication.
- [low] typo p159 — The CPG's own section heading is misspelled 'CONTRINDICATIONS' (missing the 'a'); the app spells it correctly, so this is a source defect recorded for completeness.  
  _fix:_ No app change. Report the misspelling to the CPG authors for the next edition; do not propagate the typo into the app.
